# VFO Session-Learned Gotchas — full registry (#1–#476)

> Split out of `SESSION_REFERENCE.md` on 2026-06-19 to keep the live hub lean. This is the **complete** numbered list; the hub keeps only a curated ALWAYS-APPLIES subset.
>
> **Numbers are permanent.** Gotchas are referenced as `#N` from `SESSION_REFERENCE.md`, the operator prompts, and across `docs/` — **NEVER renumber.** Add new gotchas to the END of this list, incrementing from the current max — **grep for it, do not trust this sentence** (it has been stale before). **Prefer amending an existing entry in place over minting a near-duplicate** — grep for the behaviour before you take a new number (#90 has been amended twice this way).
>
> Pre-existing ordering quirk preserved verbatim: **#87 physically precedes #86.** (Do not "fix" it — references are by number, not position.)

---

**1. pg_cron auth uses the NEW `sb_secret_xxx` key, not the legacy `eyJ` JWT.**
When writing a pg_cron job that POSTs to the function URL with Authorization: Bearer <key>, use the value from "Publishable and secret API keys" tab in Supabase Dashboard. The function's SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY env var maps to that newer key, NOT the legacy service_role JWT. Both look similar; the wrong one returns 401.

**2. Test a cron immediately by EXECUTE-ing its stored command.**
No need to wait for the schedule:

```sql
DO $$
DECLARE cmd text;
BEGIN
  SELECT command INTO cmd FROM cron.job WHERE jobname = '<JOBNAME>';
  EXECUTE cmd;
END $$;
```

Then poll `net._http_response` ~10s later. The key never leaves Postgres.

**3. PowerShell here-string commit messages break on apostrophes.** Use `git commit -F <tmpfile>` instead of `git commit -m @'...'@`. The @'..'@ closing token parses incorrectly when the body has straight apostrophes, splitting your message across multiple pathspec args. The Bash tool's `cat > file << 'EOF' ... EOF` heredoc is cleaner if you're already operating in a Bash context.

**4. Stripe Connect transfers fail with `insufficient_capabilities_for_transfer`** if the destination account lacks the `transfers` capability — common in test mode. Verify with `acct.capabilities.transfers === 'active'` on the connect account BEFORE wiring it as `members.stripe_account_id`. Surface failures via admin notifications when relevant; the transfer call itself returns a normal Stripe error body.

**5. All 4 Stripe env vars must point at the same Stripe account in matching modes.** `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` + `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` = live mode of account X; `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY_SANDBOX` + `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET_SANDBOX` = test mode of account X. If they drift (e.g., sandbox key for account A but sandbox webhook secret for account B, or one secret missing entirely): incoming webhooks 401-silently, and `GET /v1/customers/{id}/payment_methods` for a customer in the "wrong" account returns empty data (handler maps this to `no_payment_method`). Verify with `dashboard.stripe.com/test/customers/<cus_id>` — the URL works for the account the user is currently logged into; "No such customer" means the env points elsewhere.

**6. `recN_status` is now written `'Sent'` by `automation_CONTRACT_invoicereceipt`** (and the tax equivalent writes `rec{N}_status='Sent'` on `client_tax_plans`). AutomationPanel displays NULL as the literal string "pending" via a frontend fallback. New rows get 'Sent' automatically; pre-existing rows that show "pending" despite `recN_email_sent=true` can be backfilled with: `UPDATE pipeline_map1 SET recN_status='Sent' WHERE recN_email_sent=true AND recN_status IS NULL;`

**7. Function-log endpoint only returns HTTP status lines.** The Supabase MCP `get_logs` for `edge-function` shows method/status/timing but NOT the `console.log()` contents from inside handlers. To debug handler behavior, instrument with an extra DB write or inspect `net._http_response` after firing.

**8. PowerShell `git pull | tail -3` silently fails** — `tail` is not a PS cmdlet. The pipeline error can prevent `git pull` from running at all. Use `Select-Object -Last 3` instead, or just omit the pipe.

**9. `pipeline_sandbox_config` must have a row for every pipeline that uses sandbox-aware handlers.** Currently `'MAP 1'`, `'TAX'`, `'ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'`, and `'ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'`. Without the row, handler defaults to live mode and external services (BoldSign, Stripe) get the wrong-mode API keys — webhooks often don't fire, or fire to the wrong account where you can't see them. When adding a new pipeline, INSERT a matching `pipeline_sandbox_config` row before any handler tries to read it. **Exception:** PIP Meetings intentionally reuses the `'MAP 1'` row (rides on Holistic Planning's sandbox toggle) — no separate `'PIP'` row exists.

**10. `boldsign-webhook` deploy requires `--no-verify-jwt` flag every time.** Default Supabase CLI deploy enables JWT verification on the function → BoldSign gets 401 silently and webhook events drop. `config.toml` has `verify_jwt = false` for stickiness, but always pass the flag too — the live registry setting is what matters and the flag overrides it.

**11. Chain auth pattern: forward `body.token`, not just the Authorization header.** The frontend's `callApi()` sends `Authorization: Bearer <SUPABASE_ANON_KEY>` (the anon key, not the admin session token) and puts the admin session token in `body.token`. The auth middleware reads `body.token` for session validation. When one AUTH handler chains to another via HTTP fetch, the chained call's body must include `token: body.token` — forwarding the Authorization header alone leaves the chain with anon-level auth and the chained handler 401s.

**12. MIME headers: never push empty strings for CC/BCC.** When building raw RFC 2822 emails for Gmail's drafts API, the first empty line in the headers block is the header/body separator. Adding an empty `Cc:`/`Bcc:` slot to the headers array causes Gmail to interpret everything from the next line down (Subject, MIME-Version, etc.) as body text. Build conditionally:

```js
const lines = [`To: ${to}`];
if (cc.length) lines.push(`Cc: ${cc.join(", ")}`);
if (bcc.length) lines.push(`Bcc: ${bcc.join(", ")}`);
lines.push(`Subject: ${subj}`, "MIME-Version: 1.0", ...);
```

DO NOT use a `.filter(l => l !== undefined)` that lets empty strings through. **Also avoid em dashes (—) in Gmail Subject headers** — they require RFC 2047 encoding for non-ASCII; without it Gmail renders the subject as mojibake (`â€"`). Use ASCII hyphens (`-`) in subject lines unless you implement the full `=?utf-8?B?<base64>?=` encoder.

**13. Stripe webhook routes by `metadata.pipeline` and `metadata.payment_kind`.** When creating PaymentIntents / Checkout Sessions, set: `metadata.pipeline` to `'MAP 1'`, `'TAX'`, `'ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'`, or `'PIP'`; `metadata.payment_kind` to a domain-specific tag (`'retainer'` for Tax 3, `'implementation'` for Tax 5 off-session charge, `'onboarding'` for advisor onboarding, `'purchase'` for PIP Meetings purchase, or for MAP1 `metadata.payment_number=N`). The webhook handler reads these to decide which table, which column to flip, and which downstream chains to fire. Missing metadata → the webhook falls through to the legacy MAP1 path and may write to the wrong row.

**14. Drive API can't fetch publicly-shared files via OAuth — use Supabase Storage instead.** "Anyone with the link" Drive files return 404 from `drive/v3/files/{id}?alt=media` even with valid OAuth. Either the file must be owned by the OAuth account, or in a Shared Drive with explicit membership. For static public-fetch assets, host them in a public Supabase Storage bucket and fetch the URL directly with no auth — same code, fewer auth hops, works every time.

**15. Stripe idempotency keys must auto-rotate per retry attempt, not be reused.** A key like `tax-impl-{plan_id}-{date}` will collide after a DB state reset because Stripe returns `Keys for idempotent requests can only be used with the same parameters` even though the params look the same to you (Stripe fingerprints the WHOLE request including timestamps inside the request body). Include a suffix that changes naturally when the retry context changes — e.g. embed the prior `payment_intent_id` suffix: `tax-impl-{plan_id}-{retainer_pi_last8}-{date}`. After reset → retainer PI suffix changes → idempotency key changes → Stripe accepts the new charge.

**16. Public chain target with AUTH origin: move to PUBLIC_HANDLERS with internal auth gate.** Pattern: a PUBLIC token-page (e.g. `/tax-postreview-decide`) needs to chain to a handler that was originally admin-only (e.g. refund). Don't try to spoof an admin token from the public side. Instead, move the target handler to `PUBLIC_HANDLERS` and add an inline gate at the top:

```js
const isServiceRole = req.headers.get('Authorization')?.includes(SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY);
const adminSession = await validateAdminSession(body.token);
if (!isServiceRole && !adminSession) return 401;
```

This accepts BOTH service-role bearer (used by the chain) AND admin session token (used by direct admin button clicks), with no behavior change for the latter.

**17. Notification rows use column `message`, not `body`.** The notifications table schema is `(id, recipient, client_id, pipeline, title, message, link, read, dismissible, created_at)`. Handlers that write `body:` silently insert NULL into `message` and the bell shows blank rows. Always use `message`.

**18. Notification `link` must be a real frontend route.** Use `/admin/client/<id>?tab=tax` (or `?tab=automation` for MAP1), NOT `/clients/<id>`. For advisor onboarding (no per-client detail route), use `/admin?tab=advisors&section=advisor_onboarding`. Wrong link → bell click redirects to login (route doesn't exist → auth gate trips). When backfilling old rows: `UPDATE notifications SET link='/admin/client/'||client_id||'?tab=tax' WHERE link LIKE '/clients/%';`

**19. `notifications.dismissible` boolean controls the Done button.** Default `true`. FYI notifications (e.g. "Tax 3 client paid") get `dismissible=true` → bell shows a green Done button that marks read + filters locally. Action-required notifications (e.g. "Tax 3 Yes path — pricing form needed", "Tax 3 ExtraMeeting requested — schedule") get `dismissible=false` → no Done button; they clear only when the admin completes the underlying action (which side-effects an UPDATE on `notifications` to set read=true for all matching rows).

**20. Em dash (—) vs en dash (–) in status_options.** The DB seed had en dash `–` in some status_options rows but the frontend `statusColors` map was keyed on em dash `—`. Status pills rendered grey because the lookup missed. When adding new status values, mirror the EXACT punctuation in BOTH the DB seed AND the frontend color map; prefer em dash consistently.

**21. Linking a client to a program needs BOTH the `clients.enrollment_id` AND a `client_enrollments` join row.** `clients.enrollment_id` is the primary/display enrollment; `client_enrollments(client_id, enrollment_id)` is the M:N join the frontend uses to populate program client lists. Inserting only one of the two means the client won't appear in the program's UI until an admin clicks "Add existing client" (which inserts the missing join row). When creating test fixtures, insert both.

**22. The tax revshare sweep is retry-only.** It picks up plans where `*_rev_share='Pending'` or `*_rev_paid='Failed'` — NOT plans where revshare was never started. Fresh paid plans must reach the Tax 4 admin decision (and then either the 24h Continue auto-lock OR an explicit client click on the Continue green button OR an Undecided→Proceed/Confirmed click) to set `Pending` first. The earlier "never-attempted" branch in the sweep was a latent bug that auto-fired revshare on any paid plan once Tracy's sheet was populated, bypassing the Tax 4 admin decision; the fix removed that branch.

**23. Client-visible tax labels are program-aware via `utils/program-label.ts`.** `programLabel(programId)` returns `"VFO Tax Planning"` for program_id=4 and `"VFO Holistic Planning"` for program_id=1 or NULL legacy. Five call sites (invoice header, invoice footer, retainer-receipt header, implementation-receipt header, Stripe Checkout product name, Stripe off-session implementation charge description, BoldSign agreement title). Internal-only labels (`notifications.pipeline='TAX'`, `email_templates.pipeline='TAX'`, `pipeline_sandbox_config.pipeline='TAX'`) stay program-agnostic — they're lookup keys, not display names. Same idea applies to advisor and PIP — internal `pipeline='ADVISOR_ONBOARDING'` / `pipeline='PIP'` are lookup keys; advisor-facing labels use full plan names ("VFO Fast Track" / "Partnership Fast Track" / "Corporate Membership"), PIP-facing labels use the purchase description ("Tax Planning" or "N Additional PIP Meeting(s)").

**24. `tax_save_deposit_pi` extracts the LAST `pi_...` substring from input.** Defensive against paste-over where the admin doesn't clear the existing value before pasting a new PaymentIntent ID — without this the column ends up containing two concatenated `pi_...` strings and the subsequent refund fails. Also accepts Stripe dashboard URLs containing `/payments/pi_...`.

**25. `pipeline_map1.client_id` is UNIQUE.** Migration `pipeline_map1_unique_client_id` (2026-05-21) added the constraint to prevent the historical drift where stale handler-INSERT branches silently created duplicate rows for the same client. Any code path that tries to insert a second row for an existing client_id now hard-fails with Postgres `23505 unique_violation`. MAP 1 handlers all use `.maybeSingle()` to look up by client_id; legacy "if no row found, INSERT new one" fallback branches in some handlers (notably `pipfu-decision.ts`) are now dead code but harmless. Same audit not yet applied to `client_tax_plans`. `client_priority_tracks` intentionally does NOT have a UNIQUE constraint on `client_id` — clients can have many priority tracks (regular + tax + pip rows distinguished by `track_type`, plus multiple PIP meetings per client).

**26. Tracy's revshare sheet structure (MAP 1 + tax).** Master sheet `1PvUEWwTH70OBHabdHPh2SS9U7isITOzHmSd11GoHGJ0`, Home Page tab: client_ref in column A, hyperlink to a batch sheet in column I. Each batch sheet has tabs named per client last name. Within each batch tab, the revshare handler reads `G7:O200` and matches a row where column I = the receipt number (`rec{N}_number` for MAP 1; `<kind>_receipt_number` for tax) AND column J = expected payment amount (gross_fee for one-time / gross_fee/4 for Quarterly / retainer amount for tax). Columns K+L+M+N+O must sum exactly to column J for Tracy's-verified gate to pass. If any check fails, the handler returns `pending: true` and the daily sweep retries. Advisor onboarding does NOT touch Tracy's sheet — all advisor-created members default to Money Mapping. PIP Meetings revshare ALSO does NOT touch Tracy's sheet — `pip_purchase_member_share` and `pip_purchase_vfos_share` are admin-entered at form-submit time and trusted as-is.

**27. Money Mapping member email banner shows the share amount.** Both MAP 1 (`actions/pipeline/contract-revshare.ts`), tax (`actions/tax/revshare.ts`), and PIP (`actions/msm/pip-revshare.ts`) render the Money Mapping confirmation banner with the same dark-blue (`#00488d`) solid background, white text, label-and-amount two-row stack as the Revenue Share banner — only the label differs ("Allocated to Money Mapping" vs "Your revenue share"). Don't revert to the lighter-styled label-only banner pattern.

**28. PostgREST `.neq('col', value)` excludes NULL rows.** A query like `.neq('payment_status', 'succeeded')` will NOT return rows where `payment_status IS NULL` — which is the common case for unpaid records. To match both NULL and non-succeeded values use: `.or('payment_status.is.null,payment_status.neq.succeeded')`. This bit the advisor sweep payment-stall query during Phase 6 testing — no rows matched until the filter was fixed.

**29. Onboarding-created advisor attributes (numbering now via #48).** Members created via `automation_ADVISOR_createmember` (Stage 3 button) are `member_type='Implementation'`, `elite_status='Active'`, `advisor_model='New Model'`, `member_category='advisor'`, `revenue_decision='Money Mapping'`, and `members.onboarding_id` = the `advisor_onboarding.id` FK (`ON DELETE SET NULL`). **The old fixed `>= 60100` member-number scan is GONE** — numbers are now assigned by the category-relative helper `nextMemberNumber('advisor','New Model')` (see #48). Manual Add Advisor form rows take whichever `advisor_model` the admin picks (required radio, no default — Legacy Model / New Model). The legacy existing members were backfilled to `advisor_model='Legacy Model'` in migration `members_advisor_model_column`, then to `member_category='advisor'` in `add_member_category`.

**30. `callApi` retry rule: reads retry once on timeout, writes never.** The frontend `src/lib/api.js` enforces an `AbortController` timeout on every fetch — **20 seconds by default, and 60 seconds for `automation_*` WRITE actions as of 2026-08-03 (two-tier, see #319)**. On timeout: actions matching `^load_|_load(_|$)` (plus the explicit `vault_list`) silently retry once after a 1-second pause — they're idempotent reads, so a duplicate request is harmless and a retry usually catches a now-warm edge function. All other actions throw `"Request timed out after <limit>s. Please try again."` immediately (the message reports whichever tier applied), because the server may have already processed the request and a blind retry could double-write (this exact bug created 2 CIQ rows from one click during a cold start before the fix; it recurred as a double Gmail draft on 2026-08-03 via a MANUAL resubmit after a false 20s timeout — the reason for the 60s write tier, #319). Non-timeout failures with `res === null` (network blip, CORS, offline) still retry up to 3 times with 2-second gaps — unchanged. If you add a new read action whose name doesn't match the regex (e.g., `get_*`, `fetch_*`), add it to the explicit allowlist in `isReadAction()`. NEVER add a write action to that list.

**31. Frontend caching layer in `src/lib/api.js`.** Module-level promise cache (NOT a React Context, NOT a state library — just `let cachedDataPromise` and a `Map` keyed by stringified payload). Two entry points: `loadCachedData()` for `load_data` (no key), `loadCachedAction(action, payload)` for keyed reads (currently wired for `msm_load_programs`, `msm_load_training_track`, `msm_load_client_track`). Subsequent calls within the same session return the original promise — instant. Cache invalidates on `clearSession` (logout) and exposes `clearCachedData()` / `clearActionCache(action?)` for explicit refresh. Failed promises auto-evict from the cache so retries can resolve. When adding caching for a new read, only do it for actions whose result is truly stable per-session (program structures, member rosters, etc.). Anything that mutates in-session (notifications, pipeline rows, plan progress) must NOT be cached or stale data will leak.

**32. Skeleton placeholder conventions** (`src/components/shared/Skeleton.jsx`). Building blocks: `Skeleton({width, height, style})`, `SkeletonRow({withPill, withDate})`, `SkeletonCard({rows, title})`, `SkeletonStats({count})`. Two rules guide what to skeleton: **(a)** for **conditional sections** (Stripe card, certs, notes — exist or don't per record), do NOT skeleton — the page should only grow as data lands, never shrink. **(b)** for **lists with unknown count** (CIQ rows, tax plans, history rows — section always exists but count varies), show **2-3 placeholder rows by default** — communicates "loading some list" without misleading on the count. Always wrap the skeleton in the same outer container (max-width, padding) as the loaded content so chrome doesn't shift. Header chrome (page title, tabs, back button) renders immediately and is NOT inside the skeleton — only the content area swaps. Pulse animation lives in `src/index.css` (`.vfo-skeleton` class + `vfo-skeleton-pulse` keyframes).

**33. Pass `enrollment_id` via `location.state` when navigating from a program-specific client list.** A client can be enrolled in multiple programs (Holistic + Tax). When a member clicks a client from the Tax Planning view, the click handler must pass `enrollment_id` via `navigate('/member/client/${id}', { state: { enrollment_id: enrollment.id } })`. Otherwise `ClientDetail` falls back to `client.enrollment_id` (the primary enrollment, usually Holistic) and renders the wrong program's tabs. Already wired in `MemberMSMTracking.jsx`; `msm_load_client_home` accepts the param and picks the correct enrollment server-side. Admin side uses a different navigation pattern and was confirmed clean.

**34. GitHub Pages browser-HTML cache after `npm run deploy`.** Even after the gh-pages push succeeds and the CDN is updated (verify by curling the live URL and checking the bundle filename in `<script>` / `<link>` tags), users will often see the OLD bundle for a few minutes because their browser cached the `index.html`. Plain Ctrl+Shift+R doesn't always evict the HTML cache. The reliable fixes are: (1) DevTools → Network → "Disable cache" checkbox → reload, (2) incognito window, or (3) wait 5-10 minutes for the browser's own HTML revalidation. Don't conclude the deploy "didn't work" if the live HTML serves the new bundle but the user's browser still shows old content — that's local cache, not deploy failure.

**35. PIP Meetings diverges from MAP1/Tax/Advisor in three ways.** (a) **No Tracy's-sheet verification** — `pip_purchase_member_share` and `pip_purchase_vfos_share` are entered by the admin in the Phase 3 form and trusted as-is; `automation_PIP_revshare` does a Stripe Connect transfer for `pip_purchase_member_share` directly (or Money Mapping no-op). (b) **No retry sweep** — there's no `pip_revshare_sweep` cron; failed transfers leave `pip_rev_share_status='Pending'` and need manual re-fire via service-role HTTP to `automation_PIP_revshare`. (c) **Reuses MAP 1 sandbox config** — no separate `'PIP'` row in `pipeline_sandbox_config`. PIP also unlocks locked child meetings on Additional PIP revshare success: `UPDATE client_priority_tracks SET pip_paid=true WHERE pip_purchased_from_track_id=<source>`.

**36. Docs/ exist in both main checkout AND every worktree.** Path guardrail applies to docs too — `C:\vfo-react\docs\...` is the main checkout (forbidden for Edit/Write); the worktree copy is `C:\vfo-react\.claude\worktrees\<branch>\docs\...`. Easy to slip on because docs feel "shared" and the Read tool happily reads either path. If you accidentally edit the main copy: `cd C:\vfo-react; git checkout -- docs/<file>` to revert, then re-apply via the worktree path. Git status in BOTH locations should be checked before commit.

**37. `CREATE TABLE LIKE … INCLUDING ALL` preserves column names verbatim.** When cloning `advisor_onboarding` → `accountant_onboarding` for the accountant onboarding pipeline, the column `agreement_signed_by_advisor_at` was carried into the new table unchanged. Backend code wrote to `agreement_signed_by_accountant_at` (after a global token swap) and would have crashed silently the first time an accountant signed. The fix: explicit `ALTER TABLE accountant_onboarding RENAME COLUMN agreement_signed_by_advisor_at TO agreement_signed_by_accountant_at`. Lesson: after a `LIKE INCLUDING ALL` clone, audit columns whose names embed the source table's domain and rename them to match the new domain. (Also relevant: `LIKE INCLUDING ALL` carries the SAME column-default expression, including any reference to the SOURCE sequence — create a new sequence + `ALTER COLUMN id SET DEFAULT nextval('<new_seq>')` after the clone or both tables will share PK space.)

**38. BoldSign field-read parser: be permissive and soft-fail.** The advisor `ceo-countersign.ts` handler had a latent bug since launch: `propData.signerDetails.find(s => s.order === 1)` never matched because BoldSign's actual key is something else (we don't know what — `signerOrder` also didn't match). Every advisor onboarding silently wrote `selected_*=false, payment_amount=0`, and the downstream `_stripecustomer` chain 400'd on the `payment_amount <= 0` guard. The fix that ships: try multiple position-key variants (`signerOrder` / `order` / `signer_order`) AND fall back to `signerDetails[0]`, multi-key field-id matching (`id` / `fieldId` / `name` / `label`), multi-value truthy check (`value` / `checked` / `selected`, with `"on"` / `"true"` / `true` / `"checked"` / `"Checked"` / `1` / `"1"` as truthy). If after all that nothing is detected, insert an admin notification with `recipient: 'admin'` + diagnostic shape dump (`Object.keys(propData)`, signerKeys, fieldKeys), and **continue to send Anton's countersign Gmail** — never block on the read failure. Downstream `_stripecustomer` still has its `payment_amount > 0` guard as the safety net. Same pattern applied to `accountant/ceo-countersign.ts`. Lesson: when parsing third-party API responses, fall back through plausible key names, surface failures loudly to admin, and never silently halt a user-visible flow.

**39. `member_logins` is one-row-per-email.** The `load-login-setup.ts` handlers (advisor + accountant) check `member_logins` by email and return `state: 'already_setup'` if a row exists for that email. If two onboarding rows (advisor + accountant, or just two accountants) share an email, the second one's setup page will refuse to issue a fresh passcode. During testing this can manifest as "Login Already Set Up" or (if the test email had a stale row from a prior delete-and-retry cycle) "Invalid setup link". Easy unblock for testing: `DELETE FROM member_logins WHERE LOWER(email)=...` (or change the onboarding row's email). In production, this is the right behavior — one human = one portal login.

**40. Login-setup chain is idempotent on `login_setup_email_sent_at`.** `automation_*_createmember` checks `if (ob.login_setup_email_sent_at) return` before firing `_loginsetupemail`. When you partially reset an onboarding row for retesting (e.g. NULL out `member_number` + `member_created_at` but leave the login-setup columns), the next Create button fires create-member, hits the idempotent "already created" branch (because `ob.member_number` is set by the retry), then `ensureLoginSetupAndFireEmail` short-circuits because `login_setup_email_sent_at` is still set. No new email goes out, and the old email's token points at the deleted member. To force a fresh login-setup email on the new member, also NULL `login_setup_token` + `login_setup_token_expires_at` + `login_setup_email_sent_at`, then either re-click Create or fire `automation_*_loginsetupemail` directly via service-role HTTP.

**41. Accountant attributes (numbering + classification now via #48 — the accountant-only filter is GONE).** The old `ACCOUNTANT_TYPES` heuristic and the `[30000,90000)` / `[90000,∞)` fixed ranges (and the 59526-collision they caused) are **removed** — replaced by the durable `member_category` tag + `nextMemberNumber('accountant', <model>)` (see #48). Accountant-onboarding-created members are `member_type='Implementation'`, `advisor_model='New Model'`, `member_category='accountant'`. **The "no `revenue_decision`" half of this entry is DEAD as of 2026-08-12 / v730 — see #375; accountants now get `'Money Mapping'` from the onboarding pipeline and a required field on the Add form.** (Amended in place, not renumbered.) The hardcoded `ACCOUNTANT_TYPES` list was deleted from `actions/accountant/create-member.ts`; the frontend `MembersPanel.jsx` keeps the list ONLY as the Add-Accountant Member-Type dropdown options, never for classification.

**42. `document_numbers` table needs a per-pipeline FK column per onboarding type.** The handler `actions/accountant/invoice-receipt.ts` (cloned from advisor) inserted into `document_numbers` with an `accountant_onboarding_id` column reference. The column didn't exist (the table only had `client_id` and `advisor_onboarding_id`), so the insert failed silently in supabase-js (no exception propagation). The receipt/invoice number was still written back to `accountant_onboarding.invoice_number` from the same handler, so the user saw a number but the `document_numbers` tracking row was missing — meaning the next invoice could re-use the same sequence number. The fix: explicit `ALTER TABLE document_numbers ADD COLUMN accountant_onboarding_id bigint REFERENCES accountant_onboarding(id) ON DELETE SET NULL`. Lesson: any new onboarding pipeline that uses `document_numbers` for tracking needs its OWN FK column added — don't assume the clone migration carried it.

**43. `MemberSetupPage.jsx` falls through advisor → accountant token lookup.** The `/member-setup?token=...` page is shared between advisor + accountant onboarding flows. The page tries `automation_ADVISOR_loadloginsetup` first; on `state: 'invalid'` it falls through to `automation_ACCOUNTANT_loadloginsetup`. It remembers which pipeline matched in local state and fires the matching submit action (`automation_*_submitloginsetup`). One shared page, two backends — no need to clone the page per pipeline. If a future onboarding pipeline (e.g. specialist) ever needs a login-setup token, extend the same fallthrough.

**44. `MembersPanel.jsx` extracts `MemberDirectoryView` shared by Advisors + Accountants.** Both panels are now thin wrappers around a single `MemberDirectoryView` component (search + list + click-into-profile multi-tab). Wrappers pass type-specific props: `displayMembers` (filtered list), `addForm` (`<AddAdvisorForm/>` or `<AddAccountantForm/>`), `selectedKey` / `featureTabKey` (sessionStorage keys — separate so advisor + accountant selections don't collide), and `hiddenFields` (array of field strings to suppress in `MemberProfile`). **Accountants USED to pass `hiddenFields={['revenue_decision']}`; that stopped on 2026-08-12 / v730 (#375) and both wrappers now pass nothing — the accountant profile is identical to the advisor one.** The prop survives as the extension point: to hide profile fields/tabs per type later, just add their key to the array — no other code change required. `MemberProfile` reads `hiddenFields` and skips rendering matching sections in both the Details and Edit views.

**45. `deno check` baseline cleared to 0 (2026-05-29).** The 7 long-deferred TS18047 "possibly null" errors were fixed with explicit null-guards — no `!` / no `as any`. (a) `actions/pipeline/pipfu-decision.ts`: a single `if (!existing) return json({ error: "Failed to resolve pipeline_map1 row" }, 500);` after the upsert-then-reassign block narrows `existing` for all 4 downstream dereferences; the residual null came from `.insert().select().single()` being typed `Row | null`, not from the `.maybeSingle()` lookup. (b) `router/webhooks.ts`: the `payment_intent.succeeded` quarterly P2–P4 block was changed from `if (paymentNum >= 2 && paymentNum <= 4)` to `if (pipeRow && paymentNum >= 2 && paymentNum <= 4)`. This is behavior-relevant: `payment_intent.succeeded` also fires for tax/advisor/accountant/PIP payments whose customers are NOT in `pipeline_map1`, so `pipeRow` is genuinely null for them — a null + `payment_number` 2–4 now falls through to the `if (!pipeRow)` tax block instead of NPE-ing. Verified end-to-end on v331: pipfu-decision exercised on Undecided + Yes branches; a real sandbox off-session P2 charge flipped `pay2_status='succeeded'` + chained invoice/receipt + revshare. **The standing CLAUDE.md/`.refactor-resume.md` "NEVER touch the pipeRow null-check pattern without approval" rule now refers to a completed fix — the guard is in place; future edits to that block should preserve the `pipeRow &&` narrow.**

**46. BoldSign webhook `signerDetails` is an ARRAY, parsed as an object (latent, inert).** Both `router/webhooks.ts` and the standalone `boldsign-webhook/index.ts` read the signer email on every "Signed" event as `body.data?.signerDetails?.signerEmail || body.data?.signerDetails?.emailAddress || ""` — but BoldSign delivers `signerDetails` as an **array** of `{signerName, signerEmail, status}`, so `.signerEmail` off the array is always `undefined` → always `""`. The `if (signerEmail === ceoEmail)` branch (ceoEmail hardcoded `aanderson@elitert.com`, NOT sandbox-aware) therefore never matches. **Currently harmless ONLY because (a) the counterparty always signs FIRST so its "Signed" event correctly takes the else/"counterparty" branch, and (b) the CEO (Anton) always signs LAST, so his signature is recorded by the "Completed" event — which doesn't use the email check at all.** 8 call sites (4 in each file). Found in the Phase 2 audit (no fix shipped — it can't misbehave under the "Anton signs last" invariant). **If signing order ever changes, a 3rd signer is added after Anton, or any flow starts relying on the per-signer "Signed" event to set `ceo_signed`, fix this first:** parse the array (`.find(s => emailMatches(s))` / fall back to `[0]`) AND compare against the sandbox-aware CEO email, not the hardcoded one. Other parsers audited in Phase 2 (tax/MAP1 `ceo-countersign` sign-link, all `load-payment`, Stripe `payment_intents?expand` in webhooks, `charge-implementation`, revshare Google-Sheets `.find`) use documented keys with guards/soft-fails — verified safe.

**47. Passcodes are salted PBKDF2 now; the legacy `passcode` column is GONE (2026-05-29, v335).** Phase 4 replaced unsalted SHA-256 with **PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 210k iterations, 16-byte random salt**, stored as `pbkdf2$sha256$<iter>$<saltB64>$<hashB64>` in `allowed_admins.passcode_hash` / `member_logins.passcode_hash` (helpers `hashPasscodeSalted` / `verifyPasscodeSalted` / `isSaltedHash` in `utils/crypto.ts`; shared `verifyPasscode` in `utils/passcode-verify.ts`). Login handlers (`admin_login`, `member_login`, legacy `login`) now **fetch by email then verify in code** (can't match-in-query with a salt). Writers (`create_member_login`, `update_member_login`, `create_admin`, `update_my_passcode`, advisor/accountant `submit-login-setup`) write only `passcode_hash`. The rollout was: add nullable `passcode_hash` → dual-write + transparent re-hash on each successful legacy login → once all rows salted, drop the legacy `passcode` column (migrations `add_passcode_hash_columns`, `passcode_drop_not_null`, `drop_legacy_passcode_column`). **Consequence: v331–v334 can no longer be reverted to (they read the dropped `passcode` column) — rollback is forward-only from v335.** PBKDF2 chosen over bcrypt/scrypt because it's native Web Crypto (no external Deno module / no edge-runtime worker risk); iterations live in the hash string so cost can be raised later without invalidating existing hashes.

**48. Member taxonomy + category-relative numbering (2026-05-29, v336).** Two linked changes that replace the fixed member-number ranges and the ACCOUNTANT_TYPES heuristic (supersedes the numbering/classification halves of #29 and #41).
- **`members.member_category`** (`'advisor' | 'accountant' | NULL`, CHECK-constrained; NULL = uncategorized incl. corporate). Combined with `advisor_model` ('Legacy Model'|'New Model') it forms the **4 numbering buckets**. Migration `add_member_category` backfilled the 20 legacy advisors → `advisor`; onboarding FKs → their category.
- **`member_number_baselines`** table (`member_category, advisor_model, baseline`, PK on the pair) holds admin-controlled starting numbers. Seeded (migration `add_member_number_baselines` + a follow-up): advisor/New=**60000**, accountant/New=**30000**, accountant/Legacy=**90000**. Legacy advisor has existing rows so it self-derives (no baseline needed).
- **`utils/member-number.ts` → `nextMemberNumber(supabase, category, model)`**: scans `members` for the max integer `member_number` in that bucket → `+1`; if the bucket is empty, uses the baseline; if empty AND no baseline, returns an actionable error (NEVER guesses). Non-integer numbers (corporate `<parent>-C<n>`, suffixed legacy ids) are skipped.
- **All three create paths** use the helper: `actions/advisor/create-member.ts` (sets `member_category='advisor'`, ripped out the `>=60100` scan), `actions/accountant/create-member.ts` (sets `'accountant'`, ripped out the ACCOUNTANT_TYPES block + 30000/90000), `actions/members/add-full.ts` (accepts `member_category`, auto-generates when `member_number` is blank, and — **from 2026-05-29 until 2026-08-12** — did not default `revenue_decision='Revenue Share'` for accountants. **That carve-out was REVERSED on 2026-08-12 / v730 (#375): the default is now uniform for every category**, because the "stray Revenue Share" it was written to prevent turned out to be the correct value.)
- **`load_data`** now exposes `member_category` + `accountant_onboarding_id` on each merged member object.
- **Frontend**: admin `MembersPanel` `AccountantsPanel`/`AdvisorsPanel` filter by `member_category` (`accountant` vs everyone-not-accountant) — the ACCOUNTANT_TYPES classification heuristic is retired (the list survives only as the Add-Accountant dropdown options). Both Add forms drop client-side integer generation (leave the number blank → backend autogens) and send `member_category` (corporate stays `null` and keeps its `-C` numbering). Member-side `MemberPortal.MemberProfile` hides Revenue Decision when `member_category==='accountant'`.
- **Deploy state:** backend live at v336; **frontend NOT yet deployed** — until it ships, the live site's old Add forms still send explicit numbers + no `member_category` (the new backend accepts those, tagging `member_category=null`). Onboarding "Create Advisor/Accountant" buttons are fully correct the moment the backend is live.

**49. MAP 1 tracker — Setup phase removed; PIP-scheduled steps are now date entry (2026-05-29).** No backend code change — DB data + frontend `ClientTrackViewV2.jsx` only.
- Deleted `program_client_phases` "MAP 1 - Setup" (program_id=1, was phase_order 1) + its only task "Set up client on tracker" (`task_code='C1'`, `status_options='auto'`). 0 `client_progress` rows referenced C1. Remaining map1 phases renumbered `phase_order` 1–5; **"MAP 1 - Initial Contact" is now the first phase**.
- Tasks C4.1 / C4.2 renamed **"Date scheduled for PIP 1"** / **"Date scheduled for PIP follow-up"** and `status_options` set to the **new `'date_entry'` sentinel**. `ClientTrackViewV2` renders `status_options==='date_entry'` as an `<input type=date>` (admin) / date pill (member); `saveDateEntry` stores the entered date in `client_progress.completed_date` with `status='Scheduled'` — **no new column, no backend change**. Mirrors the PIP-meeting date UX but persists generically (PIP Meetings instead uses a dedicated `client_priority_tracks.pip_scheduled_date` column). `completePhase`'s Initial-Contact auto-complete list was trimmed to `['Call outcome']` so the date steps aren't auto-stamped.
- **Deploy ordering:** the Setup delete + the C4.1/C4.2 rename were applied to prod during testing (harmless to the old bundle). The `status_options='date_entry'` flip is **HELD until after the frontend deploys** — otherwise the live (old) bundle renders the sentinel as a literal dropdown option. Flip it as the final step, then verify the date pickers live.

**50. 90-Day Plan (training track) trimmed — review phases gone, `task_code` column dropped, "Completed By" removed (2026-05-29).** Migration `remove_training_review_phases_and_task_code`; frontend `MSMTracking.jsx` (admin) + `MemberMSMTracking.jsx` (member). Rollback snapshot saved at `vfo-edge-functions/.claude/worktrees/<branch>/_rollback-training-snapshot.json` (not committed).
- Deleted the **6 "Review" checkpoint phases** (3 per program — MSM 4/8/12 Review, `program_training_phases` ids 6/11/16/21/26/31) + their **31 tasks** (M25–30, M45–48, M61–66, P17–21, P32–35, P44–49). 0 `member_training_progress` rows referenced them. Remaining training phases renumbered `phase_order` 1–12 per program (MSM 1 Training → MSM 12 Activity).
- **`ALTER TABLE program_training_tasks DROP COLUMN task_code`** — the `M#`/`P#` step IDs are gone from DB + UI. The old review auto-calc (keyed on codes M25/M3/M30 etc.) and the **M22/M23 number-input special-case** (also keyed on code) were removed; M22/M23 ("Send Announcement Emails" / "Call Clients") now render as their normal **0–20 status dropdown**. **`program_client_tasks.task_code` (MAP 1 C-codes) is untouched** — still drives C10/C14/C15 logic + the member MAP 1 view's code display.
- The per-phase **"Completed By" selector was removed** from `TrainingTrack`; `member_training_progress.completed_by` is no longer written from this UI (stays null). Training-task completion dates are now **read-only auto-stamped** (MAP 1 pattern, `formatDate` MM/DD at 55px) instead of editable date inputs — `saveDateChange` deleted.

**51. Website Plugin — page gated on `website_enabled`; enable toggle is admin-only (2026-05-29).** Frontend only (`MemberWebsitePlugin.jsx`, `MemberPortal.jsx`, `MembersPanel.jsx`).
- `member_plugin_settings.website_enabled=false` now **hides the member-portal "Website Plugin" tab entirely** (`MemberPortal` filters the tab button + guards the render on `memberData.website_enabled`). Previously the page rendered regardless of the flag — that was the bug.
- The member-facing **"Enable Website Plugin" toggle was removed**. `MemberWebsitePlugin` gained an `isAdmin` prop and shows the toggle only when `isAdmin` (admin `MembersPanel` passes `isAdmin={true}`; member portal passes nothing). The component's not-enabled gate changed from `&& readOnly` to `&& !isAdmin`. `website_enabled` is merged into member objects by `load_data` (from `member_plugin_settings.*`), so it's available client-side without a re-login.

**52. Members can add clients/accountants on the member side; `msm_add_client` ungated + ownership-guarded (2026-05-30, v337).** Backend only (`constants/role-gates.ts`, `actions/msm/add-client.ts`, `actions/msm/add-client-contact.ts`). The member-side "+ Add Client" button (Holistic / Tax / Partnership Fast Track — Partnership's "clients" are accountants, same `msm_add_client` action with an `-PFT` ref) was returning **403 "Forbidden — admin access required"** because `msm_add_client` was in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`. Two-layer fix that opens it up WITHOUT a privilege-escalation hole:
- **`msm_add_client` moved** `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` → `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`, and **`msm_add_client_contact` added** to `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS`. The middleware now force-overwrites `body.member_number` with the caller's own for member callers (kills member_number spoofing; admins unaffected — the rewrite only fires for role `member`).
- **`add-client.ts` ownership guard** (applies to ALL callers): after the existing enrollment fetch, `400` if the enrollment is missing, `403` if `String(enrollment.member_number) !== String(member_number)`. Confines a member to their own enrollments (can't attach a client to another member's enrollment); a no-op for admins, who always pass a matching `(enrollment_id, member_number)` pair. Also enforces a real data-integrity invariant: a client's `member_number` must match its enrollment's owner.
- **`add-client-contact.ts` ownership guard** (member callers only): if `body.member_number` is present (set = scoped member caller), `403` unless the target `client_id`'s `member_number` matches. Admins don't send `member_number`, so it's skipped for them. Closes a pre-existing gap (the action was ungated with zero ownership check).
- Verified live on v337: member positive add (own Holistic/Partnership/Tax enrollment) → 200; foreign enrollment → 403; spoofed `member_number` → 403; foreign-client contact → 403; own-client contact → 200. Admin add-client unchanged. Pipeline smoke 5/5, `deno check` 0. Gate counts now: `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` **73**, `MEMBER_SCOPED_ACTIONS` **23**.

**53. Email Templates editable CC/BCC fully wired across all 5 pipelines (Phase 2 complete, 2026-06-01, v344–348).** Every automated email send-handler now sources internal CC/BCC from `email_templates.cc_list`/`bcc_list` (jsonb) via `utils/email-recipients.ts`: `templateRecipients(tmpl, isSandbox)` returns `{cc:[],bcc:[]}` in sandbox (so sandbox suppresses internal CC/BCC — **verify in LIVE only**); `dedupeEmails()` trims/lowercases-dedupes and **drops anything not matching a valid-email regex** (kills the `extra_cc="[]"` Gmail "Invalid Cc header" bug). Pattern: counterparty CC (member/PF, plus hardcoded `tracy@vfo-services.com` on MAP1+Tax invoice/receipt) stays in handlers; template CC is **additive**; hardcoded `["aanderson@elitert.com","platham@elitert.com"]` BCC arrays were **replaced** with `dedupeEmails(tplBcc)`. Per-template seeds reconciled to preserve current behavior — **28 templates keep the aanderson/platham BCC; every other bcc_list AND every cc_list is `[]`**. A handler's template `.select()` MUST include `cc_list, bcc_list` or the BCC silently drops. Exceptions left hardcoded: `boldsign-webhook` (signer email, not a recipient), and the inline member-confirmation emails in `contract-revshare.ts` / `tax/revshare.ts` / `pip-revshare.ts` (no `email_templates` row). deno check 0; **action count unchanged (218)** — only existing handlers edited. The `ceoEmail = "aanderson@elitert.com"` constants in every `*/ceo-countersign.ts` + `*/send-agreement.ts` are BoldSign **signer** addresses, not BCC — do not touch them.

**54. Resetting a test client touches THREE stores; tax tracker statuses live in `client_tax_progress`.** (a) **Tax** reset = wipe `client_tax_plans` flow fields + **`DELETE FROM client_tax_progress WHERE tax_plan_id=<plan>`** — this table (keyed by `tax_plan_id`) holds the Tax-tracker task statuses ("Ready for Tax 3?", "Client tax planning decision"), NOT `client_progress`, NOT `client_tax_plans`. Easy to miss → the tracker keeps showing old "Yes" states after a plan wipe. (b) **MAP 1** reset = **`DELETE` the `pipeline_map1` row** (do NOT null it — `UNIQUE(client_id)` + the PIP1 reconfirmation handler INSERTs it as the flow entry point, so a leftover row → duplicate-key 500 on re-run, see #25) + clear the `client_progress` C-task rows. (c) `client_progress` holds ONLY MAP1/Holistic C-code tasks. FK note: deleting a client cascades most children but `pipeline_map1` + `notifications` are `RESTRICT` (delete first); `members.onboarding_id`/`accountant_onboarding_id` + `document_numbers` `RESTRICT`-reference the onboarding tables (NULL the member FKs before deleting an onboarding row). **After any reset the user must log out → log back in** to flush the `api.js` module cache (`loadCachedData`/`msm_load_client_track`) — a plain hard-refresh does NOT reliably clear it. **Superseded/expanded by gotcha #57 (authoritative per-pipeline reset map — and note PIP has a SECOND store too).**

**55. BoldSign LIVE webhook must be registered for the Live/Production environment, not just Sandbox.** A pipeline in LIVE mode (`boldsign_test_mode=false`) creates envelopes in BoldSign's LIVE environment; if the webhook endpoint (`…/functions/v1/boldsign-webhook`) is registered only in Sandbox, the live "Signed" event has nowhere to go → the function is never called (zero logs), `client_signed`/`ceo_signed` never advance, the CEO countersign never fires. Fix: in BoldSign → Settings → Webhooks register the endpoint for **Both** environments (BoldSign rejects duplicate URLs, so delete the sandbox-only one and recreate as Both). The endpoint needs no auth (function deploys `--no-verify-jwt`) and our handler does NOT verify the BoldSign signing secret, so a new secret generated on recreate is irrelevant. (Discovered 2026-06-01 — live MAP 1 signing silently stalled until the Both webhook was added.)

**56. The Advisor/Accountant/PIP automation-panel sandbox badge is now a working toggle (was a static `<span>`).** New shared `components/admin/SandboxModeToggle.jsx` (clickable badge + confirm modal) calls `save_sandbox_config` with the right `pipeline` (`ADVISOR_ONBOARDING` / `ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING` / `'MAP 1'` for PIP) — the backend action already accepts any pipeline string (no backend change). PIP shares the `'MAP 1'` row, so its toggle flips MAP 1 too (the modal says so). **Frontend dev-only / uncommitted** as of this session. Before this, only the MAP 1 + Tax panels had working toggles; the other three rendered a non-interactive badge, so flipping those pipelines needed a direct `pipeline_sandbox_config` DB update.

**57. Test-client reset = TWO stores per pipeline (tracker + automation); clearing one leaves the other stale (2026-06-01).** Each pipeline keeps the human checklist statuses (TRACKER) separate from the payment/email/decision state the automation reads/writes (AUTOMATION). Handlers write both during normal flow so they stay in sync; a manual reset that clears only one shows stale data that no cache-clear fixes (it's a real, untouched row — not a caching bug). **Verified this session against live schema + handler code.** See the full standalone checklist with copy-paste SQL at [docs/operations/test-client-reset.md](operations/test-client-reset.md).

| Pipeline | TRACKER store (key) | AUTOMATION store | Two-store? |
|---|---|---|---|
| MAP 1 (program_id=1) | `client_progress` (`client_id` + `task_id`; join `program_client_tasks` for C-codes) — written by `actions/msm/save-client-task.ts` | `pipeline_map1` | **Yes** |
| Tax (program_id=4) | `client_tax_progress` (`tax_plan_id` + `task_id` + `tax_specialist_id`) — written by `actions/tax/save-task.ts` | `client_tax_plans` | **Yes** |
| PIP Meetings | `priority_progress` (`priority_track_id` + `task_id`) — written by `actions/msm/save-priority-task.ts` + `pip-meeting-confirmation-email.ts`, read by `msm_load_priority_progress`. **⚠️ shared with the regular (non-PIP) MSM priority tracker — target only the PIP `priority_track_id`s, don't blanket-wipe a client's `priority_progress`.** | `client_priority_tracks` (`pip_*` cols; the PIP meeting rows are `track_type='pip'`) | **Yes** (this was previously believed single-table — it is NOT) |
| Advisor Onboarding | — none — (stage statuses are columns on the onboarding row) | `advisor_onboarding` | No — single table |
| Accountant Onboarding | — none — (stage statuses are columns on the onboarding row) | `accountant_onboarding` | No — single table |

Full-reset extras (verify each per case): **`document_numbers`** (cols `id, type, number UNIQUE, client_id, advisor_onboarding_id, accountant_onboarding_id` — MAP1/Tax/PIP rows keyed by `client_id` + `type` string, NOT by tax_plan_id/pip; advisor/accountant by their `*_onboarding_id` FK; leave behind → the "existing invoice" lookup can reuse a sequence number); **`notifications`** (by `client_id` + `pipeline`); for onboarding pipelines that reached Stage 3, a created **`members`** row + **`member_logins`** (by email). External (not DB, won't reset by SQL): BoldSign documents + Stripe customers/PaymentIntents persist. **After ANY reset: logout → login** to flush the `api.js` module cache (`loadCachedData` / `loadCachedAction('msm_load_client_track' | 'msm_load_priority_progress', …)`); a hard-refresh is unreliable. Unrelated progress tables that are NOT part of these 5 pipelines (don't confuse them): `member_training_progress` (90-day training track, keyed by member) and `specialist_onboarding_progress` (a separate Specialist onboarding pipeline). Supersedes the partial reset notes in #54.

**58. Accountant Undecided email attaches a partnership-branched PDF (v349, 2026-06-01).** `actions/accountant/decision.ts` Undecided branch now picks the bucket PDF by `ob.accountant_partnership` — `'Accountant Partnership'` → `Accountant_Implementation_Agreement_Partnership.pdf`, anything else (incl. NULL) → `Accountant_Implementation_Agreement_No_Partnership.pdf` — from the public `accountant-onboarding-agreements` bucket, mirroring how the Yes-path `send-agreement.ts` picks the BoldSign template by the same dropdown. Previously it fetched a single `Accountant_Implementation_Agreement.pdf` regardless of branch. Graceful no-attachment fallback preserved if the PDF is missing. Both PDFs are uploaded; advisor's single `Advisor_Implementation_Agreement.pdf` is also uploaded (advisor has no partnership branch). Isolated single-handler change; `deno check` 0; action count unchanged (218).

**59. Specialist Onboarding automation (Stages 1–2) built 2026-06-02, v360 — backend live, FRONTEND NOT DEPLOYED.** *(SUPERSEDED 2026-06-03 by gotchas #64–#68: Stage 2 exec voting + Stage 3 background-check Stripe payment + reminder sweep were added; the "no Stripe / no `SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING` sandbox row" claims at the end of this entry are now FALSE — the row exists and specialist payments use Stripe. Frontend still not deployed.)* First automation on the previously manual Specialist Onboarding tracker (`specialist_onboarding` + `specialist_onboarding_progress`/`_meetings`/`_votes`). pipeline label = `SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING`. 5 new actions in `actions/onboarding/` (prelim-email, stage2-email [AUTH]; load-sif, submit-sif, revshare-decide [PUBLIC]) + extended `save-meeting.ts`. New columns: `specialist_onboarding.sif_token` (unique partial idx), `sif_data` (jsonb), `sif_submitted_at`; `specialist_onboarding_meetings.rev_proposal_text`/`rev_proposal_token` (unique partial idx)/`rev_proposal_email_sent_at`/`rev_proposal_response` (`'Approved'`|`'Propose an edit'`)/`rev_proposal_response_at`. 3 `email_templates` rows (`SPECIALIST_yes` id74, `SPECIALIST_no` id75, `SPECIALIST_step2_progress` id76 — all cc_list/bcc_list `[]`) + 1 `agreement_templates` row (id12, `Specialist`/`Single`, `html_body` holds the agreement HTML but the email attaches the static bucket PDF instead). Public bucket `specialist-onboarding-assets`. Routes `/specialist-sif` + `/specialist-revshare-decide` (both PUBLIC token pages, DecidePage-style confirmation screens). **All emails are Gmail DRAFTS** (match advisor/tax). The Stage 1 email's SIF button + the email's Approve/Propose-edit buttons point at the PRODUCTION URL — they only work end-to-end after `npm run deploy`; until then test on localhost by swapping the host. Stop at ANY stage fires the shared `SPECIALIST_no` decline email (currently wired on Stage 1 + Stage 2 Stop; later stages are a follow-up). **`pipeline_sandbox_config` has NO `SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING` row** → the email handlers default `isSandbox=false` (live recipient) — fine because the specialist flow only drafts Gmails (no Stripe/BoldSign yet); add a row before wiring any sandbox-aware external service (gotcha #9). See [docs/flows/specialist-onboarding.md](specialist-onboarding.md).

**60. Notifications can target a specific admin by email (not only `'admin'`/`'all'`).** `actions/notifications/load.ts` filters `recipient = session.email OR 'admin' OR 'all'`. So `recipient:'<login-email>'` is delivered ONLY to the admin whose `allowed_admins.email` matches (and is stored/compared lowercased — `create_admin` lowercases on insert). This session added **Tracy Miller** (`tnmiller@elitert.com`, role `admin`, created via the superadmin-only Admin Editor → Add Admin UI) and the specialist SIF-submit + rev-share-button-click handlers insert `recipient:'tnmiller@elitert.com'` notifications. A notification with a recipient email that has no matching `allowed_admins` row is stored but never shown (this is why they appeared "missing" mid-build before Tracy's account existed).

**61. AdminPortal now deep-links via `?tab` / `?section` / `?onboarding` query params (2026-06-02).** `pages/AdminPortal.jsx` previously drove tabs/sections from `sessionStorage` ONLY and ignored URL params; the advisor/tax notification links like `/admin?tab=...&section=...` therefore landed on the last-used tab. Added a `useEffect([location.search])` that, when `?tab` is present, sets `activeTab` + the matching per-tab section state (advisors/accountants/specialists/automation) and persists to sessionStorage. So ALL pipelines' notification links now deep-link, not just specialist. `SpecialistOnboarding.jsx` additionally reads `?onboarding=<id>` (via `useSearchParams`) to open that specialist's detail directly. Notification links are now `/admin?tab=specialists&section=specialist_onboarding&onboarding=<id>`.

**62. Specialist Stage 2 checklist checkboxes are toggleable pre-submit via an `'unchecked'` sentinel.** The shared `getTaskStatus` in `SpecialistOnboarding.jsx` now returns `null` for status `''` OR `'unchecked'` (treated as not-done). Because the backend `save-progress.ts` (and the frontend `saveProgress`) default an empty status to `'completed'`, unchecking writes the explicit string `'unchecked'`. Only the Stage 2 checklist passes `toggle` to `CheckItem` (allows clicking when already done); every other `CheckItem` usage still locks on first click. Lets the admin check/uncheck freely until the meeting is logged, and it persists across refresh.

**63. Specialist emails are sent the moment a meeting is logged — order matters for the rev-share token.** `SpecialistOnboarding.jsx` `logMeeting` saves the meeting FIRST (so `save-meeting.ts` mints `rev_proposal_token` when a proposal is attached), captures `result.meeting.id`, THEN calls `automation_SPECIALIST_stage2email` with `meeting_id` so the handler can read the proposal + token off the meeting and build the Approve/Propose-edit buttons. The "Still interested" buttons collapse to a single button (no date/no-date) when the current checks would complete ALL Stage 2 items (no next meeting to schedule).

**64. Specialist Stage 2 exec voting is ACCOUNT-SCOPED with server-side reveal (2026-06-03, v374).** `automation_SPECIALIST_execvote` (AUTH, admin-only) derives the voter from `auth.session.email` (`constants/specialist-execs.ts` maps `aanderson@elitert.com`→Anton, `platham@elitert.com`→Paul; anyone else → 403). Two rounds, stored in `specialist_onboarding_votes` with a NEW **`vote_round`** column (unique constraint swapped to `(onboarding_id, stage, voter_name, vote_round)` — `save-vote.ts` onConflict updated to match; stage-4 votes default `vote_round=1`). **`load.ts` (`load_onboarding`) redacts a vote's value (sets `vote=null`, `revealed=false`) for stage-2 votes UNLESS the caller is that voter OR both execs have voted that round** — real privacy, not just UI hiding. `load_onboarding` + `automation_SPECIALIST_execvote` are dispatched with `c.auth` (the only handlers that receive `AuthContext`). Outcomes: both-Approved (R1 or R2) → auto-draft `SPECIALIST_step3` + **auto-advance `current_stage` to 3** (done in `step3-email.ts`); both-Denied → `automation_SPECIALIST_deniedemail` + `status='stopped'`; round-2 split → DELETE round-2 votes + re-vote notif. The `SpecialistAutomationPanel` (admin overview) shows individual votes per exec per round — privacy applies only to the live voting UI, not the audit panel. **(2026-06-04 update)** `automation_SPECIALIST_execvote` is now **stage-aware** (`body.stage`, default 2) and `load.ts` redacts BOTH stage 2 and stage 4; Stage 4's final exec approval uses the identical 2-round account-scoped flow (see gotcha #73). **Stage-4 votes now go through `execvote`, NOT `save_onboarding_vote`.** Stage-4 both-Approved is a **no-op** (does NOT advance the stage — Stage-4 sign/pay isn't built yet); both-Denied → stop + decline. `vote_r2_opened_at` is stamped **only for stage 2** — stamping it for stage 4 triggered phantom Stage-2 sweep round-2 reminders (fixed 2026-06-04).

**65. Specialist background-check payment = first Stripe in the specialist pipeline (2026-06-03).** Webhook cascade in `router/webhooks.ts` gained a SPECIALIST branch after the accountant lookup miss (looked up by `bg_stripe_customer_id`; `metadata.pipeline='SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING'`/`payment_kind='background_check'`/`bg_type=core|max`) in BOTH `checkout.session.completed` (card → immediate chain; ACH → `processing`) and `payment_intent.succeeded` (ACH clearance), plus a new `payment_intent.payment_failed` block (marks `bg_payment_status='failed'` + Tracy FYI — catches ACH-settlement failures only; card declines never webhook). Card → `automation_SPECIALIST_bgconfirmation` + `automation_SPECIALIST_bgreceipt` both fire at once; ACH → confirmation at authorization, receipt on clearance. `bg-receipt.ts` generates **invoice + receipt PDFs** (`specialist-html-templates.ts`, blue-INVOICE / green-RECEIPT matching MAP1/PIP), numbers them via `document_numbers` (added a **`specialist_onboarding_id` FK** column, gotcha #42), and archives to Drive folder **`VFO Specialist Onboarding/<Name> - Specialist`** under the **hardcoded** parent `13eFhVvCgABtRgRbg0l4s2sUilOzMjrWb` (the shared folder that also holds the clients folder — NOT inside it). The `SPECIALIST_bg_receipt` email body is now **fully written** (receipt + next-steps + process image + DD-checklist buttons + Exhibit A rev-share copy); the `SPECIALIST_bg_confirmation|card`/`|ach` confirmations use MAP-1-style wording with `[CARD_FEE_TEXT]`/`[PROCESSING_TIME]` filled in `bg-confirmation.ts`. New `specialist_onboarding` columns: `bg_step3_email_sent_at`, `bg_checkout_token` (uniq idx), `bg_stripe_customer_id`, `bg_payment_intent_id`, `bg_payment_status`, `bg_payment_method_type`, `bg_acct_last4`, `bg_card_processing_fee`, `bg_payment_completed_at`, `bg_confirmation_email_sent_at`, `bg_invoice_number`, `bg_invoice_drive_id`, `bg_receipt_number`, `bg_receipt_drive_id`, `bg_receipt_email_sent_at`, `further_questions_token` (uniq idx), `further_questions_requested_at`, `further_questions_resolved_at`, `further_questions_resolution`. Email templates ids 77–82 (`SPECIALIST_denied`, `_step3`, `_step3_proceed`, `_bg_receipt`, `_bg_confirmation|card`, `|ach`).

**66. Specialist reminder sweep (`specialist-sweep-daily` 07:00 UTC).** `automation_SPECIALIST_sweep` (PUBLIC, service-role) mirrors the advisor/tax 48h-reminder/96h-PF-notify ladder. **As of this session it has 7 stalls** (the original per-meeting rev-share stall #2 + its `revButtons` helper + `SPECIALIST_revshare_reminder` template were **removed** as dead code): (1) SIF not submitted, (2) exec votes R1/R2, (3) Core/Max/Questions choice, (4) DD checklist not submitted (base `ddc_email_sent_at`, paused while `ddc_help_requested_at` set), (5) final rev-share unanswered (base `ddc_email_sent_at`, `rev_share_final_response` null), (6) agreement not signed (base `agreement_sent_at` — the signature reminder fetches a fresh BoldSign embedded link from `lic_boldsign_document_id`), (7) license payment not made (base `lic_payment_link_sent_at`, `lic_payment_status` null/failed). NO 14-day auto-decline. Reminder emails to the specialist (or only the missing exec(s) for votes); 96h are dismissible Tracy FYIs. Stage-4 timer-guard columns added this session: `{ddc_reminder_sent_at, ddc_pf_notified_at, rev_share_final_reminder_sent_at, rev_share_final_pf_notified_at, agreement_sign_reminder_sent_at, agreement_sign_pf_notified_at, lic_payment_reminder_sent_at, lic_payment_pf_notified_at}`. Timer bases stamped by: `prelim-email.ts` (`sif_email_sent_at`), `save-progress.ts` (`vote_r1_opened_at`), `exec-vote.ts` (`vote_r2_opened_at`), `step3-email.ts` (`bg_step3_email_sent_at`), `bg-receipt.ts` (`ddc_email_sent_at`), `agreement-send.ts` (`agreement_sent_at`), `license-payment-email.ts` (`lic_payment_link_sent_at`). Reminder templates: ids 83–86 + **97–100** (`SPECIALIST_ddc_reminder`/`_revfinal_reminder`/`_signature_reminder`/`_licpayment_reminder`). **Cron install pattern (key-safe):** a `DO` block read the advisor job's `command`, `replace()`-d the action to `automation_SPECIALIST_sweep`, and re-scheduled — so the `sb_secret` key never left Postgres. Doc: `supabase/cron/specialist-sweep.sql`.

Two new admin login accounts created this session for exec voting.** _(SUPERSEDED in part by #291: Tim Gacsy left ~2026-07-13, his `allowed_admins` row was deleted, and every notification hardcoded to him was rerouted on 2026-07-27 — the tax-risk-notes prompts now go to Tracy.)_ `allowed_admins`: **Tim Gacsy** (`tgacsy@elitert.com`, id 8 — receives tax-risk-notes action notifs) and **Anton Anderson** (`aanderson@elitert.com`, id 9 — Anton had NO admin login before; required because Stage 2 voting is account-scoped, gotcha #64). Paul Latham (`platham@elitert.com`, id 4) + Tracy (id 7) pre-existed. Created via the superadmin Add Admin UI (passcodes set by the user). Per gotcha #60, a notification to an email with no matching `allowed_admins` row is stored-but-invisible.

**68. Shared `utils/gmail-draft.ts` + `NotificationBell` global fixes (2026-06-03).** New `utils/gmail-draft.ts` (`getGmailAccessToken`, `draftGmail({to,subject,htmlBody,cc,bcc,attachments})`, `VFO_SIGNATURE`, `PORTAL_BASE`, `payLinks`, `token32`) dedupes the OAuth+MIME+drafts boilerplate across the new specialist handlers. `NotificationBell.jsx` gained three GLOBAL behaviors (apply to all pipelines): clicking a dismissible/FYI notification now marks it read + removes it; navigation appends a changing `_n=<ts>` nonce so re-clicking the SAME deep-link re-fires the destination's `location.search` effect (was a silent no-op); and a `window` `vfo:notifications-changed` event listener lets an admin action refresh the bell immediately (specialist notes-save + exec vote + further-questions-resolve dispatch it). The instant-refresh dispatch is currently wired only on specialist actions — extending it to other pipelines is a one-line add per call site.

**69. First PRIVATE Storage bucket — `specialist-dd-materials` (2026-06-04).** The Specialist DD Checklist collects sensitive uploads (headshot, licenses, E&O insurance, PLR/TOL, sample materials) so they live in a **private** bucket, not the public `specialist-onboarding-assets`. **Upload** (public token form `/specialist-ddc`, no login): `automation_SPECIALIST_ddcuploadurl` validates the `ddc_token`, then `supabase.storage.from(bucket).createSignedUploadUrl(path)` (service-role) returns an absolute signed URL; the browser **PUTs multipart `FormData`** (file under an empty field name + a `cacheControl` field, header `x-upsert:true`, NO Content-Type — browser sets the boundary) — this matches supabase-js `uploadToSignedUrl` for a Blob; a raw-binary PUT can fail. Paths are namespaced `<onboarding_id>/<slot>/<rand>_<file>`. **Download** (admin): `automation_SPECIALIST_ddcdownload` (AUTH) mints a 300s `createSignedUrl`, but first asserts `path.startsWith(`${onboarding_id}/`)` so an admin can't fetch an arbitrary object. No anon write/read policy on the bucket — all access is service-role-mediated via these two handlers. Direct `DELETE FROM storage.objects` is blocked by a trigger (`storage.protect_delete`) — use the Storage API to delete; orphaned objects from a reset are harmless (refs cleared from `ddc_data`).

**70. Rev-share final proposal keeps history — never overwrite `rev_share_prepared` (2026-06-04).** The Stage 2 final proposal text lives in `specialist_onboarding_progress` (stage 2, `task_key='rev_share_prepared'`) and the email + exec "View details" read it. When Tracy edits the proposal in Step 3 (`automation_SPECIALIST_revsharefinalize` with `final_text`), the edited text is written to a SEPARATE column `specialist_onboarding.rev_share_final_text` and `rev_share_prepared` is left untouched — so the UI shows Original + Edited, and the execs' view still reflects exactly what they voted on. The handler updates the progress row's notes ONLY with the `'edited'`/`'kept'` marker on `rev_share_finalized` (stage 3) — it does NOT route the text edit through `save_onboarding_progress` (which would re-fire the reviewer-notes/voting side effects keyed on `rev_share_prepared`). Effective final text = `rev_share_final_text || rev_share_prepared`.

**71. `ddc_review_log` jsonb paper trail (2026-06-04).** `specialist_onboarding.ddc_review_log` (jsonb array, default `[]`) records every DD review round so the admin tracker can show how many approve/deny cycles happened. Appended (read-modify-write, low concurrency) by: `ddc-submit.ts` `{type:'submitted'}`, `ddc-approve.ts` `{type:'approved'}`, `ddc-edits.ts` `{type:'denied', reason}`. The admin reads it via `load_onboarding` (select *) and renders each `denied`/`approved` entry as a bullet (denied shows its reason inline). Current review state is still `ddc_review_status` (`pending_review`/`approved`/`edits_requested`); the log is the immutable history.

**72. RESOLVED (2026-06-04) — `SPECIALIST_ddc_approved` email body is now written (template id 87).** No longer a TBD placeholder. It fires when Tracy clicks "DD Checklist Approved" (`automation_SPECIALIST_ddcapprove`) and now reports the status of the other two Stage-3 items via the shared completion-email helper (see gotcha #73). The other prior-session TBD (`SPECIALIST_bg_receipt`, id 80) was already written.

**73. Specialist Stage-3 completion emails + all-three gate + Stage-4 final exec approval (2026-06-04, branch `claude/epic-robinson-b8609d`).** NEW shared util `utils/specialist-stage3-emails.ts` exports `sendStage3CompletionEmail(supabase, onboardingId, completedItem)` (drafts the per-item "complete" email — `SPECIALIST_bg_passed` id 89 / `SPECIALIST_ddc_approved` id 87 / `SPECIALIST_revshare_complete` id 90 — each with a blue "Step 3 progress" card; idempotent via `<item>_complete_emailed` stage-3 progress markers) and `maybeAdvanceStage3(supabase, onboardingId)` (advances `current_stage` 3→4 ONLY when bg `bg_results_received='passed'` + `ddc_review_status='approved'` + rev `rev_share_final_response='Approved'` OR `rev_share_finalized`; on entry fires Tracy+Tim-if-tax notes reminders). Both are called from `save-progress.ts` (bg-passed branch), `ddc-approve.ts`, `revshare-final.ts` (Approved branch), `revshare-finalize.ts`. **Stage 4 final exec approval** reuses Stage 2's machinery: `exec-vote.ts` keys off `body.stage`; `load.ts` redacts stages 2 AND 4; `save-progress.ts` opens voting per stage (stage 2 also needs `rev_share_prepared`, stage 4 just the notes). Stage-4 both-Approved **now auto-sends the agreement** (`exec-vote.ts` `applyApproval(4)` chains `automation_SPECIALIST_sendagreement` — wired this session; see gotcha #75). Frontend (`SpecialistOnboarding.jsx`): `ExecVoteRow` + `castExecVote` take a `stage` arg; Stage 4 has reviewer-notes + 2-round vote + `Stage4DetailsDropdown` (which now also shows Tracy/Tim notes); the old Personal-folder/Details&benefits blocks + legacy advance button were removed and replaced with **7 AutoSteps** (agreement_sent / agreement_signed_specialist / agreement_signed_ceo / payment_link_sent / payment_made / confirmation_email_sent / invoice_receipt_sent) — **now driven** by the Stage-4 license flow (webhook chains mark them; first `invoice.paid` advances Stage 4→5). New email templates 89/90.

**74. `deno check` writes a v5 `deno.lock` the Supabase CLI bundler rejects.** After `deno check` (which creates/updates `supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/deno.lock` at lockfile **version 5**), the **local Docker** `supabase functions deploy vfo-admin-api` fails with `Unsupported lockfile version '5'` (the installed CLI 2.78.1 bundles with an older Deno). **Preferred fix (used this session): add `--use-api`** — `supabase functions deploy vfo-admin-api --project-ref ejpsprsmhpufwogbmxjv --use-api` bundles server-side (no local Docker Deno), which accepts the v5 lockfile and leaves it in place. Alternative: `Remove-Item supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/deno.lock` before a non-`--use-api` deploy (the lockfile is a gitignored throwaway; the bundler proceeds without it). Updating the CLI would also fix it.

**75. The $99/mo Specialist License is the FIRST Stripe SUBSCRIPTION in the codebase.** Everything else is one-time PaymentIntents or off-session sweeps. `actions/onboarding/license-checkout.ts` builds a Checkout Session with **`mode=subscription`** + inline monthly `price_data` (card grossed-up 2.9%+$0.30 via a higher `unit_amount`; ACH flat $99). This introduced **`invoice.paid` + `invoice.payment_failed`** handling in `router/webhooks.ts` — those two events **MUST be enabled on the Stripe webhook endpoint** or recurring billing + receipts never fire. They were added to the **sandbox** `vfo-admin-api` endpoint this session; the **LIVE** endpoint still has only `checkout.session.completed` + `payment_intent.succeeded` and needs them added before any real specialist pays. Auto-renewal is fully Stripe-managed (no custom sweep). The agreement auto-send (`exec-vote.ts` `applyApproval(4)` → `automation_SPECIALIST_sendagreement`) is **backgrounded via `EdgeRuntime.waitUntil`** so the voter's request returns under the frontend's 20s timeout; `sendagreement` was moved AUTH→PUBLIC with a svc-role/admin gate so the chain can call it.

**76. Stripe moved `invoice.subscription` off the top level in newer API versions.** A subscription invoice's subscription ref is now at **`invoice.parent.subscription_details.subscription`**, NOT `invoice.subscription` (which is absent). The license `invoice.paid` handler reads `invoice.subscription || invoice.parent?.subscription_details?.subscription`. A guard that requires only the top-level field silently skips every subscription invoice.

**77. Subscription `checkout.session.completed` and `invoice.paid` race — capture the payment method in whichever lands first.** For a card subscription Stripe fires both near-simultaneously. Original bug: `invoice.paid` arrived first, set `lic_payment_status='succeeded'` but NOT `lic_payment_method_type` (only the checkout handler set that), then chained `licconfirmation`/`licinvoicereceipt` which 400'd ("Payment not yet recorded"); then `checkout.session.completed` saw status already succeeded and skipped → nothing finished. Fix: the `invoice.paid` block now fetches the subscription's `default_payment_method` and sets `lic_payment_method_type`/`lic_acct_last4`/`lic_card_processing_fee` + marks `payment_made` **if not already set**, so it's self-sufficient regardless of event order.

**78. Notification idempotency must match by title + `read=false`, not just `dismissible=false`.** Multiple non-dismissible notifications can share the same recipient+link (e.g. Tracy's "submit the revenue share proposal" then "add your general notes"). The "already exists?" check in `save-progress.ts` previously matched any non-dismissible notif at that link, so a *cleared* (read=true) earlier prompt blocked creation of the next one. Always include `.eq('title', t.title).eq('read', false)` in these existence checks. Same reason the rev-share-submit handler now skips a reviewer who has already saved their notes (notes are always-visible, so they can be filled before the submit).

**79. Specialist agreement uses coordinate-based BoldSign (field_map) + a per-account BrandId.** `agreement_templates` id=12 (`pipeline=SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING`) now has a populated `field_map` (`specialist_address` p1; `specialist_signature`/`specialist_print_name`/`specialist_date` + `ert_signature`/`ert_date` p7) read from the user-built BoldSign template `a04ead86-…` via `/v1/template/properties`, plus `boldsign_template_id`. `agreement-send.ts` renders the HTML→PDF fresh each send and places fields by those coords (the signature page sits BEFORE the variable-length Exhibit A `[PROPOSED_REVENUE_SHARE]`, so coords are stable). **BrandId is per-BoldSign-account**: sandbox `6ca78a6b-85c6-4154-9512-d4606f8a87f5`, live `f6b2e092-73a4-438e-b786-ebd20e472732` — picked by `isSandbox` (an invalid brand for the active account is rejected). Test-clock subscriptions need a PM made from `tok_visa` (the shared `pm_card_visa` test method is not available on this account).

**80. PFT PF notifications route by `assigned_pf` name → login email; Rachael is hardcoded.** `actions/pft/_shared.ts` holds `PF_EMAILS` (`evan anderson`→`eanderson@`, `bridger silvester`→`bsilvester@`, `ian welham`→`iwelham@`) + `RACHAEL_EMAIL=rhopson@elitert.com`. `notifyAssignedPf` resolves the client's `assigned_pf` name via that map (falls back to `recipient:'admin'` if no match — i.e. no Assigned PF set, or a name not in the map); `notifyPfAndRachael` = PF + Rachael. So a PF only gets a *targeted* notification when (a) the client has an Assigned PF set in the Profile tab AND (b) that name is in `PF_EMAILS` AND (c) a matching `allowed_admins` login exists (gotcha #60). FT-email responses (confirm OR another-meeting) and the VFO-Associate decision notify PF+Rachael; discovery-complete / discovery-4-day / FT-4-day notify PF only. Add new PFs to BOTH `PF_EMAILS` and the `ClientDetail.jsx` Assigned-PF dropdown.

**81. Onboarding notifications deep-link to the specific record via `?onboarding=<id>`.** Accountant + Advisor onboarding panels now read `searchParams.get('onboarding')` on mount (mirrors Specialist's existing `?onboarding` per gotcha #61) and open that record's detail. ALL onboarding notification `link`s now append `&onboarding=<id>` — PFT (`ft-response`, `decision-email`), accountant/advisor `ceo-countersign` + `sweep` (the `addAccountantNotif`/`addAdvisorNotif` helpers — their old `void onboardingId` no-ops were removed) + `client-decision` (was bare `/admin`). When adding a new onboarding notification, always include the id so it lands on the person, not the list.

**82. VFO Associate accountants skip Stages 1 & 2 of Accountant Onboarding.** New `accountant_onboarding.accountant_type` column (`'VFO FT'` | `'VFO Associate'`), set on the "+ New Onboarding" form (required dropdown) and on the PFT handoff inserts. When `accountant_type==='VFO Associate'`: `AccountantOnboarding.jsx` hides Stage 1 (Preliminary Meeting) + Stage 2 (PC Admin) and unlocks Stage 3 immediately; `actions/accountant/create-member.ts` **bypasses the `invoice_sent_at` payment gate** for associates (they pay nothing). The detail header + list pill show the type. VFO FT (and legacy NULL) keep the full 3-stage flow.

**83. MAP 1 PF notifications route by Assigned-PF name → @elitert.com LOGIN email — NOT the @vfo-services.com CC map.** `constants/map1-pfs.ts` (`pfNotificationRecipient`) maps `clients.assigned_pf` (Evan/Bridger/Ian) → their `allowed_admins` login email (`eanderson@`/`bsilvester@`/`iwelham@`elitert.com), because the notification bell filters on `session.email`. `utils/pf-emails.ts` is a DIFFERENT map (the PFs' `@vfo-services.com` email-sending/CC identity) — routing a notification there silently lands in NO bell. Keep the two separate; add a new PF to BOTH `map1-pfs.ts` and the `ClientDetail.jsx` Assigned-PF dropdown. Falls back to `recipient:'admin'` (shared bell) when no PF is assigned or the name isn't mapped.

**84. Storage writes from the edge function MUST use the supabase-js storage client, NOT a hand-rolled `Authorization: Bearer <SERVICE_ROLE_KEY>` to `/storage/v1/object`.** The `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` env var is the new `sb_secret_…` format (gotcha #1); the Storage REST API rejects it with `403 Invalid Compact JWS`. `createClient(url, SERVICE_KEY).storage.from(bucket).upload(...)` / `.createSignedUploadUrl(...)` / `.createSignedUrl(...)` / `.list(...)` / `.remove(...)` all work fine (the JS client handles auth). This bit the throwaway PDF-to-bucket uploader mid-session.

**85. The CLIENT auth role is deny-by-default.** A third login type (`client_logins` + `client_login`) shares the `admin_sessions` table. `middleware/auth.ts` role precedence: `allowed_admins`→admin, else `client_logins`→client (sets `auth.callerClientId`), else member. A `client` session may ONLY call `CLIENT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (`client_vault_list`/`_upload_url`/`_download`/`_delete`) — any other action returns 403 BEFORE the admin/member gates.
