Those 4 handlers scope to `auth.callerClientId` (from the session, NEVER the body), so a client can only touch their own `<client_id>/` files. **CAVEAT:** if a client's email is also in `allowed_admins`, admin wins — fine for real clients, but for testing set a client's email to a non-admin address (a session created by `client_login` is re-typed as admin on the next request if the email matches an admin).

**87. `PIP1_reconfirmation|No` is shared by THREE handlers — don't delete it.** Besides the PIP confirmation step, the MAP 1 PCADMIN decline paths `actions/pipeline/pcadmin-final-decision.ts` (C15 final decision = No) and `pcadmin-extra-meeting.ts` (extra meeting = No) both load `PIP1_reconfirmation|No` with `.maybeSingle()` + `if (tmpl && client)` — so a **missing** template makes them **silently skip the decline email** (no crash, no draft). This session's PIP rework deleted all 3 `PIP1_reconfirmation` templates (`|Yes`/`|No`/`|No (member…)`) and migrated the PIP **confirmation step** to the new `PIP_meeting_confirm`/`PIP_meeting_declined`; the regression was caught in the staleness audit. Fix: re-created a **single neutral `PIP1_reconfirmation|No`** (id 125) and pointed both PCADMIN handlers at it unconditionally (no more member-variant branch). Lesson: grep the whole `actions/` tree for a `template_name` before deleting an `email_templates` row — these silent-skip guards hide the breakage. (`PIP1_reconfirmation|Yes` + the member `|No` variant stay deleted — nothing else referenced them.)

**86. `agreement_templates` uniqueness now includes `payer_type`.** The old `UNIQUE(pipeline, service_level, payment_plan)` was widened to `UNIQUE(pipeline, service_level, payment_plan, payer_type)` so a member-paid variant can coexist with the client one for the same level+plan. `contract-send-agreement.ts` selects with `.eq("payer_type", memberPays ? "member" : "client").eq("active", true)` and expects exactly one active row. Existing rows were backfilled to `payer_type='client'`; the 6 member-paid rows (ids 14–19) are `payer_type='member'`. If you add member rows for another pipeline, set `active=false` until the selecting handler filters `payer_type`, or you'll break the client `.maybeSingle()` with a multiple-rows error.

**88. Tax member-pays mirrors MAP 1, with TAX twists.** `client_tax_plans.member_paying_on_behalf` flips ~14 tax handlers (To member / Cc client + member email-template variant `(member signing/paying on clients behalf)` + member as signer/Stripe-payer + invoice Bill-To=member). The member agreement is `agreement_templates` **id 20** (TAX/Tax Planning/Single/`payer_type=member`) — so `actions/tax/send-agreement.ts` now MUST `.eq("payer_type", memberPays ? "member" : "client")` (per gotcha #86, now that 2 TAX agreement rows exist; without it the client `.maybeSingle()` breaks). The member agreement HTML wraps its member-contribution section in `<!--MC-->…<!--/MC-->`; `send-agreement.ts` **strips that block at render** — REQUIRED because the BoldSign `field_map` coords on id 20 were captured from the *stripped* PDF, so rendering the un-stripped HTML would shift the signature boxes. (Client-only emails — High Level Meeting confirm — don't flip.)

**89. Tax stores a $0 member share as NULL, not 0.** `tax/decision.ts` does `member_share = parseFloat(fd.memberShare) || null`, so $0 → NULL (MAP 1 stores a literal `0`). Any zero-share detection (e.g. the green "N/A" revshare pill on Client decision 1) must treat NULL as zero-share: `member_share == null || isZeroShare(member_share)`. Using MAP 1's bare `isZeroShare()` (which is `parseFloat('')===0` → `NaN===0` → false) silently fails for tax.

**90. Standalone-program notifications MUST carry `?program=<id>` in the link.** A client can be enrolled in BOTH VFO Holistic (program 1) AND VFO Tax Planning (program 4). `ClientDetail` loads ONE program per visit; from a notification (plain URL, no `location.state`) `msm/load-client-home` defaults to `clients.enrollment_id` (Holistic), so a Tax-Planning notification opened the WRONG program's tab. Fix: all tax notification `link`s append `&program=${plan.program_id}`; `ClientDetail.jsx` reads `?program=`; `load-client-home.ts` accepts `program_id` and resolves the matching enrollment (client_enrollments ⋈ member_enrollments) before defaulting. MAP1/PFT/Advisor/Acct were unaffected (single default program) — but any future standalone program needs this.

*[AMENDED 2026-08-14 — this regressed on a brand-new bell and was caught on LIVE, not in review, so treat the rule as a checklist item rather than a habit.]* `TAX_assess_vault_uploaded_pf` (the vault hand-off bell, #400) shipped in v743 with `link: /admin/client/<id>?tab=vault` and **no `&program=`**. Clicking it opened the client's **Holistic** profile — the exact failure this entry describes — because `load-client-home` fell back to `clients.enrollment_id`. Fixed to `?tab=vault&program=<plan.program_id || 1>` and the one already-minted live row was patched. **Two things generalise.** First, the omission is easy precisely because `?tab=vault` *looks* complete — the tab is right, so the link reads correct in review and only misbehaves for a dual-enrolled client, which is a minority of rows. Second, **a tax bell pointing at the VAULT tab needs `&program=` just as much as one pointing at the tax tab** — the program governs which enrollment `ClientDetail` resolves, not which tab it lands on. Grep any new tax `notifyByRule` call for `program=` before shipping it; the plan row is already in scope at every one of these call sites, so there is never a reason to omit it.

**91. Tax 4 "record the decision" reminder is IN-APP, not a daily email.** _(RECIPIENTS SUPERSEDED by #291 on 2026-07-27: the bell now goes to the assigned PF + the allocated tax planner + Tracy, with a `/tax-planner/...` link override on the planner's row per #292. The mechanism below is otherwise unchanged.)_ Replaced the old daily `TAX_meeting_nudge|Yes` Gmail. `revshare-sweep.ts` raises ONE persistent action-required notification (`dismissible:false`) to `tgacsy@elitert.com` (Tim) + `tnmiller@elitert.com` (Tracy) — title `Client decision 1 needed — <client>` — when `tax4_meeting_date < today` AND `post_review_decision IS NULL`, once per plan (guard `tax4_meeting_reminder_last_sent_at`); `postreview-decision.ts` clears it (`.ilike("title","Client decision 1 needed%")`). The date is set by the new **High Level Meeting Confirmation Email** step (`automation_TAX_highlevelmeeting_confirm`, task `status_options='tax_hlm_confirm'`, replaced the old `tax_meeting_date` date-picker). To TEST the reminder: confirm with a PAST date, then fire the sweep — but it has an internal service-role gate (plain curl → 401), so run it via `DO $$ DECLARE c text; BEGIN SELECT command INTO c FROM cron.job WHERE jobid=5; EXECUTE c; END $$;` (executes the stored cron command without the `sb_secret` key ever appearing in chat). (`tnmiller@elitert.com` = Tracy Miller — the code comments' "Trevor" was wrong.)

**92. Document numbers (INV/REC) are now allocated collision-safe via `utils/doc-numbers.ts` `allocateDocNumber()` — don't revert to insert-once.** `document_numbers.number` is `UNIQUE`. The starting sequence is a count (global for most invoices/receipts; per-owner for MAP 1 + Tax *receipts*), but a count can produce an already-taken number when a `client_ref` is reused (test clients sharing a member ref) or under a race. The OLD code did `count → build → insert` and **ignored the insert error**, so on a collision the counter silently stalled and the SAME number got reused (the bug that surfaced: a plan's retainer AND implementation receipts both landed on `…-0001`, so the implementation revshare falsely verified against the retainer's Tracy-sheet row). `allocateDocNumber` bumps the seq and retries until the UNIQUE insert succeeds. Wired into ALL INV/REC sites: tax retainer (`invoice-receipt.ts`) + implementation (`implementation-receipt.ts`), MAP 1 (`contract-invoice-receipt.ts`), PIP (`pip-invoice-receipt.ts`), advisor/accountant (`*/invoice-receipt.ts`), specialist bg (`bg-receipt.ts`) + license (`license-invoice-receipt.ts`). In production each client has a UNIQUE `client_ref` so collisions were rare — the bug only bit reused test refs — but the helper also closes the documented `document_numbers` race.

**93. `document_numbers` requires EXACTLY ONE owner (client/advisor/accountant/specialist).** Migration `document_numbers_widen_owner_check` rewrote the `document_numbers_exactly_one_owner` CHECK to `((client_id NOT NULL)::int + (advisor_onboarding_id NOT NULL)::int + (accountant_onboarding_id NOT NULL)::int + (specialist_onboarding_id NOT NULL)::int) = 1`. The OLD CHECK only permitted client_id OR advisor_onboarding_id — so **every accountant/specialist INV/REC insert silently failed the CHECK** (0 such rows ever persisted; their numbers were computed but never recorded, so the counter never advanced for them). When stamping a document number, set exactly one owner FK.

**94. MAP 1 `c24_email_sent` (and similar `*_email_sent` flags) are BOOLEAN, not `'Yes'`.** `map1/ClientTrackViewV2.jsx` had `c24_email_sent === 'Yes'` for the "Member notified of revenue share" step — but the column is a Postgres `boolean` (`true`), so `true === 'Yes'` is false and the step stayed grey forever. Fixed to `=== true`. When a MAP 1 track step stays grey after the action clearly happened, check whether it's comparing a boolean column to the string `'Yes'`. (Text status columns like `c18_ceo_signed`/`c17_client_signed` ARE `'Yes'`/`'No'` strings — know which is which.)

**95. Tax implementation: revshare must be explicitly kicked off, and ACH off-session needs `payment_method_types[]`.** (a) The tax-revshare sweep is **retry-only** (only re-fires plans already at `rev_share='Pending'`/`'Failed'`), so the implementation revshare must be *started* by an explicit chain — the Stripe webhook now chains `automation_TAX_revshare` (payment_kind=implementation) right after `automation_TAX_implementation_receipt`. Without it the implementation revshare never fired (the retry-only sweep skipped it forever). (b) `charge-implementation.ts` must set `payment_method_types[]` to the saved method's type (`us_bank_account`/`card`) on the off-session PaymentIntent — otherwise an ACH-paid retainer's implementation charge is rejected with "us_bank_account is not allowed for this PaymentIntent". (The retainer checkout already saves the ACH mandate via `setup_future_usage=off_session`; MAP 1's `contract-chargescheduled-sweep` always set `payment_method_types[]` and so always worked.)

**96. TAX notifications never use the shared `admin` bell — route via `utils/tax-notify.ts`.** _(RECIPIENTS SUPERSEDED by #291 on 2026-07-27: `TIM_EMAIL` is deleted, `TAX_OWNERS` is `[TRACY_EMAIL]` alone, and the Tax 4/5 client-decision bells go to `taxDecisionRecipients(assigned_pf, plannerEmail)` = assigned PF + allocated tax planner, Tracy fallback. The "never `admin`" rule and the PF map below still stand.)_ Every tax notification goes to a specific person: **Tax 3 / Setup-phase → assigned PF** (`taxPfRecipients(client.assigned_pf)`, falls back to Tim+Tracy when no mapped PF — never `admin`); **Tax 4/5 client-decision FYIs → Tim only** (`TIM_EMAIL`); **Tax 4 "Client decision 1 needed" meeting nudge → Tim + Tracy** (`TAX_OWNERS`); **Tax 4/5 Undecided 96h "hasn't responded — reach out" → assigned PF**. The PF→login-email map in `tax-notify.ts` mirrors `constants/map1-pfs.ts` (Evan/Bridger/Ian @elitert.com) but with a Tim+Tracy fallback instead of `admin`. The notification bell filters on `session.email` OR `'admin'` OR `'all'`, so a notification to a `@vfo-services.com` CC address (not a login) would land in no bell.

**97. MAP 1 "PC Admin" phase state is PIPELINE-DRIVEN, not task-progress-driven.** In `map1/ClientTrackViewV2.jsx`, the PC Admin phase body is special-rendered from the `pipeline_map1` row (the c15 decision + the payment/revshare auto-step cascade), and its `program_client_tasks` never receive a progress status. So the generic `getPhaseState` (which checks `progress[t.id]?.status` on non-`auto` tasks) left the PC Admin header dot grey/"Not started" forever even when every cascade bullet was green. Fixed by special-casing PC Admin in `getPhaseState` → `pcAdminPhaseState()`, which reads the pipeline row: declined (PIP-FU `No` / c15 `No`) or Yes-path-complete (revshare resolved/zero-share AND `c24_email_sent === true`/zero-share) → `done`; decision-made-but-cascade-in-progress → `active`. The top-of-track COMPLETED/PROGRESS % counter was likewise fixed to count PC Admin's non-`auto` tasks complete when the phase resolves done (else the % never reached 100). Lesson: any phase whose completion lives on a domain table rather than `program_client_tasks` needs its own state derivation.

**98. `html { overflow-y: scroll }` is load-bearing — don't revert to `auto`.** With `auto`, short pages have no scrollbar and long pages do, so the content width changes ~8px and the whole layout visibly shifts sideways on navigation. `src/index.css` forces the scrollbar track always-on (the styled slim scrollbar has a transparent track, so short pages just show a thin empty gutter). If a future change makes pages jump horizontally between routes, check this rule first.

**99. Frontend restyling is codemod-propagated — the repeating patterns are COPY-PASTED inline across ~70 files, not shared components.** Phase cards, task/AutoStep rows, status pills, progress headers, sub-tab bars, inputs are near-identical inline-style strings per file; restyle them with string/AST codemods (working examples lived in the redesign worktree root: `retheme*.mjs`, `contrast-pass.mjs`, `phase2a.mjs`, `phase2b.mjs` — session tooling, never committed; `@babel/parser` is available in node_modules). Keep-out zones when sweeping styles: `MemberWebsitePlugin.jsx` `member.bg_color || '#…'` hex defaults are member-website DATA, the `EmailTemplatesPanel.jsx` preview (white bg/#333/Arial) renders an email, and white text on colored/gradient buttons stays white. Design rules that must survive future passes: status colors live in bullet dots + pills ONLY (labels always ink #16264a, titles navy #002973); inset panels are tinted #eef2f9 with a real #dde5f2 border (never near-white on white).

**100. SPECIALIST is a FOURTH auth role (`specialist_logins`), deny-by-default like CLIENT.** `middleware/auth.ts` role precedence is now: `allowed_admins`→admin, else `client_logins`→client, else **`specialist_logins`→specialist** (sets `auth.callerSpecialistId` = the specialist's `experts.id`), else member. A `specialist` session may ONLY call `SPECIALIST_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (`specialist_vault_list`/`_upload_url`/`_download`/`_delete`) — any other action 403s. Those 4 handlers scope to `auth.callerSpecialistId` (from the session, NEVER the body) + path-prefix-check `<expert_id>/`, so a specialist can only touch their own files. `specialist_login` (pre-auth in index.ts) returns `role:"specialist"` + `expert_id`; sessions live in the shared `admin_sessions` table. **Same CAVEAT as CLIENT (#85): if a specialist's email is also in `allowed_admins`, admin wins** → the specialist session re-types to admin and can't use the vault (`callerSpecialistId` null → 403). For testing use a non-admin email/alias. `AuthContext` gained `callerSpecialistId`.

**101. Specialists are NOT members — go-live writes `experts` + `specialist_logins`, never `members`.** `automation_SPECIALIST_createspecialist` (the Stage-5 "Add to Showroom & Send Specialist Login" button) builds an `experts` directory row from the SIF (`sif_data.strategy_expertise`→`D&B_strategy_expertise`, `benefits_summary`→`D&B_summary_benefits`, `process_steps`→`D&B_professional_process`, etc.; the 4 `D&B_audit_risk_*` only when `sif_data.is_tax_specialist='Yes'`; `background_check` = `specialist_onboarding.background_check_type` Core/Max), copies the DD-checklist files into `specialist-documents/<expert_id>/`, writes back `specialist_onboarding.expert_id`/`expert_created_at`, then chains the login-setup email. Headshot, ecosystems, CIQ topics, tax-risk mindset, revenue share are NOT in the SIF → left for the admin to finish in Search Specialists. Login-setup mirrors advisor/accountant (token → `/member-setup` fallthrough advisor→accountant→specialist → set passcode → `/specialist/login`), but the login row goes to `specialist_logins` (separate portal), not `member_logins`. Idempotent on `expert_id`.

**102. `experts.id` has no sequence DEFAULT — assign max+1 on insert.** Both the manual Add Specialist form (frontend `SpecialistsPanel.jsx`) and `automation_SPECIALIST_createspecialist` compute `id = max(experts.id)+1` before insert (the pg_dump baseline never gave `experts.id` a default; a bare insert fails not-null). Low collision risk (admin-driven, rare). `save_specialist` re-sorts `sort_order` alphabetically after an insert.

**103. Stage-5 Specialist completion is gated on the headshot + bios checkboxes, with a Tracy notification ladder.** The "Add to Showroom & Send Specialist Login" button creates the specialist + sends login but does NOT complete the onboarding. Completion (`status='completed'`) happens in `save-progress.ts` only when BOTH stage-5 `headshot_added` AND `bios_added` are done. `getStageState` (admin tracker) returns `done` for ALL stages when `status='completed'`. Tracy (`tnmiller@elitert.com`) action-required ladder (all `dismissible:false`, title+unread-idempotent): first `invoice.paid` (Stage 4→5, in `license-invoice-receipt.ts`) raises "Send VFO Skool invite - <name>" + "Create the VFO Specialist & send login - <name>"; `automation_SPECIALIST_skoolinvite` clears the first, `automation_SPECIALIST_createspecialist` clears the second and raises "Add headshot (white background) for <name>" + "Add specialist bios for <name>"; ticking each checkbox (`save-progress.ts`) clears the matching one. The "Open specialist →" Stage-5 links + `?expert=<id>` deep-link auto-open that specialist in Search Specialists (which now has **Edit Specialist | Vault** tabs).

**104. The card-fee DISPLAY must use the gross-up everywhere — naive `base*0.029+0.30` under-states vs the actual charge.** Every backend checkout charges the gross-up `(base+0.30)/(1-0.029)` (the amount that nets `base` after Stripe's 2.9%+$0.30), and the webhook stores `card_processing_fee = amountReceived - base` (the real gross-up fee) — so charges + receipts are correct. But 4 frontend pay pages (`PayPage`/`TaxPayPage`/`PipPayPage`/`SpecialistPayPage`) were *displaying* the naive `base*0.029+0.30`, ~$0.83 low on $950 (showed $977.85 vs Stripe's $978.68). Fixed all 4 to `const cardTotal = round((base+0.30)/(1-0.029)*100)/100; const cardFee = round((cardTotal-base)*100)/100` — matching Advisor/Accountant pay pages (already correct). Display-only; no charge logic changed. When adding a new pay page, copy the AdvisorPayPage formula, NOT the naive one.

**105. Specialist portal pages mirror the CLIENT portal pattern.** `SpecialistLogin.jsx` (≈`ClientLogin`), `SpecialistPortal.jsx` (≈`ClientPortal` but ONE Vault tab, no Showroom), `components/specialist/SpecialistVault.jsx` (≈`ClientVault` but a single "General Documentation" section — same wording "Any other documents you would like to share with your VFO team."). Routes `/specialist/login` + `/specialist` in `App.jsx`. `MemberSetupPage.jsx` falls through advisor→accountant→**specialist** and redirects specialists to `/specialist/login` (portal label is kind-aware). Admin-side `SpecialistAdminVault.jsx` (Search Specialists → Vault tab) is read-only (list + view, no delete).

**106. Direct delete from `storage.objects` is gated by a trigger with an escape hatch: `set storage.allow_delete_query='true'`.** The `protect_objects_delete` BEFORE-DELETE trigger calls `storage.protect_delete()`, which RAISES unless `current_setting('storage.allow_delete_query', true)='true'`. So a privileged SQL session (e.g. the Supabase MCP) CAN delete object rows by running `select set_config('storage.allow_delete_query','true', false);` first, then `delete from storage.objects where bucket_id=… and name like '<prefix>/%'`. This removes the objects from ALL listings/vaults (the underlying blob may linger orphaned in the backend store but is invisible — harmless). Use this for test-data cleanup when no local `.env.local`/service-role key is available to call the Storage API. (`.env.local` does NOT exist in the worktree filesystem — gitignored and not present — so the supabase-js Storage client can't be run locally for deletes.)

**107. A panel that early-returns a different component BEFORE its hooks can't switch in place — give it a `key`, and deep-link navigation needs a changing nonce (2026-06-10).** `SpecialistsPanel.jsx` does `if (section === 'specialist_onboarding') return <SpecialistOnboarding/>` **before** its ~15 `useState`/`useEffect` hooks. When the section prop flips between `specialist_onboarding` and `specialist_search` on the SAME mounted instance, React sees the hook count jump 0↔15 and the render trips ("rendered more/fewer hooks") — intermittently leaving the old view on screen. Fix: `AdminPortal.jsx` renders `<SpecialistsPanel key={specialistsSection} …>` so a section change is a clean unmount/remount (no in-place hook-count change), which ALSO re-runs the panel's `?expert=` deep-link effect on mount. Second half of the same bug: `navigate()` to a URL identical to the current one is a **no-op** in react-router (no `location.search` change → effects don't re-fire, no error). The Stage-5 "Open specialist →" link therefore worked once then did nothing on repeat. Fix: append a changing `&_n=${Date.now()}` nonce (same pattern as `NotificationBell`, gotcha #68) so every click changes the URL. Proper structural fix (deferred): move the early-return AFTER the hooks.

**108. Two webhook events that both chain the same idempotent send race a read-then-write guard — claim atomically (2026-06-10).** A **card** Stripe subscription fires `checkout.session.completed` AND `invoice.paid` (billing_reason `subscription_create`) near-simultaneously, and `router/webhooks.ts` chains `automation_SPECIALIST_licconfirmation` from BOTH (the dual-chain is deliberate — gotcha #77: `invoice.paid` can arrive first and `checkout.session.completed` then skips). The handler's `if (lic_confirmation_email_sent_at) return` guard is **check-then-act**: both concurrent calls read null before either writes → **two confirmation emails**. Fix in `license-confirmation-email.ts`: replace the read-guard's write with an **atomic claim** — `update … set lic_confirmation_email_sent_at = now() where id = ? and lic_confirmation_email_sent_at is null` returning rows; only ONE caller gets a row back and proceeds to draft, the loser returns early. Roll the stamp back to null if the Gmail draft itself fails so a retry can send. (ACH doesn't hit this — its two events are days apart.) General pattern for any idempotent send two events can trigger together.

**109. `experts.id` has no sequence, so a deleted id is RECYCLED by the next go-live — clean the orphaned vault folder on reset (2026-06-10).** `automation_SPECIALIST_createspecialist` assigns `id = max(experts.id)+1` (gotcha #102). When a test specialist with the current max id is deleted, the NEXT go-live reuses that exact id, and its DD-file copy writes to `specialist-documents/<recycled_id>/`. So after deleting a test specialist you may find an orphaned vault folder under an id now reused by someone else — find orphans with `select split_part(name,'/',1) from storage.objects where bucket_id='specialist-documents' and split_part(name,'/',1) not in (select id::text from experts)` and delete via the gotcha-#106 escape hatch. Deleting an `experts` row cascades its `specialist_logins` (FK CASCADE) and NULLs `specialist_onboarding.expert_id` (FK SET NULL) — so after the expert is gone you can't recover its id from the onboarding row; capture it before deleting, or use the orphan query.

**110. Frontend design kit conventions + phase-state DISPLAY semantics (screen redesign, 2026-06-10).** Three shared presentational kits are now the canonical building blocks — reuse them instead of hand-rolling: **`components/shared/TrackKit.jsx`** (`TrackHero` hero card with gradient strip/eyebrow/title/dot-meta/% bar/phase stepper — `accent={false}` drops the strip, omitting `title` turns it into a pure progress card; `PhaseBadge` numbered circle for phase/stage headers; `ListHeader` navy title + count chip), **`components/shared/TokenShell.jsx`** (branded wrapper for ALL public token pages — navy header + wordmark + centered card; wrap every branch incl. loading/error), and **`components/admin/automation/StepKit.jsx`** additions (`PanelHero` with integrated stat row, `EmptyState`, `TableCard`). Hero hierarchy rule: ONE full hero per screen — nested headers go flat (program header) or progress-only (90 Day Plan). Status on heroes = dot + ink text, not pills. Phase-state DISPLAY rules changed (display-only — saves untouched): MAP 1 **Allocation**'s two "Move to …" tasks count as alternatives (either one = done; unused one excluded from totals once one is set); **Tax 3** shows done only when the retainer cascade ended AND every visible task is statused (decline path included — set "No show" etc. to green it); **`tax_hlm_confirm`/`tax_presentation_link`** count from `client_tax_plans` columns (they have no `client_tax_progress` rows); **Regular**'s "Additional information required" children (DB-resident in MAP 3) count with MAP 2 where they render, and only when info is required. If a track's dots/% look "wrong", check these rules before suspecting data. **Addendum (2026-06-11):** the MAP 1 **PC Admin** phase header "In progress · X/Y" pill is ALSO pipeline-driven, not progress-driven — its `program_client_tasks` rows never get a status, so the generic `doneTasks/nonAutoTasks.length` reads "0/N" (N = the raw DB row count, unrelated to the branch-dependent sub-steps shown). `ClientTrackViewV2.jsx` now derives the pill from `pcAdminStepCounts()`, which mirrors the body's `autoStep()` done-conditions for the active decision branch (Yes / Undecided→Yes / Undecided→ExtraMeeting). KEEP that helper in sync with the PC Admin body IIFE if the autoStep rows change. (PC Admin done/active state itself already comes from `pcAdminPhaseState()`; the header count was the missing piece.)

**111. `save_member` 500s with "Empty or invalid json" when called WITHOUT `settings` — pre-existing backend bug, exclusions never saved. FIXED + DEPLOYED v454 (2026-06-11).** `actions/members/save.ts` unconditionally ran `member_plugin_settings.update(settings)` first; the admin Specialists tab (`MembersPanel.jsx`) and member-portal specialists view (`MemberPortal.jsx`) send only `{member_number, exclusions}`, so `.update(undefined)` → PostgREST rejected the empty body → handler returned 500 BEFORE the `member_exclusions` delete/insert. Net effect: specialist enable/disable saves silently never persisted from those two screens (the Website Plugin path passes `settings` and worked). **Fix shipped:** the settings update is now guarded `if (settings && Object.keys(settings).length > 0) { … }`; exclusions delete/insert runs unconditionally as before. Deployed in `vfo-admin-api` v454. General lesson: a single handler serving two callers with disjoint payload shapes must guard each table write by whether its key was actually sent.

**112. Running `deno check` locally before `supabase functions deploy` regenerates `deno.lock` at version 5, which the CLI bundler's embedded Deno rejects (2026-06-11).** `supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/deno.lock` is gitignored (absent in a fresh worktree). Running the verification-gate `deno check` with the local Deno 2.7.x writes a v5 lockfile; the next `supabase functions deploy` then fails at bundle time with `Unsupported lockfile version '5'. Try upgrading Deno or recreating the lockfile`. Fix: delete the lockfile (`rm supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/deno.lock`) and re-run the deploy — it bundles fine without it (or regenerates a compatible one). It's safe to delete (gitignored, regenerable). Order of operations after a backend change: `deno check` (gate) → if deploying, `rm deno.lock` → `supabase functions deploy`.

**113. The in-portal "Showroom" tab is a NATIVE React reproduction (`MemberShowroom.jsx`), NOT the hosted embeddable widget (2026-06-12).** The embeddable plugin members paste on their own websites is a standalone artifact in Storage (`vfo-widget/vfo-widget.js`, **not in the repo**); it ALWAYS fetches `member_plugin_settings?manage_key=…` and applies that member's custom colours/font — there is no standard-mode/override (only `data-vfo-key` + `data-container` attributes). So the portal's Showroom tab can't reuse it without inheriting per-member styling. Instead `components/member/MemberShowroom.jsx` reproduces the widget's structure/CSS in React with a hardcoded `S` settings object (standard portal colours bg #fff / text #002973 / card #0b4bad / card-text #fff / accent #fb895a; last-initial-only ON, size 14, filter mode, search+count on) and reads NONE of `member_plugin_settings`. Identical on every account — only the specialist list differs (experts minus that member's `member_exclusions`, categories from `vfo_ecosystem_assignments`). The SAME component renders in all three portals: member (`MemberPortal.jsx`, full-width), admin (`MembersPanel.jsx` member-detail Showroom tab — `ecoMap` is threaded down through `MemberDirectoryView`), client (`ClientPortal.jsx`). If the hosted widget's look ever changes, this React copy must be updated to match (two sources of truth — accepted; the alternative was editing the global hosted widget that everyone embeds).

**114. Client-portal data beyond the vault needs a dedicated `callerClientId`-scoped action — clients are fenced to `CLIENT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (2026-06-12).** A `client` session may ONLY call the allowlist (was 4 vault actions; now +`client_showroom_load`); `load_data` etc. are denied (and would leak every member's data anyway). `actions/data/client-showroom-load.ts` (AUTH) reads `auth.callerClientId` → `clients.member_number` (the connected member, text col) → returns experts + `vfo_ecosystem_assignments` + that member's `member_exclusions` only (no member PII, no appearance settings). Scoped server-side to the caller's own client id — a client can never pass another id. Pattern for any future client-portal data need: add a `callerClientId`-scoped handler, register in `dispatch.ts` AUTH_HANDLERS, AND add the name to `CLIENT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (forget the last step → the handler 403s for clients).

**115. A modal that locks page scroll must use `useLayoutEffect` + scrollbar-width padding, or the screen flickers/shifts (2026-06-12).** Because `html { overflow-y: scroll }` is load-bearing (gotcha #98), a modal that sets `html`/`body` `overflow:hidden` removes the scrollbar gutter → content reflows ~15px wider. Two-part fix in `MemberShowroom.jsx`'s modal: (a) **reserve the gutter** — measure `window.innerWidth - documentElement.clientWidth` BEFORE locking and re-add it as `documentElement.style.paddingRight`; (b) run the lock in **`useLayoutEffect`, not `useEffect`**, so it applies before the browser paints the modal's first frame — a `useEffect` lock lands one frame late and the background visibly jumps as the modal appears. The modal/overlay entrance animations were also removed (instant open) to kill any residual flicker. Restore overflow + padding on cleanup.

**116. The Tax flow's `program_client_phases`/`program_client_tasks` are DUPLICATED per program — a change to a tax step must be applied to BOTH copies (2026-06-12).** Holistic Tax Priorities (program 1, phases 18–24) and standalone Tax Planning (program 4, phases 25–32) have SEPARATE task rows, even though they share the same `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, the same `TAX` email templates, and the same `client_tax_plans` table. Example: 'Tax 6 - Implementation' is phase 24 (prog 1, tasks 109–113) AND phase 32 (prog 4, tasks 142–146). When editing a tax step (rename / `status_options` / delete) update the matching task in BOTH phases or the two programs drift. Contrast: **Regular Priorities is program 1 ONLY** (`track_type='regular'`, phases 7–10), so its step edits touch one set. The tax tab's new Live/Stopped toggle (`msm_update_tax_status`) writes `client_tax_plans.status`, shared by both programs — mirrors Regular's `msm_update_priority_status` on `client_priority_tracks.status`.

**117. "New Model Sale" team email = `email_templates` row id 155 (pipeline `TEAM`/`new_model_sale`) that sends TO `email_templates.to_list` (new jsonb col), not cc/bcc (2026-06-15).** Fired by `utils/new-model-sale-email.ts` from advisor/accountant `create-member` (the final onboarding click). `to_list` is the editable "Team (To)" recipient list — `EmailTemplatesPanel.jsx` special-cases the `TEAM` pipeline to show a single Team-recipients editor (bound to `to_list`) instead of CC/BCC, and `automation_save_email_template` (`actions/email-templates/save.ts`) persists `to_list` via `sanitizeEmails`. Sandbox drafts To the pipeline's `sandbox_email`. Send-once via an atomic claim on `<table>.new_model_sale_email_sent_at` (gotcha #108 pattern); a draft failure rolls the stamp back. Body has 3 sections (Member details / Billing & contract / Platform access); the agreement bullets (Type of agreement / Agreement selection(s) / Agreement signed) live under Billing, and "Agreement signed" = `formatLongDate(invoice_sent_at)` (matches the receipt date). Frontend `NewModelSaleModal.jsx` (opens on the Stage-3 Create button) collects closer/setter/introduced-by + Company/Website + member-introduction and passes them into `create-member`; it exports `SALES_TEAM_NAMES` (the 5 hardcoded dropdown names — Rachael Hopson/Ian Welham/Anton Anderson/Paul Latham/Seth Hartford), reused by the Stage-1 Team Member step.

**118. Advisor/accountant onboarding notifications route to the onboarding's chosen "Team Member Responsible" (2026-06-15).** New `constants/onboarding-team.ts` `teamMemberRecipient(name)` maps the 5 team names → their `@elitert.com` login email (fallback `admin`). The Stage-1 "Team Member Responsible" dropdown step (after Preliminary Meeting; accountant **VFO-FT only** — it lives inside Stage 1 which Associates skip) saves `<table>.onboarding_team_member` via `save_advisor_team_member`/`save_accountant_team_member` (AUTH) + stamps `onboarding_team_member_at`, and **gates the Decision buttons** until set. The sweep 96h stalls, the clicked-Yes/No FYIs, and a NEW **"ready to create" action-required** (`dismissible:false`, raised in `invoice-receipt.ts` when `invoice_sent_at` is stamped, cleared in `create-member.ts` on create) all route via `teamMemberRecipient(onboarding_team_member)`, falling back to the shared `admin` bell when unset. **Seth Hartford's login (`shartford@elitert.com`) does not exist yet** → routing to Seth is stored-but-invisible until the user creates it (gotcha #60). `Plan-checkbox read failed` deliberately stays on `admin` (dev/diagnostic, not a closer task).

**119. Advisor/accountant onboarding AUTH actions are now in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (security fix, 2026-06-15).** `middleware/auth.ts:116` only 403s a **member** for actions ON `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`; any AUTH action NOT listed is allowed for members by default. The 16 advisor/accountant onboarding admin actions (`load_*_onboardings`/`_onboarding`, `create_*_onboarding`, `save_*_prelim_meeting`, `save_accountant_partnership`, `save_*_team_member`, `automation_{ADVISOR,ACCOUNTANT}_decision`, `automation_{ADVISOR,ACCOUNTANT}_createmember`, `automation_load_{advisor,accountant}_pipelines`) were unlisted → a logged-in member could call them. Now gated. The PUBLIC token/chain/cron handlers (sendagreement, clientdecision, sweep, ceocountersign, *email, stripecustomer, etc.) are intentionally NOT listed (they bypass the auth gate by design). NOTE: other non-advisor/accountant admin AUTH actions may have the same latent gap — not audited this session.

**120. Advisor/accountant agreements no longer carry an Effective Date (2026-06-15) — matches specialist.** `[EFFECTIVE_DATE]` deleted from `agreement_templates.html_body` ids **9** (advisor) + **10/11** (accountant), so the recital renders "effective as of  (the Effective Date)" blank — same as specialist (id 12). The `.replace(/\[EFFECTIVE_DATE\]/g, …)` in `advisor/send-agreement.ts` + `accountant/send-agreement.ts` is now a **dead no-op** (left in place; the today's-date computation is also dead). The static Undecided-path bucket PDFs (`advisor-onboarding-agreements/Advisor_Implementation_Agreement.pdf`, `accountant-onboarding-agreements/Accountant_Implementation_Agreement_{Partnership,No_Partnership}.pdf`) were regenerated from the stripped HTML via a throwaway `temp-agreement-pdfs` edge function (deployed `--no-verify-jwt`, html2pdf→Gmail draft, then **deleted**). The separate "6-month transition / renewal" date (`renewal_date`, payment+6mo rounded to the 15th — gotcha in `router/webhooks.ts`) is UNCHANGED and distinct from the Effective Date.

**121. Onboarding detail self-heals a stale deep-link to a deleted record (2026-06-15).** `AdvisorOnboarding.jsx` + `AccountantOnboarding.jsx`: a stale `?onboarding=<deleted id>` (left in the URL after a pre-deletion notification click) used to 404 on `load_*_onboarding` and stick on a bare "Onboarding not found." Now `loadDetail` bounces back to the list on a not-found (catch matching `"not found"` OR a null `data.onboarding`), `onBack` clears the `onboarding`+`_n` URL params (so it can't re-fire), and the not-found render gained a "Back to list" button. Frontend-only.

**122. Action-required Jake failure alerts need a programmatic auto-clear, or they become permanent clutter (2026-06-15).** `utils/notify-jake-failure.ts` `actionRequired:true` makes a notification non-dismissible. Only use it for failures with a reliable recovery event that calls `clearJakeFailure(title)` / `clearJakeFailuresContaining(substr)` — rev-share success, `customer.subscription.updated`→active, `charge.dispute.closed`. Everything resolved out-of-band (ACH first-payment bounce, refund failure, transfer reversed, bg / quarterly / implementation charge fail) stays a **dismissible** FYI, else it persists forever. Second trap: when one event BOTH posts a notice AND clears by a shared key, **clear BEFORE posting** — `charge.dispute.closed` posts "Chargeback closed (won/lost) … (charge X)" whose title contains the charge id, so calling `clearJakeFailuresContaining("charge X")` afterward marked the outcome read too (came back `read=true`); reorder to clear-then-post so the won/lost outcome stays visible (the v465 fix, caught by synthetic-event verification).

**123. A client enrolled in BOTH Holistic (program 1) and standalone Tax Planning (program 4) has two `client_tax_plans` rows; the Tax tab's plan picker must show the program or the wrong plan gets paid (2026-06-15).** `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` loads ALL of a client's tax plans by `client_id` only (`tax_load_plans` → `load-plans.ts`, no program filter) and the picker rendered each as a bare "Tax Plan" + created_at with NO program. Two same-looking rows → an admin clicks the wrong one → the decision + payment chain run against that `tax_plan_id` (the backend handlers correctly use the passed id — the bug is the UI sending the wrong one; the payment then lands on e.g. the program-1 Holistic plan when the admin meant standalone Tax Planning). Fixed by labelling each plan row `plan.program_id === 4 ? 'VFO Tax Planning' : 'VFO Holistic Planning · Tax Priorities'`. The track/phases ARE program-scoped (`TaxPrioritiesTab` line ~1842 loads by `programId`) but the plan list was not. Discovered via the new Payments tab (a "Tax Planning" retainer showed a HOLISTIC tag because it had been paid onto the client's program-1 plan). **FE half fixed 2026-07-23 — `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` now program-scopes the plan list at its single state entry point via `plansForProgram` (NULL = Holistic), so a program view renders ONLY its own plans; the backend `tax_load_plans` no-filter behaviour and the program-4-gated upload auto-stamp asymmetry remain — see #269.**

**124. Date-only columns are stamped in UTC — render them from a timestamptz field, not the date string, or evening payments show the next day (2026-06-15).** `pipeline_map1.pay{N}_date`, `client_tax_plans.retainer_date`/`implementation_charge_date`/`refund_date`/`deposit_refund_date` are Postgres `date` columns set by the webhook via `new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]` = the **UTC** calendar day. A payment at 8:37 PM Eastern (00:37 UTC) stamps the *next* day. The Payments-tab `normalize.ts` builders therefore prefer a `timestamptz` field where one exists (`invoice_email_sent_at` for MAP 1 pay1, `pay{N}_paid_at` for 2–4, `retainer_invoice_email_sent_at` for the tax retainer); `PaymentsTable.fmtDate` renders an ISO timestamp in the browser's local tz (correct day) but renders a date-only `YYYY-MM-DD` **literally** (no tz shift — `new Date("YYYY-MM-DD")` parses as UTC midnight and slips back a day for users west of UTC). Implementation/refund/deposit have no timestamp alternative → still date-only (can read a day ahead for a late-evening charge).

**125. The 3 `*_payments_load` actions are portal-ready but admin-gated (2026-06-15).** Each handler in `actions/payments/` has a role branch — `client`→`auth.callerClientId`, `member`→`auth.callerMemberNumber`, `specialist`→`auth.callerSpecialistId` (ignores any passed id) — but all 3 are in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, so only admins reach them now. To expose the read-only view in the self-service portals (Track 3 chat): move `client_payments_load`→`CLIENT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`, `specialist_payments_load`→`SPECIALIST_ALLOWED_ACTIONS`, and remove `member_payments_load` from `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (it already self-scopes). No handler change needed.

**126. New `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` gate locks the Automation tab to Jake — and PUBLIC_HANDLERS bypass auth entirely, so you can only "gate" an AUTH action (2026-06-16).** `middleware/auth.ts` gained a gate (after the client/specialist deny gates, BEFORE the admin-only gate): `if (SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS.includes(action) && !isSuperadmin) return 403`. The list (`constants/role-gates.ts`, 12 entries) is the Automation-panel-EXCLUSIVE actions only — each is fired by exactly one Automation-tab panel and nowhere else (grep-verified). Frontend `AdminPortal.jsx` hides the Automation `NavDropdown` (+ 2 entry guards) unless `session.is_superadmin`. **The trap that cost a deploy:** `index.ts` dispatches `PUBLIC_HANDLERS` BEFORE `authenticate()`, so any action in PUBLIC_HANDLERS never reaches the superadmin (or admin-only) gate. The first gate attempt missed `automation_TAX_paidbycheck`/`_checkcleared`/`automation_load_tax_plans` because they were PUBLIC — gate-testing as a synthetic non-Jake admin caught the 200, and they had to be MOVED to AUTH_HANDLERS first (see #127). Lesson: before "gating" an action confirm it's in AUTH_HANDLERS; a PUBLIC action can't be gated by middleware. KEEP OFF the list: `automation_load_pipeline_data` (shared with the client-detail MAP 1 track tab) + onboarding-workflow + per-client track-tab actions. Verify the partition via the synthetic-`admin_sessions` gate-test trick (insert token+email+expires_at; email decides role; delete after).

**127. Tax-pipeline auth hardening — 8 admin-clicked Tax actions made AUTH + ADMIN_ONLY, mirroring MAP 1's `PCADMIN_*` (2026-06-16).** A systemic pre-existing gap: client-detail Tax admin actions were either PUBLIC/no-auth (`automation_TAX_pricing`/`_extrameeting`/`_save_meeting_date` — callable with no token) or AUTH-but-member-callable (`automation_TAX_decision`/`_readyfortax3`/`_highlevelmeeting_confirm`/`_presentation_schedule`/`_depositrefund` — not in ADMIN_ONLY), whereas MAP 1's equivalents were already AUTH + ADMIN_ONLY. Fix: moved the 3 PUBLIC ones to `AUTH_HANDLERS` + added all 8 to `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`. **Admin-only, NOT superadmin** — the per-client Tax track tab (`TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`) must stay usable by all admins; only the Automation TAB is Jake-only. `automation_TAX_pricing`/`_extrameeting` forward `req.headers.get("Authorization")` + `body.token` to a chained `automation_TAX_sendagreement`; they keep `c.req` after the move (AuthedHandler context provides it) and the chain still authenticates via the forwarded `body.token`, exactly like MAP 1's AUTH `automation_PCADMIN_pricing`. Verified end-to-end live (a real admin pricing run drove sendagreement → agreement signed). Other PUBLIC admin actions may still have this gap — not audited beyond Tax this session.

**128. MAP 1 Payments-tab must add the card fee to EVERY card installment, not just payment 1 (2026-06-16).** `actions/payments/normalize.ts` `buildMap1Rows` displayed `base + (i === 1 ? cardFee : 0)`, so scheduled quarterly card installments 2–4 showed the bare base (e.g. $1,350) — but `contract-chargescheduled-sweep.ts` charges every card installment the SAME gross-up `(base + 0.30) / (1 - 0.029)` (≈$1,390.63); only ACH charges base. So the Payments tab under-stated scheduled card rows. Fixed to `base + (isCard ? cardFee : 0)` (`isCard = payment_method_type === "card"`); the saved per-installment `card_processing_fee` is identical across equal quarterly installments, so reusing it is exact. Display-only — actual charges were always correct. (Distinct from gotcha #104, the pay-PAGE card-fee gross-up.)

**129. The admin GLOBAL Payments page is DB-only + has its OWN rev-share-payout source, distinct from the per-person tabs (2026-06-16).** `all_payments_load` (`actions/payments/all-payments-load.ts`, superadmin-only) aggregates every DB-resident payment across all clients/members/specialists and tags each row with `person`+`personType`. By design it does NOT do the slow per-person live-Stripe fan-out, so member rev-share payouts + specialist month-by-month license invoices are NOT pulled from Stripe here. Instead it DERIVES rev-share payout rows from the DB (`pipeline_map1.rec{N}_rev_paid='Yes'` → a money-out row to the connected member; share = `member_share`/4 for Quarterly, mirroring `contract-revshare.ts`). **Consequence: the global page and the per-person Member tab can show the SAME payout from two sources** — global = DB-derived (fast); per-person = the exact Stripe transfer ledger. Specialist license history stays per-specialist-tab only. The shared `PaymentsTable.jsx` renders both modes: it auto-shows a Person column + person-type ("Who") filter only when rows carry `person`; it folds rows whose `key` matches `map1-<id>-p<n>` / `tax-<id>-<kind>` into one expandable chevron parent (standalone payments untouched); and `AllPaymentsTab` passes a `buckets` prop (Payments received vs Revenue share payouts, keyed on `category==='Rev-share payouts'`) that REPLACES the per-person category/Who chips on the global view. The `fee` field on `PayRow` is the card-fee portion INCLUDED in `amount` (base = amount − fee), set on MAP 1 card + specialist bg rows only — tax/PIP/onboarding fee semantics were not confirmed, so those show a single amount.

**130. Phase D — admin-initiated payment-method change: setup-mode Checkout + per-engagement Stripe customers (2026-06-16).** An admin (Jake-only — `payments_send_card_update` in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`) drafts a `/update-card?token=` email; the person enters a new card/bank on Stripe's hosted **`mode:'setup'`** Checkout (no charge), and a NEW `router/webhooks.ts` branch (`checkout.session.completed` + `session.mode==='setup'` + `metadata.payment_kind==='card_update'`) reads the SetupIntent's PM and saves it as the engagement's default (`default_payment_method_id` + customer `invoice_settings.default_payment_method` + specialist subscription PM). KEY: **each engagement has its OWN Stripe customer** (`contract-stripe-customer.ts` creates one per row; for member-paid MAP 1/Tax it's the MEMBER's customer), so a card-update is **per-engagement** — `card_update_tokens` is keyed to the PERSON, the `/update-card` page lists every reusable engagement they pay, and each "Update" mints a separate setup session (metadata `pipeline`+`row_id`) for that engagement's customer. `payments_loadcardupdate`/`_cardupdate_checkout` are in PUBLIC_HANDLERS (bypass auth by design, like the `/pay` token pages); `_cardupdate_checkout` re-enumerates the token's person + verifies the requested `row_id` belongs to them. Stripe shows a "Bank via Link" option even on a card-typed setup session (account-level Link) — the card/bank pre-pick on our page is cosmetic; the webhook saves whichever PM the person actually enters.

**131. Per-installment method/last4 freeze on a MAP 1 card-update — the Payments display is per-installment, not row-uniform (2026-06-16; supersedes #128).** `pipeline_map1` stores ONE `payment_method_type`/`acct_last4`/`card_processing_fee` (the CURRENT method), but after a card-update different installments may have been charged on different methods. So the card-update webhook, BEFORE switching, **freezes** every already-paid/processing installment's method + last4 into `pay{N}_method`/`pay{N}_last4` (capturing what they were actually charged with), then sets the new method + recomputes `card_processing_fee`. `normalize.ts buildMap1Rows` derives each row's fee/method/last4 from `pay{N}_method`/`pay{N}_last4` if frozen (paid), else the row's current method (scheduled / never-switched) — via new `cardFeeFor(base)` (the exact `(base+0.30)/(1-0.029)` charge formula). Result: a past ACH installment keeps showing no fee + its bank ••last4, a past card keeps its fee + its card ••last4, scheduled installments project the new method. `card_processing_fee` is still recomputed for the per-installment RECEIPT (point-in-time). Frontend `PaymentsTable.jsx` group-overview row reads the LATEST installment's method (current), not installment 1's. Rows pre-dating the columns have null `pay{N}_method`/`_last4` → fall back to current (backfill by hand if a historical mismatch matters).

**132. MAP 1 installments 2-4 now get the same two-stage emails as payment 1 — confirmation at charge + receipt on clear (2026-06-16). (PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED 2026-07-15: part (b) — the sweep's per-installment confirmation chain — was removed by user decision (double emails on automatic charges confused clients); installments 2-4 AND the tax implementation charge now send the receipt-on-clear as the ONLY client email. Parts (a)/(c)/(d) — the `payment_number` param, the immediate `pay{N}_status` stamp, and the receipt rewording — remain live.)** The confirmation email (`contract-confirmation-email.ts`) was payment-1-only (hardcoded "Payment 1 of 4", once-only `confirmation_status` guard, PF "first payment" notification); installments 2-4 got only a receipt that fired on `payment_intent.succeeded` (post-clear for ACH) but contradictorily carried the "allow 2-4 business days to clear" `[PROCESSING_TIME]`. Fix: (a) `contract-confirmation-email.ts` accepts `payment_number` (defaults 1) — guard/notification/`confirmation_status` stay payment-1-only; (b) `contract-chargescheduled-sweep.ts` chains the confirmation per installment after a successful charge (HTTP fetch, service-role); (c) the sweep stamps `pay{N}_status` (card→succeeded, ACH→processing) immediately so the tab shows the real state (also makes a same-day cron re-run skip the installment — it's in skipStatuses); (d) `contract-invoice-receipt.ts` reworded the subsequent-receipt ACH `[PROCESSING_TIME]` to "Your payment has now cleared." Audit: Advisor/Accountant (one-time) + Tax (retainer & implementation each get their own confirmation) clean; **Specialist monthly license months 2+ get no confirmation but its receipt is clean — left as-is** (a monthly "processing" email would be noise).

**133. Email subject headers must be RFC2047-encoded for non-ASCII (2026-06-16).** A subject with an em-dash ("—") arrived as mojibake ("â€"") because email HEADERS are ASCII-only (the HTML BODY was fine — it's declared UTF-8). `utils/gmail-draft.ts` now wraps non-ASCII subjects as `=?UTF-8?B?<base64>?=` (ASCII subjects pass through byte-identical), fixing it for every `draftGmail` caller. Other inline senders that build `Subject:` raw (e.g. `contract-payment-email.ts`) share the latent issue — keep DB-template subjects ASCII (hyphen not em-dash) unless the sender encodes.

**134. JSX must live in a `.jsx` file — the rollup production build (`vite build` / `npm run deploy`) rejects JSX in a `.js` file even though the esbuild dev server (`npm run dev`) accepts it (2026-06-17).** A shared atoms file `growth/ui.js` (it exports JSX components) passed dev/HMR but broke `npx vite build` ("Failed to parse source for import analysis… name the file with the .jsx or .tsx extension"); renamed to `ui.jsx` (extension-less `import './ui'` still resolves). Renaming a file mid-session ALSO strands the running dev server on the old module graph ("Failed to load url …/ui.js — Does the file exist?") → **restart `npm run dev` after any rename**. Lesson: run `npx vite build` (not just rely on HMR) before declaring a frontend phase done — the dev server won't catch this class of error. **AMENDED 2026-08-14: the rule is about the FILE, not about whether the file looks like a component.** `shareLegState.jsx` is mostly plain predicate functions (`legState`, `noteColor`) with three small render helpers at the bottom, and the instinct on a file like that is to call it `shareLegState.js` because "it isn't a component file" — `@vitejs/plugin-react` transforms `.jsx`/`.tsx` only, so it fails the build exactly like a components file would. **If a module contains a single angle bracket of JSX it is a `.jsx` file**, which is why the existing `taxShared.jsx` / `holisticShared.jsx` / `pipShared.jsx` helpers all carry the extension despite exporting mostly data-shaping functions.

**135. Install a new pg_cron sweep WITHOUT hand-handling the service-role key by CLONING an existing sweep's command (2026-06-17).** A cron command embeds the service-role key in its `net.http_post` headers (the `supabase/cron/*.sql` files carry a `<REPLACE_WITH_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY>` placeholder for a from-scratch install). To add `growth-overdue-sweep-daily` without ever reading/printing the key: `SELECT cron.schedule('growth-overdue-sweep-daily','0 10 * * *', replace((SELECT command FROM cron.job WHERE jobname='advisor-sweep-daily' LIMIT 1),'automation_ADVISOR_sweep','automation_GROWTH_overdue_sweep'))` — clones an existing cron's command (key + URL intact) and swaps only the action name. Verify with `position('automation_GROWTH_overdue_sweep' in command)>0 AND position('Bearer ' in command)>0` (no key printed). Fire on demand to test: `DO $$ DECLARE cmd text; BEGIN SELECT command INTO cmd FROM cron.job WHERE jobname='growth-overdue-sweep-daily'; EXECUTE cmd; END $$;` then `SELECT status_code, content FROM net._http_response ORDER BY created DESC LIMIT 1`. 11 sweeps now run (02:00→10:00 UTC); growth is jobid 12 at 10:00.

**136. Growth Plan re-score COPIES prior actions verbatim into the new version — do NOT revert it to reseeding the 20 defaults (2026-06-17).** `growth_plan_save_score` archives the current plan and inserts a new `is_current` score; on a RE-SCORE (a prior current plan exists) it copies EVERY prior `growth_plan_actions` row into the new score_id (preserving G2–G5 edits, accountability progress, custom priorities, sub-tasks) and remaps each sub-task's `parent_action_id` to the new ids via the stable `action_number` (insert copies with parent null → `.select('id, action_number')` → second-pass UPDATE). A first-time score (no prior) still seeds the 20 defaults from `body.actions`. If you touch save-score, KEEP the copy-wholesale + remap or re-scoring silently wipes the advisor's plan + custom items. `accountability_mode` also carries forward from the prior score.

**137. The Growth Plan assigned-admin picker uses a NEW admin-only `growth_plan_load_admins`, NOT `load_admins` (2026-06-17).** `load_admins` (admin management) is gated superadmin-only IN-HANDLER (`auth.session.email !== SUPERADMIN_EMAIL → 403`) and only called by `AdminEditor.jsx`, so a non-superadmin admin building an advisor's Growth Plan would get an empty picker. `growth_plan_load_admins` (`actions/growth/load-admins.ts`, in `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`) returns `{name,email}` from `allowed_admins` (12 rows, all role='admin') for ANY admin; the chosen `email` is stored on `growth_plan_scores.assigned_admin_email` and is the accountability-notification recipient. Members never call it (they don't score).

**138. Adding an off-plan Growth action to the One Page Plan must set `g2_status='potential'` alongside `g3_status='one_page_plan'` — setting only g3 leaves a parked item half-parked (2026-06-18).** The One Page Plan filters On-Plan by `g3_status==='one_page_plan'` but Parked by `g2_status==='park' || g3_status==='park'`. The original member "Add a Priority" set only `g3='one_page_plan'`, so a PARKED item (`g2='park'`) added to the plan still matched the parked filter → it would show in BOTH the plan and the Parking Garage. The shared `GrowthAddPriority` (member tabs + admin One Page Plan bottom switcher) Add-to-Plan now writes `{ g3_status:'one_page_plan', g2_status:'potential', g3_action_type:'new', owned_by, assisted_by, value_level, effort_level }`. The two off-plan-bucket moves: **Park** → `{ g2_status:'park', g3_status:'park' }`; **Drop** → `{ g2_status:'drop', g3_status:'' }` (lands the item in the Dropped Priorities tab). All three are plain `growth_plan_save_actions` writes — no backend change (`save_actions` already whitelists these columns).

**139. `msm_add_client` client-ref suffix is `MAX(existing)+1`, not `count+1` (2026-06-18).** count-based numbering re-collides with a surviving higher-numbered client after a non-last client is deleted; now takes `MAX(suffix)+1` on the regular + PFT paths.

**140. Member bulk-import requires PAIRED `members` + `member_plugin_settings` rows (2026-06-18).** `load_data` builds the admin roster from `member_plugin_settings` merged with `members` by number, so a `members`-only insert is invisible; import via paired inserts, preserve OLD numbers verbatim, wire member-to-member links in a 2nd pass, MD5-verify vs the source CSV. **(The 2026-06-18 import wired that 2nd pass into `connected_member_number`; since 2026-07-31 that column is corporate-parent-only — a new import's 2nd pass belongs in `member_connections` pairs and/or `introduced_by_member_number`, per #312.)**

**141. Deny-all RLS is the ONLY thing protecting the DB — create EVERY new public table with RLS enabled + a `"Deny all access" USING(false)` policy in the SAME migration, then verify with an anon-key REST probe (2026-06-18).** The anon key is public (shipped in the frontend bundle) and `anon`/`authenticated` hold full grants on every table, so a table left RLS-off — OR carrying a `{public} USING(true)` policy — is fully read/write-able by anyone at `…/rest/v1/<table>`. The C1 audit found 8 such tables (`card_update_tokens`/`document_numbers`/`member_number_baselines`/`pft_engagement` RLS-off; `pipeline_map1`/`email_templates`/`pipelines`/`pipeline_sandbox_config` had a `USING(true)` ALL policy named "Allow all for authenticated"). Probe: `curl --head -H "apikey:<anon>" -H "Authorization:Bearer <anon>" -H "Prefer:count=exact" "<base>/rest/v1/<table>?select=*"` → `Content-Range: */0` = locked.

**142. Service-role bypasses RLS → authorization is 100% app-layer; member-facing AUTH handlers MUST re-check ownership against `auth.callerMemberNumber`, NEVER a body id (2026-06-18).** The edge fn runs as service-role, so RLS does NOT protect a handler that reads/writes by a body-supplied `client_id`/`ciq_id`/`enrollment_id` — C2 found ~20 such IDOR handlers. Guard pattern: `utils/client-ownership.ts` `denyIfNotOwnClient`/`denyIfNotOwnEnrollment` + `actions/ciq/shared.ts denyIfNotOwnCiq` (each returns a 403 Response or null). Role-gates are deny-by-OMISSION (an action absent from `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` is member-callable) and matched by EXACT name — a stale/typo'd gate entry (e.g. `save_client_note` vs the real `add_client_note`) silently leaves a handler ungated.

**143. Function EXECUTE is granted to PUBLIC by default — to revoke, `REVOKE EXECUTE … FROM public` (revoking from anon/authenticated alone is NOT enough) (2026-06-18).** M7b: the Supabase "anon can execute SECURITY DEFINER function" advisor warning only cleared after revoking from `public`. service_role keeps its own explicit grant, and a trigger fires regardless of EXECUTE grants, so it's safe.

**144. Frontend `callApi` (`api.js`) hard-redirects to `/vfo-portal/` on ANY 401 EXCEPT login actions (the `LOGIN_ACTIONS` allowlist), which now throw the error inline (2026-06-18).** Without the carve-out a failed login (401 "Invalid credentials") bounced off the login page with no message — and hid the H1 429. Add any new login-type action to `LOGIN_ACTIONS`; genuine session-expiry 401s on non-login actions still clear the session + redirect.

**145. Production deploys need an EXPLICIT "deploy" — the auto-mode classifier BLOCKS `supabase functions deploy` / `npm run deploy` on ambiguous approvals ("continue" / "yes" / "whatever you recommend") (2026-06-18).** Wait for the literal word before deploying.

**146. The Bash tool's cwd PERSISTS across calls — a `cd <react worktree>` for `npm run build` then a backend `supabase functions deploy` failed with 400 "Entrypoint path does not exist" (it looked for `supabase/functions/…` under the react worktree) (2026-06-18).** Always `cd` to the correct repo's worktree (or use absolute paths) immediately before a deploy.

**147. Hiding rows from a SHARED data source — filter in the rendering COMPONENT, not only the loader. Feature C hides Lost/Removed specialists from showrooms, but `load_data` returns ALL experts to admins (the admin Search-Specialists list needs the inactive ones), and the admin's showroom PREVIEW reuses the same `MemberShowroom` component as the member/client portals — so a loader-only filter still showed inactive specialists in the admin preview (2026-06-19).** Put the `status === 'Active'` filter in `MemberShowroom` itself (it covers all three showroom surfaces); keep defense-in-depth filters in `load_data`/`client_showroom_load` for the non-admin payloads.

**148. The public website widget (`vfo-widget.js`, embedded on external sites via `data-vfo-key=<manage_key>`) reads the `experts` table DIRECTLY through the anon key + the `experts` `"Public read"` RLS policy — there is NO backend handler serving it (2026-06-19).** To hide rows from the widget you change the RLS policy (`USING(status='Active')`), not a handler. The edge function uses service-role and bypasses RLS, so admin surfaces are unaffected.

**149. `save_specialist` (and any `update({...form})` passthrough) sends form fields verbatim — an empty date `<input>` is `''`, which Postgres rejects with "invalid input syntax for type date" (2026-06-19).** Convert empty date strings to `null` in the frontend before saving (`form.join_date || null`), and clear `leave_date` to `null` when status flips back to Active.

**150. Worktree `npm run dev` resolves `node_modules` from the MAIN checkout (Node walks up the tree; git worktrees have no `node_modules` of their own) — a dependency a prior session added to `package.json` but never installed in the main checkout (e.g. `@sentry/react`) breaks the worktree dev server with "Failed to resolve import" (2026-06-19).** Fix: `npm install <pkg> --no-save` in `C:\vfo-react` (the shared store, so no tracked file changes); restart the dev server.

**151. In a Vite `index.html`, a `public/` asset referenced as `%BASE_URL%<file>` gets DOUBLE-based — `%BASE_URL%` expands to the base (`/vfo-portal/`) AND Vite separately re-prepends the base to root-relative attribute values, yielding `/vfo-portal/vfo-portal/<file>` which 404s; the browser then silently falls back to the cached/old favicon, so it LOOKS like a cache problem (2026-06-19).** Use a plain root-relative href: `<link rel="icon" href="/vfo-icon.svg">` → Vite prepends the base exactly once → `/vfo-portal/vfo-icon.svg`. Verify both the dev server (`curl -s localhost:5173/vfo-portal/ | grep 'rel="icon"'`) and the built `dist/index.html` emit the SINGLE-base path. (Favicon swaps also need a hard refresh / new tab — tab-icon cache is the stickiest browser cache.)

**152. Migration "reflect-paid" pattern — to make an already-paid installment/retainer INERT to BOTH the charge sweep AND the revshare sweep, replicate the engine's real no-share-due completed state (2026-06-23).** Set `pay{N}_status='succeeded'` (or tax `retainer_status='succeeded'`) + the receipt number (`rec{N}_number` / `retainer_receipt_number`) + `rec{N}_rev_share='Completed - Revenue Share'|'Completed - Money Mapping'` + `rec{N}_rev_paid='N/A — No Share Due'`. The charge sweep skips on `pay{N}_status` being in its skip-set; the revshare handler/sweep treat a row as resolved ONLY when `rev_share != 'Pending'` AND `rev_paid ∈ {Yes, Money Mapping, N/A — No Share Due}`. Setting the receipt number WITHOUT the resolved rev fields makes the revshare sweep FIRE for it (→ Tracy ping). The global Payments page derives payouts from `rev_paid='Yes'`, so `'N/A — No Share Due'` = no phantom payout. Used by `actions/migration/backfill-{map1,tax}.ts`.

**153. Activating a dormant migrated row reuses the Phase D `card_update` webhook UNCHANGED — it routes by `metadata.row_id`, not a customer lookup (2026-06-23).** `router/webhooks.ts` (`checkout.session.completed` + `mode=setup` + `metadata.payment_kind='card_update'`) reads `metadata.pipeline` ('MAP 1'|'TAX'|'SPECIALIST_LICENSE') + `metadata.row_id` and writes `default_payment_method_id` + `payment_method_type` + `acct_last4` (+ recomputes MAP 1 `card_processing_fee`). So a NEW setup session stamped with those same metadata values activates a dormant migrated row with ZERO webhook changes (`actions/migration/connect-checkout.ts`). BUT that webhook does NOT set `stripe_customer_id` — `migration_send_setup_link` must create the customer + stamp it FIRST, else the chargescheduled sweep (filters `stripe_customer_id is not null` AND `payment_method_type in (card,ach)`) keeps skipping the row.

**154. `automation_TAX_charge_implementation` uses an UNDERSCORE and is a PUBLIC handler (2026-06-23).** The dispatch key is `automation_TAX_charge_implementation` (NOT `...charge-implementation`); it lives in PUBLIC_HANDLERS (chained by `implement-final-decision`). The hyphenated name 401s — it isn't a registered public action, so it falls through the auth gate ("Unauthorized — please log in again"). To fire it directly (testing), POST the underscore name with the anon key — no service-role needed. By contrast the `*_sweep` handlers ARE service-role-self-checked, so fire those by EXECUTE-ing their cron command (`do $$ declare c text; begin select command into c from cron.job where jobid=N; execute c; end $$;`) which keeps the key inside Postgres.

**155. Adding a 3rd `member_category` requires widening a CHECK on `members`, AND its numbering baseline CANNOT live in `member_number_baselines` (2026-06-29).** `members.member_category` carries `CHECK (member_category = ANY (ARRAY['advisor','accountant']) OR member_category IS NULL)` — inserting `'strategic_member'` 500s ("violates check constraint `members_member_category_check`") until you `ALTER TABLE public.members DROP CONSTRAINT members_member_category_check` then re-add it including the new value. Separately, `member_number_baselines` has CHECKs pinning `member_category` to advisor/accountant AND `advisor_model` to Legacy/New Model (both NOT NULL) — so a model-less category (Strategic Members) can get NO baseline row there; its first number lives as a code constant (`STRATEGIC_BASELINE = 20000`) in `utils/member-number.ts`, which also branches to scan members by category-only (no `advisor_model` filter) for that bucket. (A CHECK widening doesn't affect RLS, but still re-run `get_advisors` security → GREEN after any DB change.)

**156. The shared member detail-view (`MemberDirectoryView` in `MembersPanel.jsx`) is parameterised by prop, NOT forked per category — keep every new prop DEFAULTED to the existing behavior (2026-06-29).** Strategic Members reuse the same component as advisors/accountants; the differences are all props that default to today's behavior so the advisor/accountant views stay byte-identical: `showModel` (hide the Legacy/New filter + list column), `msmOptions` (limit the MSM dropdown), `extraTabs` (limit the Specialists/Showroom/Website/CIQ/GC button row), `msmBypassEnableGate` (skip the MSM program enable-gate — strategic members have no MSM Home elsewhere to toggle from), `msmAllowedPrograms` (limit the MSM-Home program toggles + drop the Advanced/PFT meeting counters), `typeOptionsOverride` (DB-group Member-Type list for Edit Profile). Same shape in `MemberKpiPanel` (`category` prop, dynamic per-group families for strategic) and `MSMTracking` (`bypassEnableGate` / `allowedProgramKeys`).

**157. A Gmail MIME draft built as a `string[]` must NOT be `.filter(Boolean)`-ed over the whole array — the empty-string lines are the part separators, and dropping them collapses the body into the headers → a BLANK email (2026-06-29).** The multipart MIME format delimits a part's headers from its content with ONE blank line; `["...Content-Type: text/html","",emailBody,...]`. A blanket `.filter(Boolean)` strips those `""`s, so Gmail renders an empty body (the invoice/receipt draft came through blank). Fix: filter ONLY the optional header lines (`Cc`/`Bcc` may be empty), then build the body/attachment lines in a SEPARATE array that is never filtered. (MAP 1's `contract-invoice-receipt.ts` does it right — `headerPart.filter(Boolean)` then `mimeLines = [...headerPart, "", ...]`.)

**158. `utils/doc-numbers.ts allocateDocNumber()` only works for a table that owns one of `document_numbers`' FK columns (client_id / advisor_onboarding_id / accountant_onboarding_id / specialist_onboarding_id) — its CHECK requires exactly one (2026-06-29).** A new table with no slot there (e.g. `specialist_revenue_requests`) can't call it. When there is exactly ONE document per row (one payment per specialist request), skip the shared counter entirely and derive a guaranteed-unique number from the row id: `INV-SPEC-<id>` / `REC-SPEC-<id>` (id is unique, so no collision, no `document_numbers` row needed). Only reach for `allocateDocNumber` when numbers must be a contiguous per-owner/global sequence.

**159. A Stripe Connect Express account that was CREATED but never finished onboarding has `capabilities.transfers !== 'active'`, and Stripe REJECTS a transfer to it — check the capability BEFORE transferring, else you get a "failed" instead of a clean "awaiting setup" (2026-06-29).** "Set Up Payment Details" creates the account + an onboarding link but does NOT activate it; until the person completes Stripe's hosted KYC, transfers bounce. The payout engine (`utils/specialist-revenue-payout.ts`) now `GET /v1/accounts/<id>` and treats `transfers !== 'active'` exactly like "no account": mint a fresh onboarding link, draft the setup email, mark the line **awaiting_connect** (and clear any prior failed-transfer Jake alert) rather than attempting a doomed transfer. Also: this feature reuses the shared `pipeline_sandbox_config` **"MAP 1"** row for ALL its Stripe ops (account creation, transfers, checkout) — same precedent as PIP — so a Connect account and the transfer to it are ALWAYS the same mode (a sandbox account + live transfer, or vice-versa, would fail).

**160. A revenue-share recipient's email must show only THAT recipient's gross (their member share + VFO's share on that one deal), never the request total — recipients must not see money tied to other members (2026-06-29).** The VFO Specialist Revenue payout passes a per-line `lineGross = member_share + vfos_share` into the `[Gross]` placeholder of the confirmation / money-mapping emails, NOT `request.gross_amount` (which sums every recipient). The Accounting/Automation panels DO show the full request total (admin-only surface) — the privacy rule is specifically about the email to the member.

**161. Stripe fires BOTH `invoice.paid` AND `invoice.payment_succeeded` for one payment (and retries on timeout) — a non-atomic read-then-write dedup guard races and double-fires the downstream chain (2026-06-30).** The specialist license `invoice.paid` webhook block read `lic_last_invoice_id`, and only wrote it near the end; when Stripe delivered both sibling events near-simultaneously, both passed the guard and both chained `automation_SPECIALIST_licinvoicereceipt` → TWO invoice/receipt emails (that handler delegates idempotency entirely to the webhook). Fix = an ATOMIC claim: `UPDATE … SET lic_last_invoice_id = <id> WHERE id = <row> AND (lic_last_invoice_id IS NULL OR lic_last_invoice_id <> <id>)` (supabase-js: `.or('lic_last_invoice_id.is.null,lic_last_invoice_id.neq.<id>')` — `.neq` alone excludes the NULL first-invoice, gotcha #28) `.select('id')`; only the event whose claim returns a row proceeds. Mode-agnostic, and also immune to Stripe's at-least-once retries. Any webhook that chains a non-idempotent side effect must claim-before-chain, not read-then-write.

**162. An ecosystem name lives in FIVE places — the DB rows AND four hardcoded lists — so renaming/adding one means touching all of them, and the out-of-repo website widget is the easy miss (2026-06-30).** Ecosystem tags are free-text rows in `vfo_ecosystem_assignments(expert_id, name)`; the showroom/admin UI hardcodes the same strings in THREE frontend arrays (`MemberShowroom.jsx` FILTER_CATS, `SpecialistsPanel.jsx` ECOSYSTEMS, `SpecialistKpiPanel.jsx` ECOSYSTEMS) AND the public website widget (`vfo-widget.js`, in the `vfo-widget` storage bucket; **source now tracked at `widget/vfo-widget.js` in the `vfo-edge-functions` repo** as of 2026-07-09 — deployed by uploading to the bucket, not a build) hardcodes it twice (`cats` + `categories`). The widget reads each specialist's category NAMES live from the DB but filters by its hardcoded button labels, so renaming the DB rows without re-uploading the widget makes its old-named filter buttons match nothing (0 results). Editing the widget in place keeps the same bucket URL → every member's existing embed keeps working (CDN cache aside; no `?v=` bump needed). Also: the in-portal showroom is ONE shared component (`MemberShowroom`) reused across 5 surfaces, gated per-surface by a `showMemberServices` prop (admin Specialists showroom + admin Members-tab preview + member portal show it; specialist portal, client portal, and the widget do not) — internal-only categories are hidden by filtering the experts in the component, not the loader (gotcha #147).

**163. The member revenue-share CONFIRMATION emails are hardcoded in the revshare handlers, NOT managed rows in `email_templates` — only the SPECIALIST-revenue pipeline's rev-share emails live in the tab (2026-07-01).** `actions/pipeline/contract-revshare.ts` (MAP 1 payment-1 + payment-N) and `actions/tax/revshare.ts` (retainer + implementation) build the "Revenue share confirmation" body inline as a template literal — there is NO `email_templates` row, so the Email Templates tab cannot edit them. (`SPECREV_revenue_share_confirmation` / `SPECREV_money_mapping_notice` etc. ARE managed rows, but that's the separate `VFO_SPECIALIST_REVENUE` pipeline.) To add a variant (this session's strategic-partner email), branch inline / add a helper (`utils/strategic-partner-email.ts`) — don't look for a template row. A member rev-share email goes to the connected member; the strategic-partner share email is a SEPARATE email to the partner company's `strategic_member_groups.contact_email` (fired from the same handler + both sweeps on a successful strategic transfer).
 **[SUPERSEDED 2026-07-04: these emails are now managed template rows — see gotcha #181 (migration email_templates_hardcoded_ports).]**

**164. The Tracy Revenue-Master cross-check was REMOVED from both revshare engines (2026-07-01) — MAP 1 now pays the instant money clears, but TAX revshare still waits on the Tax 4 client-decision gate.** `contract-revshare.ts` and `tax/revshare.ts` no longer read Tracy's sheet (no client-ref-in-col-A lookup, no batch-tab match, no `K+L+M+N+O=J` reconciliation, no "pending — Tracy's numbers not yet verified" bailout); the share pays as soon as the payment clears, amounts taken straight from the PF input form on the row (`member_share`/`vfos_share`/`strategic_partner_share`). The Google OAuth token fetch stayed (the member email still needs it); the member-contribution figure now comes from the row's `member_contribution`, not the sheet. This only changes MAP 1's timing (webhook → immediate payout). The **tax retainer** revshare is still triggered by the **Tax 4 "Client decision 1"** step (`post_review_decision='Continue - Revenue Share'` + client Confirmed / 24h Auto-Locked) and the **implementation** by the Tax 5 Proceed decision — those are business gates, not the Tracy check, and were intentionally left. So a tax retainer that has cleared but has null `post_review_decision` has NOT paid revshare yet — correct, not a bug. The daily sweeps are now purely a **failed-transfer** retry net (they will also auto-complete any legacy `Pending` rows on the next pass).

**165. The Accounting revenue/reconciliation loaders EXCLUDE sandbox rows, so a sandbox test never appears in Accounting until you flip the row's `sandbox` flag to false (2026-07-01).** `holistic/load.ts`, `holistic/tax-load.ts` (and the PIP/specialist loaders) filter `.or("sandbox.is.null,sandbox.eq.false")`. To preview a sandbox test in Accounting → VFO Services, temporarily `UPDATE pipeline_map1|client_tax_plans SET sandbox=false WHERE id=<row>`, view, then flip back to `true`. The row-level `sandbox` column is ONLY an accounting-display filter — it does NOT choose the Stripe key (that's the `pipeline_sandbox_config` toggle), so flipping it does not affect payouts.

**166. The member Payments/revenue tab derives its Stripe-transfer list LIVE from the member's attached Connect account — a reused test account shows every sandbox transfer, and clearing `stripe_account_id` empties the view on reload (2026-07-01).** Reusing ONE sandbox Connect account across multiple members/partners makes that account accumulate all sandbox transfers, so the tab shows a big cross-test list. Detaching it (`members.stripe_account_id = null`) leaves the tab with no account to query → blank on reload. Not a bug — the list is a live read of the attached account, not stored per-payment.

**167. The three "other" admin tabs (Accounting / Automation / Member Overview) are NO LONGER superadmin-only — they are per-admin tab-gated via `allowed_admins.allowed_tabs` (2026-07-01).** Superadmin (Jake) still bypasses everything. For any other admin, `middleware/auth.ts` gates the tab's backing actions through `TAB_ACTIONS` in `constants/role-gates.ts` (`tabForAction(action)` → `'accounting'|'automation'|'member_overview'`): the call is allowed ONLY if the caller's `allowed_tabs` array includes that key. This is REAL server-side authorization, not just nav visibility — a non-granted admin gets 403 on the loaders even via a deep link. The residual `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (the per-client/per-person payment tools: `migration_*`, `specialist_license_continuation_*`, `payments_send_card_update`) stay Jake-only and are NOT grantable. Jake sets grants in **Admin Editor** (`admin_update_tabs`, superadmin-gated in-handler like `load_admins`).
