An off-session ACH charge returns `'processing'` at creation and can BOUNCE days later; that late failed-event was being dropped, stranding the installment in `'processing'` forever (money silently uncollected, no alert, no client re-pay email). v612 adds two additive `payment_intent.payment_failed` branches — MAP1 installment (`metadata.payment_number` 2-4, row by `stripe_customer_id`) and TAX implementation (`metadata.payment_kind='implementation'`) — each keyed on a `'processing'` status guard (`pay{N}_status==='processing'` / `implementation_charge_status==='processing'`) so a card SYNC-decline that the sweep already handled is NOT double-alerted. Any NEW off-session ACH charge path needs its own late-failure consumer on `payment_intent.payment_failed` — creating the PI is not enough.

**230. The Payment Continuation backfills hard-guard collisions (409 + force), but RAW SQL BYPASSES EVERYTHING (2026-07-16).** `migration_backfill_map1`/`migration_backfill_tax` now return HTTP 409 `{error, conflict:true, existing_row}` when asked to update an existing row NOT signed `legacy_source='old-system'`, unless `body.force===true` (preview never 409s; it returns `existing_row` + `requires_force`). Hand/visual migration done via direct DB writes gets NONE of that protection, so it must follow the rulebook at `C:\Users\jakel_fjetgbx\visual-migration-rules.md`: payment-bearing columns belong EXCLUSIVELY to the tool (`'old-system'` rows); visual progress writes touch only `client_progress` / `client_tax_progress` / `client_tax_specialists` / `priority_progress`; decision columns are INERT without their `*_email_sent_at` twin (the timestamps arm the 24h/48h/96h sweep timers — a decision written WITHOUT its email stamp fires nothing, which is the safe hand-migration state), and `presentation_send_date` + a bare `tax4_meeting_date` are email booby-traps (they draft a client email / fire a daily Tim nudge once due). Also from this session: `migration_setup_tokens` are now SINGLE-USE — the `card_update` webhook stamps `used_at=now()` (keyed on `session.metadata.token`, present only on migration connect-card sessions, so Phase D admin card-update sessions are unaffected), `migration_connect_checkout` returns 410 on a used token, and `migration_connect_load` reports `done=true` when used.

**231. "VFO Accredited Professional Specialist" (`experts.vfo_accredited`) is hidden from EVERY showroom + the widget, but shown in Specialist Search + the Holistic/Tax pickers — the hide lives at the render choke point, NOT in `load_data` (2026-07-16).** `load_data.experts` feeds BOTH the admin/member showrooms AND the Holistic-Regular / Holistic-Tax / standalone-Tax specialist pickers, so you must NOT strip accredited specialists out of `load_data` (that breaks the pickers, where they must remain selectable). The showroom hide is a single unconditional filter in the shared `MemberShowroom.jsx` (`.filter(ex => !ex.vfo_accredited)`) — inherited by all 5 portal surfaces (member/client/specialist portals + admin Showroom tab + admin per-member preview) — MIRRORED by a filter in the anon widget (`widget/vfo-widget.js`, next to the existing `Member Services` exclusion). Adding the column required extending the anon column grant on `experts` from 7 → 8 columns (`grant select (vfo_accredited) to anon`) so the widget can filter by it; keep this list to NON-SENSITIVE columns only (companion to #201 — `ecosystem_content`, `email`, `D&B_*`, `revenue_decision`, etc. stay OFF the anon grant). `save_specialist`/`load_data` are generic column pass-throughs so no handler changed. **Consequence:** the widget hide is a DISPLAY filter, not a security boundary — `name/short_bio/long_bio/headshot_image` are already anon-readable, so a raw anon REST call can still read an accredited specialist's row. The admin-only blue tag renders ONLY in Specialist Search + the admin profile header (showrooms/widget drop the person entirely, never render the tag). A NEW showroom surface must route through `MemberShowroom` (or replicate the filter); a NEW anon-facing specialist reader must replicate BOTH the `Member Services` and `vfo_accredited` exclusions. **(2026-08-09: exactly such a reader now exists — `public/store/data.js`, the `/store/` 3D bookstore — and it does replicate all three exclusions. See **#345**.)**

**232. `experts.ecosystem_content` is an ordered ARRAY (multiple entries per ecosystem allowed); the flat `short_bio` is the COMBINED bios of all entries (2026-07-16).** A specialist can have MORE THAN ONE content entry in the SAME ecosystem (e.g. three Tax Planning write-ups). Content is therefore stored as an ordered array — each item carries its own `ecosystem` plus the entry's `short_bio` + non-tax `D&B_*`: `ecosystem_content = [ { ecosystem, short_bio, "D&B_..." }, ... ]`. This SUPERSEDES the old object-keyed shape from #220 (`{ "<eco>": {content} }`). `SpecialistsPanel.jsx`'s `entriesFor(expert, ecosNames)` reads ALL THREE shapes — new array; legacy name-keyed object (→ one entry per assigned ecosystem); NULL (oldest rows → flat columns as the first ecosystem's content) — so old rows open without loss; `save_specialist` always writes the array. Entry **0 is primary** — its `D&B_*` mirror the flat columns (agreements/BoldSign read those). `vfo_ecosystem_assignments` still gets the DISTINCT ecosystem set (showroom categories/filtering + the anon widget categories are unchanged). **The flat `short_bio`** (read by the showroom card/modal + widget + admin Search list) is now computed on save as the DISTINCT non-empty entry short bios joined `\| ` — so a multi-ecosystem specialist shows EVERY specialty on the showroom, not just the primary (fixed the "only one bio shows" bug; existing rows were backfilled via MCP from `ecosystem_content`). This is admin-only (the public showroom/widget never render the per-entry Details & Benefits toggle — they only ever show short_bio + long_bio + ecosystem tags), pure frontend + a data backfill — `save_specialist`/`load_data` are generic column pass-throughs so NO handler changed. A NEW consumer of `ecosystem_content` MUST handle the array shape (and ideally the legacy object/null fallbacks) via `entriesFor`, not assume name-keyed lookup. **UPDATE (later 2026-07-16): the Tax questionnaire is now ALSO per-entry.** The tax keys (`D&B_tax_risk_notes` + the 4 `D&B_audit_risk_*`) moved out of `SHARED_KEYS` into `CONTENT_KEYS`, so each Tax Planning entry carries its own Tax Risk Notes + Audit Risk Questionnaire (rendered inside that entry). `entriesFor` runs `seedTaxFromFlat`, which migrates the legacy single shared tax set (still in the flat `D&B_*` columns) into the FIRST Tax Planning entry that has none; on save the first Tax entry's answers are mirrored BACK to the flat columns so `create-specialist.ts` (SIF go-live writer) + any flat reader stay consistent. **`D&B_tax_risk_mindset` was removed from the portal entirely** (edit form + profile + header badge) and dropped from `TAX_KEYS` — its flat column is neither shown nor written/cleared. In the read-only profile the questionnaire uses the `dbHeading` navy sub-heading style; in the edit form it uses the normal small `labelStyle`. `D&B_tax_risk_notes` is edit-form-only (hidden on the profile).

**233. Training-track (90 Day Plan) completion is POSITIVE-ONLY and lives in one shared module — `components/shared/trainingStatus.js` (2026-07-17).** For `member_training_progress` rows (the `program_training_tasks` / MSM training track, NOT the client track), a task counts toward "N / M complete", greens the phase stepper, and flips a phase to Done ONLY on a POSITIVE status — the rule is a DENYLIST (`NOT_DONE_STATUSES = {Outstanding, Will Watch, Pending, In Progress, Stopped}`) because positives are open-ended and program-specific (`Completed`, `Have Watched`, any `N to Call`/`N to Send`, any `Built - X%`, `Type 1/2/3` all mean done). Two special values: **`N/A`** removes the task from BOTH numerator and denominator (a 4-step phase with one `N/A` becomes `/3`), via `countedTasks` filtering it out; **`Stopped`** on ANY step makes the whole "90 Day Plan:" header read **Stopped** (red), derived live by `isTrackStopped` (clear the step → plan revives). Both the admin track (`components/admin/MSMTracking.jsx`) and the member track (`components/member/MemberMSMTracking.jsx`) import the helpers — edit `trainingStatus.js` ONCE, never re-implement per file; a new not-done option must be ADDED to `NOT_DONE_STATUSES` or it silently counts as done. The admin header "90 Day Plan:" badge (`PlanStatusBadge`) updates LIVE as statuses change: the mounted `TrainingTrack` reports `planStatusLabel(phases, progress)` up to `EnrolledPanel` via `onPlanStatusChange`, and the badge shows that `liveStatus` (falling back to its own fetch until the training tab is opened). This is TRAINING-track only — the client track (`program_client_tasks`, `status_options` = discriminators like `auto`/`date_entry`) has its own vocabulary and does NOT use these helpers.

**234. A Partnership Fast Track accountant auto-links its PFT advisor as `members.connected_member_number` on member creation — the link is reverse-traced, best-effort (2026-07-17).** `actions/accountant/create-member.ts` (`automation_ACCOUNTANT_createmember`, Stage 3) sets `connected_member_number` from `resolveConnectedMemberNumber(onboarding_id)`: `pft_engagement` (the ONLY side holding the link — `accountant_onboarding` has no back-pointer) is reverse-scanned by `accountant_onboarding_id`, then hops `pft_engagement.client_id → clients.member_number` (the PFT "client" row IS the accountant; its `member_number` is the owning advisor). Wrapped in try/catch → a non-PFT accountant, a missing engagement, or any lookup error just yields `null` (member creation is never blocked). NEW accountants only — the idempotent retry path (`ob.member_number` already set) does NOT backfill, and pre-existing accountant members were NOT backfilled (user's call). This is the FIRST automatic writer of `connected_member_number` (previously only the manual admin picker in `add_member_full` / `member_profile_save` wrote it). `connection_type` is left null (the Connected Member card renders for accountants regardless; the type chip is UI-suppressed for accountants anyway). **SUPERSEDED 2026-07-31 by #312 — the reverse-trace itself is unchanged, but the DESTINATION moved.** `automation_ACCOUNTANT_createmember` no longer writes `connected_member_number` on the members insert; it now records the PFT link as a **mutual pair in `member_connections`**, written AFTER the members row exists (both sides are FKs), normalized to `member_a < member_b`, best-effort inside try/catch with a `23505` swallowed — still never blocking accountant creation. `connected_member_number` is now corporate-parent-only and every non-corporate value was cleared. `connection_type` is likewise no longer a "Connected Member card" concern: it is the **introducer's** tier on the separate `introduced_by_member_number` slot, and it is now shown for accountants too.


**235. Membership renewal math: year-1's last pull must NEVER land on/after `renewal_date` — day-15 payers renew at pay+12mo, not the "last 15th strictly before" (2026-07-17).** The sweep's renewal guard treats "any `kind='membership'` row with `due_date >= renewal_date`" as "next year already generated". A monthly payer whose first payment lands ON a 15th gets 12 pulls ending exactly 11 months later on the 15th — under the old rule ("last 15th strictly before first-payment + 12 months") that 12th pull EQUALED `renewal_date`, the guard saw it as year-2, and renewal was permanently blocked: the plan stayed Active with a past renewal date and billing silently stopped after year 1. Fix in `shared.ts renewalFromPayDate`: pay days 1-14 keep the old rule (pay Jul 8 -> renews Jun 15); pay day 15 AND after-the-15th payers renew at the 15th exactly 12 months out (pay Jul 15 -> Jul 15; pay Jul 19 -> Jul 15, unchanged). Invariant for ANY future schedule shape: every year-N row generated at activation must sort strictly BEFORE `renewal_date`, and every renewal-generated row on/after it — that pair is what makes the guard sound. Verified by a deno harness across all pay-day classes (1/8/14/15/16/19/31, year boundary, Feb).

**236. Membership pull-failure side effects (suspend + bell + email) must fire on BOTH the sync and the late-ACH path, via the shared helper (2026-07-17).** The sweep's synchronous failure arm only ever runs for CARD declines — an ACH pull returns `processing` with `res.ok` and takes the SUCCESS branch (`charged++`). The late bounce arrives days later as `payment_intent.payment_failed`, which previously only flipped rows to `declined` (money self-healed via next month's arrears bundling, but NO suspend, NO member email, NO admin bell — a pure-ACH member could fail every month forever, silently, while the sweep log said "charged"). As of v617 the webhook's membership PI block mirrors the sweep arm for rows it ACTUALLY flips (`.update(...).select()`, gated on `payment_kind='membership_pull'` — termination fees and in-checkout first-payment retries are excluded; the status filter also prevents double-firing on a card decline the sweep already marked `missed`). The member email lives in `utils/membership-payment-failure.ts` (`draftMembershipPaymentFailedEmail(supabase, plan, rows, totalOwed)`) — shared by sweep + webhook, `reminder_sent_at`-guarded, null-guards `setup_token` (mirrors `send-reminder`). Template tokens: `[Failed Amount]` = the failed charge's total (may span months), `[Amount]` = the regular per-pull ("your usual") — they are DIFFERENT tokens in `MEMBERSHIP_payment_failed` (id 192); don't collapse them. Also v617: the sweep's Stripe Idempotency-Key is LOGICAL/date-less (`membership-pull-<plan>-<sorted row ids>`, the #228 class), the post-charge ledger write failure alerts Jake ("charge SUCCEEDED but write FAILED"), and every membership-activation DB failure (`activate.ts` guard/insert/update/method-refresh) + a not-captured payment method alert too — all reusing rule key `MEMBERSHIP_charge_failed` (v612 precedent: reuse the sync path's key), dismissible (an action-required bell would be unclearable, #179).

**237. Membership sandbox test recipe: Financial Connections "Invalid Payment Accounts" fails at LINK time — simulate a late ACH bounce with the Test Institution's insufficient-funds account (••2227), and fire the sweep by EXECUTE-ing cron job 16's command (2026-07-17).** The `/membership-pay` ACH checkout requires instant verification (`verification_method=instant` in `setup-checkout.ts`), so Stripe's manual test account numbers (routing 110000000) are NOT enterable — only FC test institutions. "Invalid Payment Accounts" errors at the Link step ("account couldn't be accessed") and never saves a method; the green "Test Institution (Non-OAuth)" surfaces behavior-mapped accounts — pick the insufficient-funds one (last-4 2227): it links fine, saves as the default method, and every debit enters `processing` then fails async within ~3 minutes (the exact late-ACH signature #236 handles). To trigger a pull on demand: backdate a `scheduled` row's `due_date` (NOT to a date already used by that plan — `unique(plan_id, due_date)`) and run `DO $$ BEGIN EXECUTE (SELECT command FROM cron.job WHERE jobid = 16); END $$;` via MCP — same Bearer, key never leaves Postgres. Clean up after: delete the test plans (schedule rows CASCADE), un-suspend the member, delete pipeline `MEMBER_MEMBERSHIP` notifications.

**238. Stripe webhook side effects for a failure must live in EVERY event consumer that can flip the row, gated on rows-actually-flipped (2026-07-17).** A first-payment ACH bounce emits BOTH `payment_intent.payment_failed` AND `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, in NO guaranteed order. The membership M1 side effects (bell + email) originally lived only in the session block; in live testing the PI event won the race, flipped the row first, and the session block matched zero `processing` rows — side effects silently skipped. Fix (v620): both blocks carry the same side-effect arm, each fired ONLY when its own `.update(...).select()` actually flipped rows — whichever event wins does the work, the loser flips nothing and skips, so double-firing is structurally impossible. Pattern rule: when two webhook events can settle the same row, (a) key every side effect to the flip result, never to the event type alone, and (b) implement the side effects in BOTH consumers. First-payment bounces bell + email but do NOT suspend (member only just set up); pull bounces suspend too; termination-fee bounces bell only.

**239. `member_payment_schedule` uniqueness is per (plan_id, due_date) for `kind='membership'` ONLY — a partial unique index, not a table constraint (2026-07-17).** The original blanket `unique(plan_id, due_date)` rejected a termination fee on any day the plan already had a row due — discovered live when terminating a plan whose FIRST payment was that same day: the fee PaymentIntent was already created at Stripe, then the ledger insert 500'd, leaving the plan Active with a charged-but-unrecorded fee. Migration `20260717190000_membership_schedule_unique_per_month_kind` drops the constraint and creates `member_payment_schedule_plan_due_membership_key` UNIQUE (plan_id, due_date) WHERE kind='membership' — duplicate-month protection for pulls is preserved; termination fees are exempt (flow-guarded instead: terminate refuses already-closed plans). Recovery interplay: the fee key `membership-term-<plan>-<today>` is date-scoped, so a SAME-DAY retry replays the identical PI (no double charge) — but if the fee PI's bounce event fires BEFORE the retry's row insert exists, that event matches nothing and the row can stay `processing` forever (seen once in testing; artifact of the failed first attempt). If a termination-fee row is stuck `processing`, check the PI in Stripe before assuming it's still pending.

**240. `members.membership_suspended` is the AUTOMATION-owned suspension flag; the admin's manual toggle owns `members.suspended` — displays OR the two, writers never cross (2026-07-17).** The membership sweep/webhook failure paths suspend by setting `membership_suspended=true` and the sweep's pass 4 auto-clears ONLY `membership_suspended` when the plan is caught up — it never reads or writes `suspended`, so a manual admin suspension survives a caught-up membership plan (the old shared-flag code silently un-suspended admin-suspended members every night, and a dues failure hijacked the member-wide flag). The manual profile toggle keeps writing `suspended` only; when `membership_suspended` is set the profile shows a read-only "Suspended for membership dues — clears automatically when payments catch up" note. EVERY display treats "Suspended" as `suspended || membership_suspended` (MembersPanel roster/profile/standing filter, MemberKpiPanel lens + standing list, MemberOverviewPanel, MembershipFeesPanel pill) — a NEW members-reading surface must OR both flags, and `load_data`'s member WHITELIST + `membership_plans_load`'s member select both carry `membership_suspended` (a new consumer needs it in its select). Automation must NEVER write `suspended`; admin UIs must NEVER write `membership_suspended`.

**UPDATED 2026-08-24 (`vfo-admin-api` v778) — these three flags are no longer display-only: they GATE MONEY.** All four member payout engines (MAP 1 `contract-revshare`, `tax/revshare`, `msm/pip-revshare`, `utils/specialist-revenue-payout`) now call `memberHoldReason()` in `utils/member-payout-hold.ts` and **park the member leg in a non-terminal held status instead of transferring** — with the same precedence as the display rule plus an ordering: `suspended || membership_suspended` reads as *suspended*, `paused` reads as *paused*, suspension outranking a pause. This makes the "a new consumer needs it in its select" clause above **load-bearing rather than cosmetic**: a payout engine whose member `select` omits `suspended` / `paused` / `membership_suspended` silently pays a suspended member (which is exactly what happened to Timothy Mustapha 59448 on 2026-08-21 — two $675 MAP 1 transfers, deliberately not clawed back). Every **gating reader** must select all three columns, and clearing a flag must release: `utils/member-payout-release.ts` re-fires held legs from the profile save's transition detect and from the membership sweep's pass 4 (which fires only when NO reason remains, so an auto-cleared `membership_suspended` does not release a manually `suspended` member). The writer rule is unchanged and now matters more. Cross-ref **#432**, **#433**, **#434**, **#303**.

**241. Membership update-method links expire 30 days after last being emailed — ACTIVE plans only; the resend is the SAME `membership_send_setup_link` action branching on plan status (2026-07-17).** `member_payment_plans.setup_link_expires_at` is stamped now+30d by every emailer of the link (`send-setup-link` both variants, the failure-email helper, `send-reminder`) AND by activation (both branches — the member just used the link). `setup-load`/`setup-checkout` enforce it ONLY when `plan.status='active'` (NULL or past = 400 "link has expired"); setup_pending links never expire (unchanged design), and legacy active plans with NULL are expired-until-resent by design. For an ACTIVE plan, `membership_send_setup_link` now sends the `MEMBERSHIP_update_link` template (id 195, tokens `[First Name]`/`[UPDATE_LINK]`, Draft-mode like the rest) and returns `{ update_link: true, ... }`; the mode-mismatch guard REFUSES the update variant with a 400 rather than minting a cross-mode customer. Membership go-live now flips FOUR templates Draft→Send, not three. A new emailer of `/membership-pay?token=` links MUST also stamp `setup_link_expires_at` or the link it sends may already be dead.

**242. Step-completion dates across the admin tracking tracks are editable inline via the shared `components/shared/StepDate.jsx` (2026-07-17; UPDATED 2026-07-20 — see #249: StepDate now buffers typing and commits on blur/Enter, not per-change, AND the pattern was extended to advisor/accountant onboarding via `save_onboarding_step_date`).** Every admin step/task track auto-stamps today's date on completion; that date is now an editable compact native `<input type="date">` (the shared `StepDate` component) instead of a read-only `formatDate()` span. `StepDate` renders NOTHING when its value is empty (so not-started rows stay clean) and themes automatically via the global `color-scheme` in `index.css` (no extra dark-mode CSS). Wired into the manual-completion status-select rows of SIX files: `admin/MSMTracking.jsx` (training track), `admin/map1/ClientTrackViewV2.jsx`, `admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, `admin/regular/RegularPrioritiesTab.jsx`, `admin/pft/PFTEngagementTrack.jsx`, `admin/pip/PipMeetingsTab.jsx`. Each save path already accepted a caller-supplied date, so the onChange just calls the existing `saveTask(id, status, newDate[, extra])` — NO backend change. Deliberately LEFT read-only (NOT editable): member/`readOnly` views, `status_options==='auto'` automation read-outs, and event-driven dates (payment received, invoice/receipt sent, BoldSign/CEO signed) — those reflect real events, not steps you check off. Decision-form/checkbox-driven completions (Tax/PIP decision forms, PIP checklist, MAP 4 confirm/followup) were also left as-is (their own "Phase 2", not built). The MAP 1 client-task track (`ClientsPanel` in MSMTracking) and Tax already had an editable date input before this session.

**243. Partnership-Fast-Track-only "Complete - NO EMAIL" backfill buttons on the email-sending steps (2026-07-17).** In `admin/pft/PFTEngagementTrack.jsx` ONLY, the four email-sending steps gained solid-green (filled, vs the tinted send buttons) no-email completion controls so old-system accountants can be marked up-to-date without firing emails: the three `MeetingStep`s (Meeting 1/2/3 confirmation email) get a single "Complete - NO EMAIL" button that writes status `Complete` via the existing `saveTask` (→ `msm_save_client_task`, no email); the `DecisionStep` (Accountant decision confirmation email) gets a "Complete - NO EMAIL:" row with VFO FT / VFO Associate / No buttons that write the REAL outcome status (`VFO FT confirmed` / `VFO Associate confirmed` / `No confirmed`) so the matching Phase-6 track reveals, but send no email AND — critically — do NOT create the Accountant Onboarding record (that only happens in the email automation `automation_PFT_decisionemail`). So a no-email decision leaves the "AI PC Admin → Accountant Onboarding record created" history line correctly un-done. Frontend-only, no backend action added. Because all PFT email sends live in this one component, the change is inherently PFT-only — it cannot leak into Holistic.
**244. `extra_cc` is comma-separated TEXT and the decision forms send ARRAYS — every write path must normalize, every read goes through `utils/extra-cc.ts extraCcList()` (2026-07-18).** `PIPDecisionForm.jsx` / the tax decision form submit `ccRecipients` as a string array. `tax/decision.ts` always joined it to a comma string, but `pipeline/pipfu-decision.ts` stored the raw value — PostgREST serialized the array as a JSON string (`["a@b.com","c@d.com"]`) into the text column, the read sites' `.split(",")` produced addresses with brackets/quotes glued on, and Gmail rejected the whole email with 400 "Invalid Cc header" (killed client 75's MAP 1 agreement email on 2026-07-17; the agreement/BoldSign doc itself was created fine — only the email died). Fix: pipfu-decision now normalizes like tax (array → `trim/filter(Boolean).join(",")`), and the four read sites (contract-send-agreement, tax send-agreement, tax final-decision, tax extra-meeting) parse via the shared defensive `extraCcList()` (handles NULL, legacy JSON-array strings, and comma text). A NEW `extra_cc` writer must store a comma string (or reuse the normalization); a NEW reader must use `extraCcList()`, never a bare `.split(",")`. `dedupeEmails()` remains the second net (drops non-email junk).

**245. Four admin UI guardrails are FRONTEND-ONLY — real enforcement still lives (or doesn't) on the backend (2026-07-18).** All four gates added this session are client-side UX only; `add_member_full` is NOT in `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`, so the create/open gates are bypassable via direct API (user explicitly chose frontend-only). (a) **MSM-before-programs** (`MSMTracking.jsx ProgramToggles`): enabling a program is blocked until an MSM is assigned. The gate reads a NEW `hasMsm` prop derived from `MSMTracking`-owned `savedMsm` state, NOT `member.assigned_msm` — because the MSM-assignment save deliberately NO LONGER calls the parent `onDataChange` (that global reload flashed the profile skeleton; it now just persists + shows "Saved!" like the client-PF save). Consequence: after an MSM save, `member.assigned_msm` (from the global members list) stays STALE until a full reload — do not add logic that depends on it refreshing post-save; read `savedMsm` or re-fetch. `MsmAssignment` is now a controlled component (value/onChange/onSaved). (b) **VFO Reconciliation (Free) open-gate** (`MembersPanel.jsx MemberDirectoryView`): non-superadmin row click shows a cursor-anchored "Access Denied for this member type" flash (`position:fixed` at `e.clientX/clientY`, `pointerEvents:none`, click-only never hover, ~1.4s, inline red no-entry SVG — no emoji) and does not open. **(Updated 2026-07-20: the gate is now `canOpenReconciliation = is_superadmin || session.email === 'platham@elitert.com'` — Paul Latham, role admin, is explicitly allowlisted to open these members alongside Jake; every other non-superadmin still blocked. Add future allowlisted admins to that OR in `MemberDirectoryView`.)** **(Updated 2026-08-31: the gate now has a second axis — a module-scope `RECONCILIATION_EXEMPT_MEMBERS` list of MEMBER NUMBERS that ANY admin may open, feeding a `reconciliationLocked(m)` helper. It holds `'59524'`, the standing sandbox-forced Test Member (#251), which carries this member_type only because the test account sits on the free tier; the lock otherwise made the test member untestable by anyone but Jake and Paul. Exempt a MEMBER there; allowlist an ADMIN in the OR above — they are different axes and neither should be used for the other.)** (c) **Create gate**: `!getSession()?.is_superadmin` bails each of the 3 create-form `submit()`s with status "Access Denied"; Add sub-tabs stay open. (d) **Client PF gate** (`ClientDetail.jsx`): program tabs (`pft`/`map1`/`regular`/`tax`/`pip`) render a muted centered "Please Select a PF" card (cloned from the Regular/Tax "not yet enabled" empty-state) until `client.assigned_pf` is set; `pfLocked = !isMember && !assigned_pf` (members never gated, and it also covers `?tab=` deep-links since it gates the render, not just the click). Same session dropped the "✓" from the PF/status "Saved!" confirmations for consistency with the MSM save.

**246. "Standard Coaching" = the 5th MSM program; adding an MSM program is a `programs` data-row insert + frontend wiring in ~5 spots (the backend is fully program-agnostic). The MSM Home "Advanced/Standard Meetings" tiles count `coaching_meetings` (per enrollment), NOT `member_meetings` (2026-07-20).** Programs live in `programs` (id/name/active). `msm_load_programs` returns every `active` row; enroll/toggle/coaching-meeting/program-notes handlers key on `program_id`/`enrollment_id`/`program_name`, so a NEW program needs ZERO backend code — just an INSERT (migration `20260720000000_standard_coaching_program.sql`, id=5) plus frontend arrays. Wiring spots for a program: `admin/MSMTracking.jsx` (`PROGRAMS` array + `activeSection→key` map); `admin/MembersPanel.jsx` (`DEFAULT_MSM_OPTIONS` + the `msm_program_*` render-guard `.includes([...])`); `member/MemberMSMTracking.jsx` (`PROGRAMS` + `activeTab→key` map + Home `tabKey` map); `pages/MemberPortal.jsx` (`PROGRAM_KEYS` ×2 + `tabLabels` + `PROGRAM_ORDER`). Display ORDER is controlled by these hardcoded arrays — the DB `.order("name")` is alphabetical and ignored for layout; the member MSM nav sorts enabled programs by the canonical `PROGRAM_ORDER` so it matches the admin toggle list. **Standard = Advanced Coaching minus the Renewal tab:** gate the header/status/Edit chrome with `isCoachingLike = isCoaching || isStandard`, and a separate `isStandard` branch renders Home + Meetings only (no Renewal). **Counter data-source trap:** the MSM Home "Meeting Summary" tiles are TWO different sources — `MSM Meetings`/`PFT 90 Day Plan` count `member_meetings` by `meeting_type` (the "+ Log MSM Meeting" table; only `MSM Meeting` rows exist in practice), while `Advanced Meetings`/`Standard Meetings` count COMPLETED rows from `coaching_meetings` per enrollment (the program's Meetings tab writes there via `coaching_log_meeting`, NOT member_meetings). The pre-existing `meeting_type==='Advanced Meeting'` filter was dead (always 0) and was rewired to the coaching source this session. Counts refresh on entering the Home tab (effect on `activeSection`/`activeTab`) so a just-logged meeting shows without a reload. **Program-toggle enabling** updates local `enabledPrograms` state optimistically (no `loadData` reload → no skeleton flash; no "Saved!" text — the toggle IS the save); backend is a single `member_program_enabled` upsert/delete.

**247. Partnership Fast Track now has an Automation & Config panel + the ONLY UI for the `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK` sandbox toggle; the meeting-confirmation steps carry a SECOND "we declined them" email path; the automation panel's Meeting 1 card is gated on tracking-owner = Member (2026-07-20).** THREE related things shipped together (backend v625, +1 action → 414, FE not yet deployed). (a) **Second decline path.** `automation_PFT_meetingemail` decision vocab grew from {`confirm_date`,`confirm_no_date`,`declined`} to add **`us_declined`** — where `declined` = the client declines US (template `PFT_meeting_declined`, unchanged) and `us_declined` = WE decline THEM, which REQUIRES `decline_reason` and injects `[DECLINE_REASON]`. Template is chosen by meeting number: **Meeting 1 → `PFT_meeting1_member_declined`** (id 197, "chat with us regarding VFO Partnership Fast Track", button label "Send Email - Member Declined", status `Declined by Member`); **Meeting 2/3 → `PFT_meeting_ert_declined`** (id 196, "meet with us regarding the VFO Partnership Fast Track", button "Send Email - ERT/VFOS Declined", status `Declined by ERT/VFOS`). Both templates PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK, `active`, Draft (`send_mode=false`), To CLIENT / Cc MEMBER+ASSIGNED_PF / Bcc aanderson+platham, subject `Partnership Fast Track - [FULL_NAME]`, body `[FIRST_NAME]`+`[DECLINE_REASON]` (+ VFO signature appended in code). The frontend `MeetingStep` (`admin/pft/PFTEngagementTrack.jsx`) is now a **3-row layout** (green/green confirm · red/red client-decline + we-decline · solid-green Complete-NO-EMAIL) with an inline reason+preview card cloned from the Tax 3 decline card; the confirm buttons were renamed "Send Email - Date Confirmed/Date Not Confirmed", client-decline renamed "Send Email - Client Declined". The Meeting-2 discovery-form section now attaches ONLY on confirm decisions (guard tightened from `decision !== "declined"` to an explicit confirm check, so `us_declined` never mints a discovery token). `client` prop is now threaded from `ClientDetail.jsx` for the preview name. (b) **Automation panel.** NEW `admin/PFTAutomationPanel.jsx` at Automation & Config → Partnership Fast Track (`AdminPortal.jsx` `automationSection='pft_pipeline'`, added after Accountant Onboarding), StepKit-styled like the Advisor/Accountant panels, fed by NEW AUTH/admin action **`automation_load_pft_pipelines`** (`actions/pft/automation-load-pipelines.ts`; registered in `dispatch.ts` + BOTH role-gate lists — the ADMIN gate group AND `TAB_ACTIONS.automation`). It lists ONE row per client with ANY PFT email activity (a meeting `client_progress` row OR a `pft_engagement` row) — deliberately mirrors how the Advisor/Accountant panels only surface entities that ENTERED the flow (there is no standalone "PFT onboarding record"; the first automated touch is the Meeting 1 email). Per-client step cards read meeting outcomes from `client_progress` and the button-bearing emails (discovery/decision/FT) + which button the client clicked + when from `pft_engagement`. **The panel hero holds the ONLY UI for the PFT sandbox toggle** (`SandboxModeToggle pipeline="PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK"`) — before this the `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK` `pipeline_sandbox_config` row could only be flipped via SQL; PFT has no per-client automation surface elsewhere. (c) **Meeting-1 gate.** The panel's Meeting 1 card only shows when the Initial-Contact "Who is completing the tracking for this accountant?" step = **Member** — the loader reads that gate task's `client_progress`, returns `tracking_owner`, and NULLs Meeting 1 (and excludes Meeting-1-only clients from the list) when it's VFOS/unset; the panel renders the Meeting 1 card only when `row.tracking_owner === 'Member'`. Mirrors the track's own rule where VFOS makes the Initial-Contact steps inert.

**248. Every email-sending step in every admin track/panel carries a StepEmailsChip email-preview tag; template previews load via the plain-admin action `load_email_previews`; TEMPLATE_META moved OUT of EmailTemplatesPanel into `shared/templateMeta.js` (2026-07-20).** The shared `components/shared/StepEmailsChip.jsx` renders a tiny inline "Emails (N)" pill NEXT TO the step/label text that opens a centered modal listing every email that step can send (one card per variant, live `email_templates` subject/body + To/Cc/Bcc rendered via ROLE_LABELS). Conventions a NEW email-sending step must follow: (a) mount the chip beside the step label with `pipeline` + `templates=[{name,when}]` using EXACT `email_templates.template_name` values (incl. `|variant` suffixes); (b) the "member signing/paying on clients behalf" twin rows are AUTO-appended by the chip — never list them; (c) member-visible tracks (ClientTrackViewV2 / TaxPrioritiesTab / RegularPrioritiesTab / PipMeetingsTab / PFTEngagementTrack render with `readOnly={isMember}` from ClientDetail) MUST guard the chip with `!readOnly`; (d) pass `context={...}` mapping token inner-text to its known value (client/member/PF/onboardee names, PFT meeting phrases, membership plan facts) — resolved tokens render as plain text, unknown ones as bracketed `[Token]` chips; matching is case/underscore/space-insensitive; empty values must be OMITTED (not ''); token substitution skips HTML tag/attribute segments so links never break; (e) pass `title` = the step label (modal header). DATA path: `load_email_previews` (actions/email-templates/previews-load.ts, AUTH + ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS, deliberately NOT tab-gated — every admin may call it, unlike the Automation-tab-gated `automation_load_email_templates`) returns active rows by `pipeline`; the chip falls back to `automation_load_email_templates` if the new action errors (works for superadmin pre-deploy). CATALOG move: `mv()`/`TEMPLATE_META`/`ROLE_LABELS` now live in `components/shared/templateMeta.js` (plus `TOKEN_LEGEND`/`friendlyToken`, currently unused by the chip) and EmailTemplatesPanel imports them — a NEW templated email adds its META entry THERE (updates the location in gotcha #181). The chips are read-only display: no send logic, no action calls beyond the loader.

**249. `StepDate` commits on blur/Enter (local buffer), NOT per keystroke; editable onboarding dates go through the NEW admin action `save_onboarding_step_date` against allow-listed `*_at` columns — never a step's own save handler (2026-07-20).** TWO related changes. (a) **Typing fix** — `components/shared/StepDate.jsx` was a *controlled* native `<input type="date">` whose `onChange` fired the caller's async save (`saveTask`/`saveStepDate`) on every change; that save DISABLED the input (`disabled={saving[id]}`) and round-tripped a fresh `value` mid-edit, so typing "12" into a month segment landed as "02" (first digit clobbered before the second registered). Fixed by giving StepDate a local `useState` buffer + `useRef` editing flag: `onChange` only sets local state, and the value is pushed to the caller's `onChange` on **blur / Enter** (one save per edit). Props/signature UNCHANGED (`value`/`onChange`/`disabled`), so all 6 tracking-track callers (MAP 1/Tax/Regular/PIP/PFT/MSM) keep passing `onChange={d => save(...)}`; they now fire once on blur instead of per keystroke. A NEW StepDate caller must NOT expect a per-keystroke save. (Updates gotcha #242, which described the old per-change behavior.) (b) **Editable onboarding dates** — Advisor & Accountant onboarding step dates were read-only `formatDate()` spans over server-stamped `*_at` timestamptz columns. The manual-completion `Row`s now render `StepDate` when given a new `onDateChange` prop (Advisor: `prelim_meeting_status_at`, `onboarding_team_member_at`, `prelim_meeting_decision_at`; Accountant: those + `accountant_partnership_at`, `cc_advisor_at`); the `AutoRow` automated-event timestamps (agreement sent/signed, payment made, emails sent, member created) stay READ-ONLY — they record when real events happened, not steps you check off. There is NO per-field date param on the existing `save_advisor_*`/`save_accountant_*` handlers (they stamp `now()`), so a dedicated action was added rather than overloading the automation/email-sending handlers: `save_onboarding_step_date` (`actions/onboarding/step-date.ts`, `{pipeline, onboarding_id, field, date}`) validates `pipeline`∈{ADVISOR,ACCOUNTANT} and `field` against a per-pipeline ALLOW-LIST of the editable `*_at` columns, stores the `YYYY-MM-DD` at UTC-midnight ISO, returns the updated `{onboarding}`. Registered in `router/dispatch.ts` + `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (NOT tab-gated, mirrors the other onboarding saves). To make a NEW onboarding date editable: add its column to the pipeline's allow-list in `step-date.ts` AND pass `onDateChange={d => saveStepDate('<col>', d)}` on the `Row`. **Specialist onboarding was deliberately SKIPPED** — its stage/meeting/exec-vote flow has no per-step completion-date affordance to match (would need a new UI, not a retrofit).

**250. Client tax-return access has TWO independent levers — the git-auditable code allowlist (`constants/tax-access.ts` `isTaxAdmin`) AND the live-editable `notification_rules` recipients — and they must be changed TOGETHER; removing/adding a person in one place alone leaves the other stale (2026-07-21).** WHO CAN OPEN/SHARE/DELETE a client tax return = `isTaxAdmin(email)` (the `TAX_VIEWERS` set in `constants/tax-access.ts`) — this is CODE, so a change requires a `vfo-admin-api` DEPLOY to take effect (Option A / "change = deploy"). It gates ONLY the CLIENT `client-tax-returns` bucket (`vault_tax_download`/`_admin_upload_url`/`_delete` + `canShareBucket` in `actions/sharing/shared.ts`); the Member-vault (`member-tax-returns`) and Specialist-vault (`specialist-tax-returns`) sensitive sections are NOT allowlist-gated (any admin with vault access can open) and fire NO tax notification. WHO GETS NOTIFIED on a client sensitive-section upload = `actions/vault/upload-notify.ts` `fireUploadNotification`, which branches on a `program_id=4` (VFO Tax Planning) `client_tax_plans` row: program-4 → `TAX_returns_received`, else → `UPLOAD_tax_return_uploaded`. The recipient list resolves at runtime via `notifyByRule` = the rule's DB `recipients` column IF non-empty (already live, no deploy), ELSE the code `defaultRecipients` array passed at the call site. So to change notification recipients you edit the CODE arrays (source of truth, deploy-owed) AND the live `notification_rules.recipients`/`default_recipients` (immediate). When an admin LEAVES the team, removing their login is NOT enough: you must also pull them from `TAX_VIEWERS` (and deploy) so they lose view/share, and from both notification rules. This session removed Tim Gacsy (`tgacsy@`, left) from all three and added Tracy (`tnmiller@`) to the holistic `UPLOAD_tax_return_uploaded` rule so both bells now go to Tracy + Tray. **`TAX_VIEWERS` is FOUR people (Jake / Tray / Paul / Tracy), and since 2026-08-18 it is no longer the whole VIEW answer:** `constants/tax-access.ts` also exports `canViewClientTaxVault(supabase, email, clientId)` — the allowlist OR the admin whose session email equals `taxPfLoginEmail(clients.assigned_pf)` for THAT ONE client — and `vault_tax_list` (`can_view`) + `vault_tax_download` BOTH call it so the flag and the 403 can never disagree. The carve-out is **VIEW/download only and per-client**: `vault_tax_admin_upload_url`, `vault_tax_delete` and `canShareBucket` still call bare `isTaxAdmin` and stay four-person. A new "may this admin OPEN this client's returns" caller must call `canViewClientTaxVault`, never re-derive it. **Because view and manage split, `vault_tax_list` now returns TWO flags** — `can_view` (open) and the narrower `can_manage` (= `isTaxAdmin` OR planner, i.e. the pre-carve-out expression) — and `ClientVaultTab.jsx` keys the sensitive section's `canManage` on the first and its `canWrite` on the second, reusing the seam the ERT section already had. **EVERY write affordance there (Add, Delete, Share, drag-to-move) keys on `canWrite`; only View and the unlocked-row chrome key on `canManage`.** Widening a VIEW predicate without splitting the flag is how you ship a #309-class dead control: the button renders off `can_view` and then 403s off the unchanged server gate.

**251. Any NEW client-scoped money / email / BoldSign handler in TAX, MAP 1, or PIP MUST resolve its sandbox mode via `loadSandboxConfigForClient(sb, pipelineName, clientId)` (never the bare `loadSandboxConfig`), which is force-ON-only: it can push a case to sandbox but never a real case to live (2026-07-21).** The per-case test-member override (`constants/test-sandbox.ts` `TEST_SANDBOX_MEMBER_NUMBERS = ["59524"]`) exists so a single production member's cases run against Stripe sandbox / BoldSign test / `sandbox_email` redirect even when the pipeline's global toggle is LIVE — it lets Jake exercise the real live automations end-to-end without charging a real card. `loadSandboxConfigForClient` (`integrations/sandbox-config.ts`) first reads the global `pipeline_sandbox_config` row for the pipeline; if already sandbox it returns unchanged, else it calls `isTestSandboxClient` (resolves `clients.member_number`, falling back to the `client_ref` numeric prefix, and matches the list) and, only on a hit, returns a synthesized config with `sandbox_mode/stripe_test_mode/boldsign_test_mode=true` while PRESERVING the global `sandbox_email`. Every failure path (missing client, no member number, DB error, exception) returns the UNCHANGED global result — it is fail-safe and can only ever ADD sandbox, never weaken live. `loadTaxSandboxConfigForClient(sb, clientId)` is the TAX-pinned delegate. It is threaded through ~25 TAX handlers/sweeps + 3 TAX webhook branches, 13 MAP1 handlers, 7 PIP handlers, the chargescheduled/contract-revshare/check-reminder sweeps (PER-ROW, with a small cache since a sweep touches many clients), and the webhook MAP1/PIP/card_update branches. Person-level Connect handlers, display/read loaders, and SPECREV/membership/advisor/accountant are DELIBERATELY global (not client-scoped money movement, or no per-client member link). A new client-scoped charge/refund/transfer/email/agreement path that calls `loadSandboxConfig` directly silently ignores the test member and would hit LIVE Stripe for the test case — always use the `-ForClient` variant. **ALWAYS-APPLIES.**

**252. A revenue-share `share` value is ALWAYS a dollar amount of the TOTAL engagement, and the portion paid on any one installment is proportional to what was received: `portion = (share / totalGross) * paymentReceived` — never a percent-of-payment heuristic (2026-07-21).** The legacy code carried a booby-trap: "if share > 100 treat it as dollars, else treat it as a percent of the payment." That mis-paid every multi-payment split whenever the per-installment share happened to be < 100, and it was removed this session from EVERY consumer — tax member + strategic legs (`actions/tax/revshare.ts`), the tax planner leg (`utils/tax-planner-payout.ts`), MAP1 member + strategic legs (`actions/pipeline/contract-revshare.ts`), both sweeps' strategic retries, AND the display sites (`actions/payments/normalize.ts` `buildMap1Rows`/`buildTaxRows` — tax rows were previously shown mis-halved on both legs — plus `all-payments-load.ts`). The fallback chain for `totalGross`: TAX = `total_fee` → `retainer_amount + implementation_amount` → last-resort `share / 2`; MAP1 = `gross_fee` → `net_invoice` → legacy flat. A NEW multi-payment split consumer MUST compute `share/total × payment` (retainer and implementation each get their proportional slice, summing to the full share), and any DISPLAY of that split MUST use the same math as the payout or the Payments tab and the transfer disagree. PIP was verified already clean (no heuristic). **ALWAYS-APPLIES.**

**253. The Tax Planner Share is paid to the planner's GROUP ("company"), resolved `tax_planners.member_type` -> `tax_planning_groups.name` (exact match) -> `tax_planning_groups.stripe_account_id`; a planner's own `stripe_account_id`/`revenue_decision` are retired — the ENTIRE planner share transfers to the group (2026-07-21).** Mirrors the strategic-partner model (`strategic_member_groups`). `transferPlannerShare` (`utils/tax-planner-payout.ts`) writes `{retainer,implementation}_planner_paid` with statuses `Yes` / `Money Mapping` / `N/A — No Share Due` / `Failed`; it lands `Failed` (with a `FAILURE_tax_planner_share` action-required bell to Jake and a next-sweep retry) when the planner has NO `member_type`, the named group is missing, or the group has no Stripe account — the same three levers must all be set for a payout to land. Group RENAME cascades every member planner's `member_type` (so the exact-name link never breaks); group DELETE is guarded when any planner still references it. Idempotency key `planner-tax-<plan.id>-<kind>`; the sweep retry covers `Failed` AND stranded-NULL `*_planner_paid` rows. Confirmation emails `TAX_planner_revshare|retainer` / `|implementation` (Draft) go to the planner's own email; the Stripe transfer memo reads `Tax Planning Revenue Share - ... - Tax Planner: <name> — <group> - Retainer|Implementation`. The `tax_planners.revenue_decision` column still exists (default 'Revenue Share') but was wiped to '' and dropped from the planner UI — group membership, not a per-planner decision, governs whether/where the share lands.

**254. The "Allocate to Advanced Tax Planner" step is a task-style dropdown of ACTIVE tax planners (task ids 84 program 1 + 118 program 4); it triggers on `status_options === 'tax_planner_select'` OR a task-name fallback, allocates via the NEW action `tax_allocate_planner`, and gates the Yes-path (2026-07-21).** `tax_allocate_planner` (`actions/tax/allocate-planner.ts`, ADMIN_ONLY) sets `client_tax_plans.tax_planner_id` (a null/empty `tax_planner_id` CLEARS it — unselect), verifies the planner exists (404 otherwise), and also writes the task's `client_tax_progress` status to the plain planner name; the step chip shows `[Stripe Connected]` / `[No Stripe]` reflecting the GROUP's account (not the planner's). BACKEND + FRONTEND both gate the Yes-path: `decision` (Yes), `pricing`, and `extra-meeting` (Yes) return 400 without a `tax_planner_id`, and the frontend disables submit with "You must allocate a tax planner before submitting." **DEPLOY-TIME STEP OWED:** the live rows 84/118 still carry `status_options='Tim Gacsy|Steven Cox'`; the swap `UPDATE program_client_tasks SET status_options='tax_planner_select' WHERE id IN (84,118)` runs at the FE deploy (the name-fallback keeps the dropdown working until then).

**255. A member-driven training-track step (the member adds tracker rows; the step's status is DERIVED from the entry count, not set by an admin dropdown) keys on a sentinel `status_options` (`'tracker_accountants'` / `'tracker_clients'`) OR an exact task-name fallback, resolved in BOTH repos, and its status is written ONLY by `syncTrackerProgress` — which never overwrites `Stopped`/`N/A` (2026-07-21). ALWAYS-APPLIES.** Two 90 Day Plan steps became member-driven this session: task id 76 program 2 (Partnership Fast Track) "Add Minimum of 2 Accountants to Tracker" (kind `accountant`, threshold 2) and task id 19 program 1 (VFO Holistic) "Add Minimum 5 Clients to Client Target List" (kind `client`, threshold 5). The resolver lives in TWO places that MUST stay in sync — backend `actions/msm/tracker-config.ts` `resolveTrackerConfig` and frontend `components/shared/trackerSteps.js` `isTrackerTask` — each checking the sentinel `status_options` FIRST, then falling back to the exact task name (the same pattern as gotcha #254, used until the deploy-time swap flips the DB rows). The member adds rows via `training_tracker_add` (MEMBER-only 403 gate + `denyIfNotOwnEnrollment`; required `first_name`/`last_name`/`email`(must contain '@')/`warm_cold`, optional `firm_name`/`phone`/`source`/`notes`) into `training_tracker_entries`; an admin removes rows via `training_tracker_delete` (ADMIN-only). BOTH add and delete re-count the entries and call the shared `syncTrackerProgress`, which DERIVES the `member_training_progress` status: 0 entries → the progress row is DELETED (renders grey), 1..threshold-1 → `In Progress` (orange), ≥threshold → `Completed` (green, stamps `completed_date` on first completion + `completed_by`=member name). `syncTrackerProgress` NEVER overwrites a status of `Stopped` or `N/A` (admin escape hatches). A single **completion-only** FYI bell fires to the assigned MSM (`resolveAssignedMsmEmail`, rule keys `TRAINING_tracker_accountant_added`/`_client_added`) ONLY when an add crosses the threshold and the step goes `Completed` (`synced.status === "Completed" && total === config.threshold`) — NOT per add; wrapped in try/catch so a notify failure never fails the add. **DEPLOY-TIME STEP OWED:** the live rows still carry the legacy admin dropdown options; the swaps `UPDATE program_training_tasks SET status_options='tracker_accountants' WHERE id=76;` and `... SET status_options='tracker_clients' WHERE id=19;` run at the FE deploy (the name-fallback keeps both steps working until then, after which the old `Completed|Outstanding|Stopped` options are gone). A NEW member-driven step must extend BOTH resolvers, add its own threshold, and reuse `syncTrackerProgress`. Edge case: a member admin-marked `Completed` before this feature shows green with 0 entries until entries load on expand, then the count pill self-corrects (the progress status persists until entries are added/deleted).

**256. A role-gate list entry that matches NO dispatched action name protects nothing — grep `dispatch.ts` for the EXACT registered name when gating an action (2026-07-21).** `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (`constants/role-gates.ts`) carried the name `msm_save_training_progress`, but the actually-dispatched training-status writer is `msm_save_training_task` (registered in `router/dispatch.ts`) — there is NO `msm_save_training_progress` handler. The dead entry gated nothing, so the real `msm_save_training_task` fell through to the default (member-callable) path: any member session could set any training status on any enrollment (an ungated cross-tenant write, first noted as a latent gap in `flows/msm-tracking.md`). Fixed by adding the REAL name `msm_save_training_task` to `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (the dead name was left in place, harmless). Rule: a gate string is only as good as its match against a dispatched action — when adding OR auditing a role-gate entry, confirm the exact string appears as a registered key in `dispatch.ts` (or a login in `index.ts`); a typo or a renamed/removed action silently un-gates the handler. `see #256`.

**257. The Tax Planner portal authorization model: `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` is the ONLY boundary (a `tax_planner` caller SKIPS both the tab gate and the ADMIN_ONLY gate), so EVERY planner-callable action MUST ALSO carry an in-handler whole-GROUP-scope ownership guard; token-forwarding chains re-enter the gate AS the planner, and PUBLIC_HANDLERS chain targets bypass the gate entirely (2026-07-22). ALWAYS-APPLIES.** The Tax Planner portal (5th portal / 6th login type) added a `tax_planner` caller role to `middleware/auth.ts` (precedence admin → client → specialist → **tax_planner** [`tax_planner_logins` by session email] → member; new `AuthContext.callerTaxPlannerId`). Like clients/specialists it is **deny-by-default**: a planner may ONLY call the ~36 entries in `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (`constants/role-gates.ts`) — and because a planner is not an admin and not a member, it SKIPS both the per-tab gate (`TAB_ACTIONS`) and `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS`. The `ADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` predicate was therefore changed from `callerRole !== "admin"` to `callerRole === "member"` (verified safe: every `allowed_admins` row is role 'admin'; clients/specialists/planners are each fenced by their OWN allowlist FIRST, so the loosened admin-gate never actually reaches them). A planner can NEVER run `SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS` (an explicit unconditional guard). BECAUSE the allowlist is the only role boundary, EVERY planner-callable tax handler ALSO calls an in-handler group-scope guard from **`utils/tax-planner-ownership.ts`** — `denyIfNotPlannerClient(sb, clientId, body, json)` / `denyIfNotPlannerPlan(sb, taxPlanId, body, json)` (both no-op/null for non-planner callers) — which grants **WHOLE-GROUP rights**: a planner may view/edit any client whose `client_tax_plans` row is allocated to ANY planner sharing their `tax_planners.member_type` (Tax Planning Group; empty `member_type` = a group of one; helper `resolvePlannerGroupIds`). The guards resolve the caller from `body.token` (`admin_sessions` → allowed_admins precedence mirror FIRST → `tax_planner_logins`) so they MIRROR the middleware precedence (an admin-who-is-also-a-planner is treated as admin). Applied to ~18 tax handlers (save-task, allocate-planner [a planner may only allocate a TARGET planner inside their OWN group], load-progress, load-specialists, add-specialist, save-deposit-pi, msm/update-tax-status, decision, ready-for-tax3, highlevel-meeting-confirm, presentation-schedule, request-returns, pricing, extra-meeting, implement-decision, postreview-decision, deposit-refund, send-agreement) + client-scoped loaders (tax/load-plans, client-notes/load, msm/load-client-home, msm/load-client-progress) + vault (gen-list/download, admin-ert list/download [planner reaches ONLY entity='client', fail-closed], tax-list/download). **CHAIN CLOSURE:** `automation_TAX_decision`/`pricing`/`extrameeting` forward the caller's token to `automation_TAX_sendagreement`, which RE-ENTERS the auth gate as the planner → so `automation_TAX_sendagreement` must be BOTH allowlisted AND guarded (the entire chain closure is in-scope). By contrast `automation_TAX_refund`/`_charge_implementation` are PUBLIC_HANDLERS (bypass the gate) → intentionally NOT allowlisted. A new planner-callable action or a new link in a planner-reachable chain MUST be added to the allowlist AND carry the group-scope guard; a new PUBLIC chain target must NOT be added to the allowlist. `see #257`.

**258. Adding a NEW login type touches a FIXED checklist — and the easy-to-miss one is the `login_setup_tokens` `login_type` CHECK constraint, which 500s token creation if not widened (2026-07-22).** The Tax Planner portal added a 5th `*_logins` table. Every new login type needs, in one pass: (a) a `*_logins` table — RLS deny-all in the creating migration, `lower(email)` unique, `passcode_hash` salted PBKDF2 (here `tax_planner_logins`, also unique on `tax_planner_id`, FK → `tax_planners` ON DELETE CASCADE; migration `20260722100000`); (b) a pre-auth login action in `index.ts` (`tax_planner_login`) + the action name in the frontend `LOGIN_ACTIONS` array in `api.js` (or `callApi` hard-redirects the failed login off the screen — #144); (c) a `middleware/auth.ts` precedence branch + a new `AuthContext` field (`callerTaxPlannerId`) + a deny-by-default `*_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` allowlist; (d) the `theme.js` `applyThemeForCurrentRoute` route regex (so the portal themes); (e) the `SetPasswordPage` PORTAL map (login_type → login route); (f) the shared login-setup flow — `send_login_setup_email` + `submit_login_setup` branches that resolve/​upsert the new table; **(g) the `login_setup_tokens` `login_type` CHECK constraint MUST be widened to admit the new value** — this was the 500 bug this session: `submit_login_setup` inserts a `login_setup_tokens` row with `login_type='tax_planner'`, and the un-widened CHECK rejected it with a 23514, surfacing as a 500 (migration `20260722110000` fixed it). A self-service passcode-change action (`tax_planner_update_login`, scoped to the caller's session id) rounds it out. `see #258`.

**259. `load_notifications` returns recipient `'admin'`/`'all'` BROADCAST rows IN ADDITION to the caller's own-email rows — a new restricted role that allowlists the bell must branch to email-only scoping in BOTH the load AND the mark-read handler, or internal admin broadcasts leak to that role (2026-07-22).** The notification loaders `.or(recipient.eq.<caller>,recipient.eq.admin,recipient.eq.all)` so admins see broadcast bells. When the Tax Planner portal allowlisted `load_notifications` + `mark_notification_read` for the `tax_planner` role (the portal header carries a `NotificationBell`), the naive path would have shown a planner every `'admin'`/`'all'` internal broadcast. The fix scopes a planner to ONLY `recipient = own-email` rows in the loader, AND restricts mark-read for planners to own-recipient rows (the mark-read handler gained an optional `auth` param; dispatch passes `c.auth`). Any future restricted role that surfaces the bell must apply the SAME email-only scoping in both handlers. `see #259`.

**260. On the tax track a phase's badge number is derived from its NAME, never its render position, and the two stored "Tax 5" phases render as ONE merged card — so notes on that card file under the Specialist Allocation `phase_name` (2026-07-22). ALWAYS-APPLIES (tax track).** The tax track's business numbering never matched a positional index: program 4 (VFO Tax Planning) leads with an unnumbered `Set Up` phase (so "Tax 1" sat at position 2 and everything below was off by one), and BOTH programs store TWO phases named `Tax 5` — `Tax 5 - Education & DD (Specialist Allocation)` and `Tax 5 - Education & DD (Post Allocation)` — so the tail was off by a further one (`Tax 6 - Implementation` badged 7, or 8 on program 4). Fixed display-side ONLY (no phase renames — the names are exact-string-matched in `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` `phaseReach`, at ~14 sites in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, and stored as data in `client_notes.phase_name`): `phaseBadgeToken(name)` in `components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` maps `Set Up` → `'S'` and `/^Tax (\d+)/` → that digit, and the two Tax 5 phases render as a SINGLE card badged `5` titled "Tax 5 - Education & DD", with "Specialist Allocation" and "Post Allocation" as sub-sections inside it (each keeping its own status pill, its per-specialist repeater / lock gate, and the `+ Add Specialist` button). Badges now read `1,2,3,4,5,6` (Holistic) and `S,1,2,3,4,5,6` (Tax Planning). Three knock-ons a future editor must respect: (a) `phaseBadgeToken` returns `''` for any name that is neither `Set Up` nor `Tax <n>` — a NEW differently-named tax phase renders a BLANK badge, so extend the resolver when adding one; (b) `TrackKit.jsx` `PhaseStepper` now honours an optional per-step `number` (falling back to `i + 1`), which is what lets the merged card occupy one stepper node — all other track callers pass no `number` and are unaffected; (c) the merged card has ONE notes thread, achieved by a new optional `phaseNames` ARRAY prop on `PhaseNotesPanel` that widens what is READ while `phaseName` still governs what is WRITTEN — so every new Tax 5 note persists with `phase_name = 'Tax 5 - Education & DD (Specialist Allocation)'` regardless of which half it concerns, and shows that label as its chip in the client Profile "All Notes" list. Task counting/hero totals were deliberately NOT changed (they were already per-task, not per-card). `see #260`.

**261. A Holistic client's MAP 1 risk answer is copied onto every NEW tax plan at creation time — seeded once by `tax_start_plan`, never re-synced (2026-07-22).** Holistic clients answer the risk question once on the MAP 1 track (task "Priorities Decided / Risk Profile", stored `Completed + Risk N` / `Completed + N/A`); `actions/tax/start-plan.ts` now maps that to the tax vocabulary (`Yes — Risk N — <Mindset>`, the verbatim `status_options` strings — note Risk 2's second separator is a plain hyphen, not an em dash) and INSERTS a `client_tax_progress` row for the "Client risk profile complete" task on the newly created plan. Both task ids are resolved BY NAME within program 1 (not hardcoded 13/83) so a template rebuild doesn't silently break it. Constraints that are deliberate, not oversights: it fires ONLY at plan creation (a later MAP 1 risk correction does NOT retro-update existing plans), it is **Holistic-only** (`!program_id || program_id === 1` — program 4 clients never run a MAP 1 track, and a legacy caller omitting `program_id` defaulted to Holistic), `Completed + N/A` seeds NOTHING (no risk profile was decided), and the whole routine is wrapped in try/catch with every miss returning silently — a seeding failure must never fail the plan creation the user asked for. Visible side effect: a brand-new Holistic plan now opens at `1 / N` tasks with `Tax 1 - Diagnostic` reading "In progress" rather than "Not started", because the seeded row stamps `completed_date` = today and `completed_by` = "Auto — from MAP 1 risk profile". The seeded value stays a normal editable dropdown. `see #261`.

**262. Tax-planner per-step editing is a THREE-surface whitelist that must be kept in sync — FE `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` + BE `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` + the trimmed `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` — and task names are EXACT-matched (2026-07-22). ALWAYS-APPLIES (tax track).** When a tax planner opens a client's Tax Priorities tab in `plannerMode`, most steps are visible-but-locked; only a whitelisted subset is editable. That "editable subset" is decided in THREE independent places that must agree: (a) the frontend `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` Set (module scope in `components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`) — a name here means the row escapes the inert `plannerMode` wrapper; (b) the backend `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` Set (exported from `actions/tax/save-task.ts`) — a name here means `tax_save_task` will write the step row for a planner caller. **Derive the membership of both by reading them, never from a count written here (#402);** they happen to hold the same names today, but that is a property to check, not a rule — "Detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email" sat in (a) and not (b) from 2026-07-22 until it left both on 2026-08-18, because it saves through its OWN automation action rather than `tax_save_task` — a planner caller (`resolvePlannerGroupIds` non-null) who tries `tax_save_task` on ANY other task gets a 403 "Tax planners cannot edit this step"; (c) the `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` allowlist in `constants/role-gates.ts`, which was TRIMMED this session (removed `automation_TAX_decision`, `_readyfortax3`, `_presentation_schedule`, `_request_returns`, `_pricing`, `_extrameeting`, `_depositrefund`, `_sendagreement`, `tax_save_deposit_pi`, `tax_allocate_planner`, `msm_update_tax_status` — planners keep `tax_save_task`, `tax_save_assess_form`, `tax_add_specialist`, `automation_TAX_highlevelmeeting_confirm`, `automation_TAX_implementdecision`, `automation_TAX_postreviewdecision` + loaders/vault/notifications/portal/login actions). Task names are matched as EXACT strings, so a task RENAME must grep BOTH whitelists (the "High Level Meeting Confirmation Email" → "Detailed tax plan meeting confirmation email" rename this session, `program_client_tasks` ids 153/154, had to touch the FE Set). A NEW planner-editable step must update every applicable surface. **WIDENED 2026-09-03 (v807) — the worked example of doing all three at once:** `automation_TAX_amend_fee` joined (c) and the task names `Amend fee` / `Amend implementation fee` joined BOTH (a) and (b), because that step calls the automation action AND THEN a second `tax_save_task` to write the step row — allowlisting only the action would have let a planner amend the fee and then 403 on the step, leaving an amended plan with an unanswered step (an actual amendment self-heals through the `fee_amended_at_*` stamp, but "Keep the fee" writes no stamp and would strand the step forever). The same change added the in-handler `denyIfNotPlannerPlan` guard to `amend-fee.ts`, which had none — see **#467**. `see #262`.

**263. A child style that sets `pointerEvents: 'auto'` re-enables clicks INSIDE an ancestor's `pointerEvents: 'none'` lock — that is how CSS `pointer-events` inheritance works, and conditional pointer-events styles must use `undefined` (inherit), never `'auto'`, for their enabled branch (2026-07-22).** The planner visible-but-locked lock in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` wraps a non-whitelisted step in an outer `div` (cursor `not-allowed`) whose inner `div` sets `pointerEvents: 'none'`. A descendant explicitly setting `pointerEvents: 'auto'` (as the generic-dropdown row and the additional-info container both did) punches through that lock — CSS `pointer-events: auto` on a child overrides the inherited `none`, restoring clicks the lock was meant to suppress. The fix makes those child styles conditional: `plannerLocked ? 'none' : undefined` (NOT `'auto'`) — `undefined` lets the value inherit from the ancestor, so the lock holds while the enabled branch behaves normally. Any conditional `pointerEvents` style must reserve `'auto'` only for a deliberate un-lock and default the enabled branch to `undefined`. `see #263`.

**264. On the tax track, Tax 4 "Client decision 1" Continue is CLICK-ONLY — the 24h auto-lock is REMOVED, retainer revshare fires ONLY from the client's Confirmed/Proceed click, and BOTH the Continue and Undecided picks now share the 48h-reminder / 96h-PF-bell ladder via the same guard columns (2026-07-22). ALWAYS-APPLIES (tax track).** Previously the Tax 4 Continue branch auto-locked after 24h (the sweep wrote `post_review_client_decision='Auto-Locked'` and fired `automation_TAX_revshare` even if the client never clicked). That 24h auto-lock was REMOVED from `actions/tax/revshare-sweep.ts`: Tax 4 no longer writes `'Auto-Locked'` at all (the value survives only as historical data + the Tax 5 IMPLEMENTATION auto-lock twin, which still auto-charges — do not confuse the two). Continue now requires the client to click, and the retainer revshare fires exclusively from that click (`post_review_client_decision='Confirmed'` via `automation_TAX_postreviewdecision`). In its place the sweep gained a Continue reminder ladder mirroring the Undecided one: a 48h reminder-email tier (rule `TAX_postreview_continue_reminder_email`, reusing the `TAX_postreview|Reminder` template) and a 96h PF-bell tier (rule `TAX_postreview_continue_stalled`, title "«Client name» hasn't made a decision on Client decision 1"). CRITICAL: the Continue ladder REUSES the existing `post_review_reminder_sent_at` / `post_review_pf_notified_at` guard columns that the Undecided ladder uses — safe ONLY because Continue and Undecided are mutually exclusive per plan (a plan is one branch or the other, never both). `see #264`.

**265. A sweep "stranded leg" rescue MUST verify the UPSTREAM leg actually resolved before treating a NULL status as a crash artifact — the v633 planner-payout retry paid planner shares BEFORE the client's Tax 4 confirmation for months (2026-07-22). ALWAYS-APPLIES (money).** The tax-revshare sweep retries payouts that a crash may have left half-done. The v633 planner-payout "stranded leg" branch retried the planner share on ANY freshly paid plan where the retainer receipt was set + a planner was allocated + a share was owed + `*_planner_paid` was NULL — WITHOUT checking whether the MEMBER leg had resolved. Because the retainer receipt is set at payment time but the client's Tax 4 "Continue/Confirm" click (which is what should authorize revshare) comes later, the sweep paid the planner's share BEFORE the client confirmed — a premature payout that was masked for months by the THEN-existing 24h auto-lock (which resolved the member leg within a day). Removing that auto-lock (#264) exposed it. Fixed: a NULL planner leg is retried ONLY when the member leg resolved — `retainer_rev_paid` (or `implementation_rev_paid`) is a success value (`Yes` / `Money Mapping` / `N/A — No Share Due`); Failed-leg retries are unchanged. Live-verified fixed on v642. Rule: before a sweep rescues a NULL/stranded downstream leg, it must confirm the upstream leg genuinely completed — a NULL is only a crash artifact if everything before it is done. `see #265`.

**266. The `TAX_PLANNER` email role token resolves per-send from the plan's `tax_planner_id` via each handler's `ctx` map — a NEW tax email handler that omits `TAX_PLANNER` in its `resolveTemplateRecipients` ctx silently no-ops the admin-added "Tax Planner" Cc chip (2026-07-22).** This session added `"TAX_PLANNER"` to `RECIPIENT_ROLE_TOKENS` (`utils/email-recipients.ts`) so admins can drop a "Tax Planner" recipient chip on any tax email template; the label rides `ROLE_LABELS.TAX_PLANNER = 'Tax Planner'` in `components/shared/templateMeta.js`. The token is resolved at send time from the plan's `tax_planner_id` (helper `taxPlannerEmail(sb, taxPlannerId)` in `utils/tax-planner-notify.ts`), which each handler must pass in its `resolveTemplateRecipients` ctx — ALL 25 tax `resolveTemplateRecipients` call sites (across `actions/tax/*` + `utils/tax-planner-payout.ts`) were wired this session. A new tax email handler that forgets to pass `TAX_PLANNER` in ctx will still send, but the chip an admin added to that template silently resolves to nothing. IMPORTANT: sandbox mode SUPPRESSES ALL Cc, so the chip's effect is only observable on live-mode sends. `see #266`.

**267. TWO `NotificationBell` instances exist on a planner click path (portal header + `ClientDetail` header) — the row-click handler MUST `await` `mark_notification_read` BEFORE `navigate()`, or the destination bell's mount-load races the write and resurrects the row (2026-07-22).** A bell row-click that navigated before the mark-read write completed let the destination page's own `NotificationBell` load its notifications (including the just-clicked row, still unread in the DB) and re-display it — the row appeared to "come back". Fixed in `NotificationBell.jsx`: the click handler now marks-read AND removes the row locally BEFORE navigating (affected admin too, not just planners). Related z-index trap fixed the same session: a sibling below a sticky header must NOT share the header's z-index or it paints OVER the bell dropdown — `TaxPlannerPortal.jsx`'s tab strip carried `position:relative; zIndex:100` that painted over the bell dropdown's header strip (removed; the admin portal pattern has no z-index). The member/client/specialist portals still carry `zIndex:100` tab strips but have no header bell, so they are unaffected. Any future bell surface must apply the await-before-navigate rule; any new header-bell portal must drop competing z-index on sibling strips. `see #267`.

**268. Stripe Connect account-onboarding links (`connect.stripe.com/setup/...`) are SINGLE-USE and EXPIRE — never email a raw one; email the durable `/payout-setup?token=` page whose public action mints a FRESH link per click (2026-07-23). ALWAYS-APPLIES.** The 4 "Set Up Payment Details" emails (members / specialists / strategic groups / tax planning groups, from `actions/members|specialists|strategic|tax-planners/*stripe-connect-request.ts`) each used to embed the raw Stripe account link returned at creation time. That link is consumed on first use and expires, so a partner who clicked it twice — or came back later — dead-ended on the portal landing page (the creators had passed a bare `PORTAL_BASE` as both `refresh_url` and `return_url`). FIX: the creators now email a stable portal URL `${PORTAL_BASE}/payout-setup?token=<64-hex>` built from ONE durable token per payout entity. The token lives in the NEW table `connect_setup_tokens` (`entity_type` ∈ member/specialist/strategic_group/tax_planning_group + `entity_key`, `unique(entity_type, entity_key)`, deny-all RLS per #141); `utils/connect-setup-token.ts ensureConnectSetupToken` lazily mints ONE per entity (reuses `token32()`, insert-race-safe) so EVERY email ever sent keeps resolving. The PUBLIC action `connect_setup_link` (`actions/payouts/connect-setup-link.ts`, in `PUBLIC_HANDLERS`) resolves token → entity → `stripe_account_id`, reads the shared "MAP 1" `pipeline_sandbox_config` toggle (so it talks to whichever platform owns the account), and mints a FRESH `account_link` on EVERY click. CRITICAL: `refresh_url` must loop back to the SAME durable `/payout-setup?token=` page (Stripe's own mid-flow expiry re-mints via refresh_url) and `return_url` = `/payout-setup?done=1`; `src/pages/PayoutSetupPage.jsx` auto-POSTs the action and redirects. Errors are friendly + generic (404 invalid, 410 "no longer valid" on a Stripe error — e.g. a sandbox-created account read under the live key); the real Stripe error is `console.log`ged WITHOUT the token (a durable bearer secret — keep it out of logs). Any future Connect-onboarding flow MUST reuse this durable-token pattern rather than emailing a raw account link. `see #268`.

**269. Tax plans are program-scoped in the UI as of 2026-07-23 (`plansForProgram`, NULL = Holistic) but the BACKEND auto-stamp is still asymmetric — `automation_TAX_request_returns` stamps by plan id program-agnostically, while upload-notify's `tax_returns_received_at` stamp is hard-gated to `program_id = 4` plans — so any future tax-track surface MUST filter a client's plans by `(program_id || 1)` against its own program context (2026-07-23). ALWAYS-APPLIES (tax track).** *[Annotated 2026-08-07 (v709/v710): the UPLOAD-NOTIFY HALF OF THIS ENTRY IS SUPERSEDED — `tax_returns_received_at` is NO LONGER hard-gated to `program_id = 4`. Holistic gained its own "Request Tax Returns" step (task 175) and `upload-notify.ts` is now DUAL-CANDIDATE: newest program-4 plan unconditionally + newest Holistic plan (`.or("program_id.is.null,program_id.eq.1")`) but ONLY when `tax_returns_requested_at` is set, both stamped off one upload, ONE bell. That Holistic request-gate is what keeps pre-v709 `/tax-upload` links inert and must not be removed — see **#340**. Everything else below — the FE `plansForProgram` scoping, `tax_load_plans`'s no-filter behaviour, the program-agnostic `automation_TAX_request_returns` stamp, and the unfiltered `refreshLivePlan` — is UNCHANGED and still load-bearing.]* `tax_load_plans` (`actions/tax/load-plans.ts`) returns EVERY `client_tax_plans` row for a client with NO program filter (this is the root of #123). A tax plan created under the Holistic (program 1) view could then be driven from the VFO Tax Planning (program 4) view because `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` rendered whatever `tax_load_plans` returned under whichever program view was open. The program-4-only automation then half-worked: `automation_TAX_request_returns` stamped `tax_returns_requested_at` by plan id (worked), but the upload path's `tax_returns_received_at` auto-stamp in `actions/vault/upload-notify.ts` is hard-gated to `program_id = 4` and silently skipped for a program-1 plan. FE FIX (this session, `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`): module-scope helper `plansForProgram(plans, programId) = (plans||[]).filter(p => (p.program_id || 1) === (programId || 1))` — NULL/undefined `program_id` is legacy Holistic (program 1) — applied at the SINGLE state entry point in `loadData()` (`setTaxPlans(scopedPlans)` + the per-plan progress loop iterates the scoped list), so everything downstream (plan cards, `livePlan` selection, TrackHero counts, MSM status, track view, "+ Start Tax Plan" visibility, deep-link resolution) is scoped per program view. CRITICAL: `refreshLivePlan()` inside `TaxPlanTrackView` is deliberately NOT filtered — it fetches the already-open plan by exact id, so a program filter there would be wrong. `ClientDetail.jsx` already `parseInt`s the `?program=` param, so the strict `===` comparison is safe on admin/member/planner paths. The BACKEND `program_id = 4` gate in `upload-notify.ts` was left AS-IS by design (it is the program-4 tax-return workflow); the FE scoping is what prevents a cross-program plan from ever reaching it. `see #269`.

**270. Stripe sandbox generates its OWN demo Connect data — `tr_3...`-shaped destination-charge transfers ($100, no description/metadata, to freshly created demo connected accounts that exist in no VFO table) are Stripe noise, NOT VFO code (2026-07-23).** Three unexplained $100 sandbox transfers investigated this session turned out to be Stripe's own sandbox demo data: `tr_3...`-style destination-charge transfers to demo connected accounts, carrying no description and no metadata, with destination accounts present in no VFO table. The VFO platform uses NO `transfer_data` / `on_behalf_of` anywhere (zero grep hits across `supabase/functions`), so it never creates destination-charge transfers of that shape — every VFO payout is an explicit `POST /v1/transfers` carrying a memo.
